Edge function `deactivate-teacher-with-user` redeployed (v8) to check and surface the `users` update error instead of swallowing it. No schema/table change.

**Updated 2026-07-17 (Subject Notes — new teacher/PC → student notes feature, 1 new table + 1 new bucket; 45 tables now).** Applied via `supabase/migrations/20260801000000_subject_notes.sql`, verified live (`subject_notes` table + its 4 policies present, `subject-notes` bucket + its 5 policies present). A teacher or performance coach can file a **named note** against a student + subject — a written body and/or an uploaded file in any format (PDF/DOCX/image/…) — from the new `/teacher/notes` page (§3.4). The student sees it on their Overview tab under the matching subject (§3.7), keyed by `subject_id` + `curriculum_id` exactly like the Overview's own subject drill-down. New table **`subject_notes`** (see its own `###` section below) and new private **`subject-notes`** storage bucket (see Storage Buckets). RLS follows the established pattern: `is_admin()` → `ALL`; a teacher → `ALL` scoped to their own notes (`teacher_id = my_teacher_id()`); a performance coach → blanket `ALL` (`is_performance_coach()`); a student → `SELECT` on their own via `can_view_student(student_id)`. The bucket mirrors `homework-content`/`homework-answers` (staff upload scoped to a real `students.id` folder, staff read/delete, plus student read of only their own folder). Explicit `GRANT`s (table + identity-sequence `USAGE`) to `authenticated`/`service_role`. No edge function — direct table + storage writes. See `README.md` §2.12/§3.4/§3.7.

**Updated 2026-07-16, later still (Coordinator Logs — Upsell removed from the renewal automation).** `coordinator_log_package_statuses.upsell_opportunity` was dropped and `upsell_timing` renamed to `renewal_timing` (applied via `supabase/migrations/20260731000800_coordinator_log_remove_upsell.sql`; `coordinator_log_package_status_rows()`, `trg_snapshot_coordinator_log_package_statuses()`, and `sync_coordinator_log_for_student()` updated to match — live-verified all 73 existing per-package status rows carried their date over into `renewal_timing`). Also dropped the three already-deprecated whole-log `coordinator_logs` columns from the same "upsell" concept — `upsell_opportunity`, `recommended_upsell_course_type_ids`, `upsell_timing` — which had been superseded by the per-package table since 2026-07-15 and were live-verified 0/12 non-null/non-empty across every existing log; `coordinator_logs_month_dates_check` was rebuilt over `goal_timeline` alone. Frontend: `RenewalUpsellSection` renamed `RenewalSection` (drops its "Upsell" column, "Upsell Timing" → "Renewal Timing"); the coordinator-logs list view's dead "Upsell Opportunity"/"Recommended Upsell"/"Upsell Timing" filter+columns (wired to the now-dropped whole-log fields) removed. See `README.md` §2.11 and `db/README.md`.

**Updated 2026-07-16, later (Coordinator Logs — "Type of program" is now fixed, not coordinator-editable).** The "Type of program" field on `/admin/coordinator-logs/new` and `/teacher/coordinator-logs/new` was a `MultiSelectDropdown` the PC actively picked from (its options already constrained to the programs the student had a package in, but still an active choice — a PC could pick just a subset, e.g. only "Academic" for a student who also has a Beyond Academic pool). It's now a plain read-only `<div>` display — `CoordinatorLogFormView.tsx`'s `courseTypeIds` is no longer form state; it's derived directly (`[...studentProgramIds]`) from the same `student_packages` → program-id lookup (`course_type_id`/`package_type_id`) the old picker's options were already constrained to, so a check-in always covers every program the student currently has a package in, with no way to log a subset or a program the student doesn't have. `course_type_ids` is still written on submit (unchanged shape) and still drives which subject blocks (Academic / Profile Building / College Counselling) the form shows — only the picking mechanism changed, not what's stored. **No schema change** — `coordinator_logs.course_type_ids` stays `smallint[] not null default '{}'` (verified live via `list_tables`); a student can still hold more than one program (e.g. Academic + Beyond Academic) so the column stays an array, it's just no longer populated from a picker. See `README.md` §2.11.

**Updated 2026-07-16 (a still-open renewal request for a package the student doesn't have yet now shows "In Discussion" in the coordinator log).** `coordinator_log_package_status_rows(p_student_id)` previously only ever emitted one row per the student's *existing* unlocked `student_packages` row, matching `package_renewal_requests` onto it (same bundle-aware match `20260731000600` fixed). A renewal request for a course type nothing currently matches — e.g. a standalone "Beyond Academic"/"College Counselling" request filed for a student whose only pools of those types live inside their All-In-One bundle, or a request for a bundle the student doesn't own at all — had no existing row to attach `in_discussion` to, so it silently never appeared anywhere in the log. Live-verified this was happening to **S1**: her pending "Beyond Academic" (course_type_id 2) and acknowledged "College Counselling" (course_type_id 3) renewal requests matched none of her All-In-One bundle pools (all grouped under `package_type_id = 7`), so both were invisible in her coordinator log. Fixed via `supabase/migrations/20260731000700_coordinator_log_phantom_package_requests.sql`: `coordinator_log_package_status_rows()` now also emits a "phantom" row (`student_package_id = null`, `package_type_id = null`, `pool_label = null`) per distinct `course_type_id` among the student's still-open (`pending`/`acknowledged`) renewal requests that don't match any existing package — always `in_discussion` / `medium`, since the row only exists while the request is open. `sync_coordinator_log_for_student()`'s change-detection signature was fixed alongside it (`coalesce(student_package_id::text, 'new:' || course_type_id::text)` instead of a bare `student_package_id` — `null || ...` collapses to `null`, which `string_agg` silently drops, so a phantom row's signature previously wouldn't have participated in change detection at all). Backfilled live for every student — confirmed via `execute_sql` that S1 now has both phantom rows (`in_discussion`/`medium`) on a freshly auto-filed log (id 26, 2026-07-16), alongside her real packages correctly still `not_due`. No schema change (same 7-column return shape). See `README.md` §2.11.

**Updated 2026-07-15 (Coordinator Logs redesign — multi-program + append-only)** — applied via `supabase/migrations/20260730000000_coordinator_logs_multi_program_redesign.sql` (tracked live as version `20260715121507`), verified live via `execute_sql` (backfill correct, old columns gone, new insert shape accepted). Three schema changes to `coordinator_logs`: (1) the single `course_type_id` FK became a **`course_type_ids smallint[]`** array (a check-in can now cover several programs at once — Academic + Beyond Academic, or a bundle like All-In-One), backfilled 1:1 from the old column, no FK possible on array elements (course types are soft-deleted, not removed), GIN-indexed for the list filter's `contains` query; (2) **`ideal_outcome_by_when` was dropped** entirely (and the `coordinator_logs_month_dates_check` CHECK rebuilt over the two remaining month/year columns); (3) **`profile_building_project` (the free-text field) was dropped** (`20260731000000_…`) — the Profile Building Project is now expressed purely as the Beyond Academic subjects selected on the log; no live row had a value, so the drop was lossless. No `coordinator_log_subjects` schema change. The form is now **append-only, no edit**: selecting a student prefills every field from their latest log (`fetchLatestCoordinatorLogForStudent`) so a PC edits forward from it, and any save writes a *new* row — the `:id/edit` routes and the detail-view "Edit" button were removed, keeping full history for analysis. Which subject blocks the form shows is driven by the selected programs: Academic → Academic Subjects & Baseline Scores (the only block with baselines); Beyond Academic / Foundation Program / All-In-One → Profile Building Project (a flat Beyond Academic subject picker — the selected subjects *are* the project; no free-text field); College Counselling / All-In-One → College Counselling subjects (stored in `coordinator_log_subjects` alongside the profile-building ones, no baseline). The former "Scores (0-5)" section was merged into "Ratings" (all 9 rendered as star pickers), Ideal Outcome moved next to Primary Goal, and Primary Relationship Owner moved into the first section (fixed to `pc_cc` for a PC, admin-selectable). The per-subject `final_outcome_grade` was relabelled **"Current Grade"** and is now **versioned**: `fetchSubjectGradeHistoryForStudent` reads the whole per-subject timeline across the student's logs, the form shows earlier grades beside a blank new-grade input (prefill leaves the new current grade empty), and the read view shows the full timeline up to each log's date. No schema change for any of that (still `final_outcome_grade text`). Opening the form from a student's Coordinator Log tab deep-links the student in (`…/coordinator-logs/new?student=<id>`) so it's pre-selected and prefilled. See `README.md` §2.11/§3.2/§3.4.

**Updated 2026-07-15 (per-package renewal/upsell — auto-appended to the coordinator log; 44 tables now).** Renewal/upsell was removed from the coordinator-log form entirely and made automatic **per package/pool** — a new table **`coordinator_log_package_statuses`** holds one status row per package on each coordinator log, and the system files a **new coordinator log** (today's date, everything else carried forward, including `primary_relationship_owner` — defaulting to `pc_cc` when there's no prior log) whenever any package's status changes, so the log history is itself the dated record. Applied via `supabase/migrations/20260731000300_coordinator_log_package_statuses.sql` (superseding two same-day iterations: `20260731000100` aggregate whole-log automation and `20260731000200` a standalone `package_renewal_status_events` table, both dropped). The four aggregate `coordinator_logs` columns (`renewal_status_option_id`, `upsell_opportunity`, `recommended_upsell_course_type_ids`, `upsell_timing`) are now **deprecated** (always null/`{}` on new logs). See the `coordinator_logs` automatic-logging note and the `coordinator_log_package_statuses` section below.

**Updated 2026-07-29 (Coordinator Logs — new admin+PC-only feature, 3 new tables)** — applied via `supabase/migrations/20260729000000_coordinator_logs.sql`, verified live via `list_tables`/`get_advisors` (43 tables now, was 40; no new advisories) and impersonation (a PC could insert/read a log for their own assigned student; a plain non-PC teacher was blocked outright with a `42501` RLS violation on the same insert). A Performance Coach files a periodic coaching check-in per student — goals, subject baselines/final outcomes, ratings, renewal/upsell/referral signals — from the new **Coordinator Logs** tab, admin+PC only (no plain teacher, no student access at all). Three new tables (full detail in their own section below):
- **`coordinator_log_options`** — one shared table (discriminated by `list_key`) backing all 6 admin-editable dropdowns this feature needs (Primary Goal, Progress Status, Biggest Challenge, Next Action, Renewal Status, Referral Status) instead of 6 near-identical tables — managed only from the Admin Reports **Settings** tab (same "single admin-editable place" precedent as `no_show_settings`/`bundle_pool_settings`), seeded with the client's spreadsheet option values.
- **`coordinator_logs`** — the check-in itself: `student_id`/`teacher_id`/`course_type_id` required, everything else nullable. Repeating history (one row per check-in), same shape as `session_logs`. `goal_timeline`/`upsell_timing`/`ideal_outcome_by_when` are month+year-only real `DATE` columns (day always `01`, CHECK-enforced) — same "one real DATE column, derive month/year at display" convention as the rest of this schema (see `README.md` §4 Key Decision #1). All 9 rating fields (the original 4 plus 5 more added in a follow-up message, kept as separate fields per product decision even though some overlap in meaning) are 0-5 CHECK-constrained smallints — the UI renders the first 4 as star pickers and the other 5 as 0-5 dropdowns, but that's a display choice; the columns are identical.
- **`coordinator_log_subjects`** — child table (`ON DELETE CASCADE`) pairing a subject with its baseline score and its **current grade** at that check-in (`final_outcome_grade`, versioned across the student's logs), one row per subject added to a log.

RLS deliberately mirrors `package_renewal_requests`/`student_packages` (PC scoped through `pc_student_assignments`) rather than `session_logs`' blanket-PC-read pattern, since a PC must only reach their own assigned students here: `is_admin()` → `ALL` on all three; a PC gets `ALL` on `coordinator_logs`/`coordinator_log_subjects` only where an `exists(...)` subquery against `pc_student_assignments` confirms the student is currently assigned to them (`unassigned_at IS NULL`), and `SELECT`-only on `coordinator_log_options` (management stays admin-only). No policy at all for a plain teacher or student — fails closed. `authenticated`/`service_role` granted `SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE` + sequence `USAGE` on all three (checked explicitly per the "one rule to never forget" below). New frontend: `/admin/coordinator-logs*` and `/teacher/coordinator-logs*` (PC-only, mirroring `/teacher/students`), plus a 5th **"Coordinator Log"** tab on the shared `StudentDetailView.tsx` (`/admin/students/:id` and `/teacher/students/:id`) showing the latest check-in's snapshot with full history below it. See `README.md` §2.11/§3.2/§3.4 and `db/README.md`.

**Updated 2026-07-28, later (Homework Generator — manual photo/PDF answer grading)** — applied via `supabase/migrations/20260728010000_homework_manual_answer_grading.sql`, verified live (`whole_paper_answer`/`whole_paper_grade` columns present, `homework-answers` bucket + its 4 policies present). A student can now answer a subjective question with an uploaded photo instead of typing, or attach one whole-paper PDF covering every subjective question at once — objective (mcq/true_false/fill_blank) questions are unaffected either way. Neither is ever AI-graded: `grade-homework-submission` (redeployed v12) writes a pending placeholder (`graded_by:'manual'` per-question, or `grades.whole_paper_grade`) for the teacher to mark and annotate by hand on the review page, and a re-grade preserves an already-entered manual mark instead of resetting it. **Additive only** — `submissions.whole_paper_answer` and `grades.whole_paper_grade` (both jsonb, nullable) are the only new columns; a per-question photo answer reuses the existing `answers_json`/`per_question_json` jsonb columns with no schema change (`HomeworkAnswer` gained a photo variant, `QuestionGrade` gained `graded_by:'manual'` + an `annotations` array). New private **`homework-answers`** storage bucket for the student's own uploaded answer files (see Storage Buckets below). See `db/docs/HOMEWORK_GENERATOR_ARCHITECTURE.md` §"Manual photo/PDF answer grading" and `README.md` §2.10.

**Updated 2026-07-28 (new College Counselling subject; Ascend Offline Work "Assigned by" narrowed back to performance coaches)** — applied via `supabase/migrations/20260728000000_add_career_exploration_cc_college_counselling_subject.sql`, verified live (`subjects` now 454 rows, id 615). Added `Career Exploration - CC` as a plain College Counselling `subjects` row (`category = 'college_counselling'`, `category_id = NULL`, same shape as the existing `College Counselling`/`College Essays` rows — see `db/docs/SUBJECT_HIERARCHY.md` §3b) — named with the `- CC` suffix to stay distinct from the pre-existing Beyond Academic `Career Exploration` subject (id 607). No code change needed, per §3b's "Rule for a new College Counselling subject." Separately, **no schema change**: `SessionLogFormView.tsx`/`SessionLogDetailView.tsx`'s "Assigned by" dropdown for Ascend Offline Work (which still writes to `coordinator_teacher_id`, unchanged) had its option list narrowed back from "every active teacher" to performance-coaches-only, reverting that part of the 2026-07-24 entry below — the relabeling itself ("Assigned by" instead of "Coordinator") is unchanged. See `README.md` §2.2.

**Updated 2026-07-27 (Homework Generator — parse a multi-page paper from several photos)** — applied via `supabase/migrations/20260727000000_homework_parse_multi_page.sql`, verified live (`generated_papers.content_upload_ids` is `bigint[]`). Extends the parse-an-existing-paper flow so a teacher can photograph a multi-page paper (one photo per page) and parse all pages into a single paper. **Additive column only, no constraint change:** `generated_papers.content_upload_ids` (`bigint[]`, nullable) holds the ordered list of every uploaded page image; `content_upload_id` stays set to the **first** element so the existing `generated_papers_source_check` and every single-file parsed paper are untouched (null `content_upload_ids` = single-file, use `content_upload_id`). Chosen over a join table for consistency with the other array/jsonb-shaped columns here (`blocks`, `content_scope`). `parse-homework-paper` redeployed **v2**: it resolves the id list (`content_upload_ids` or `[content_upload_id]`), downloads every page (capping the combined size at ~50MB and the count at 20 so a pile of photos can't OOM the worker), and sends **one Gemini File-API part per page** in order with a note that they're consecutive pages of one paper. Frontend: the "Or upload an existing paper" input is now `multiple`. See `db/docs/HOMEWORK_GENERATOR_ARCHITECTURE.md` §"Parse an existing paper".

**Updated 2026-07-26 (Homework Generator — parse an existing paper)** — applied via `supabase/migrations/20260726000000_homework_parse_existing_paper.sql`, verified live via `execute_sql` (constraints read back). Two CHECK relaxations, **no new tables**: (1) `content_uploads.file_type` now also allows image types (`jpg`/`jpeg`/`png`/`webp`/`heic`) so a teacher can upload a photo/scan of a past paper, not just a document; (2) `generated_papers` gained a fifth `content_source_type`, **`parsed`** — a paper created by uploading an EXISTING paper (a PDF, or a photo/scan of one) and having Gemini transcribe its questions verbatim + build the answer key, instead of generating new questions. `generated_papers_source_check` was rewritten to require `content_upload_id` for `parsed` (same rule as `upload`); a parsed paper has no session log and no composition (`blocks` starts `[]`, then holds one display-only block per extracted section). New **`parse-homework-paper`** Edge Function (deployed via `deploy_edge_function`, `verify_jwt = true`, runs as the calling teacher like `generate-homework-paper` — RLS-enforced, no `service_role`): downloads the uploaded file, sends it to Gemini via the File API with a mixed-question-type structured schema, extracts every question verbatim + an answer key (MCQ correct option / fill-blank expected answer / mark scheme), and writes `questions_json` + section `blocks`. Requires the existing `GEMINI_API_KEY` secret (+ optional `GEMINI_API_KEY_2.._8` rotation). No new secret, no new table, **no RLS change** — the existing teacher-owns-own-row policies on `generated_papers`/`content_uploads` and the path-scoped `homework-content` staff-upload storage policy already cover it. Publishing, the student attempt view, and auto/AI grading are all unchanged (a parsed paper reuses the same `questions_json` shape, so nothing downstream needed to change). Not exercised end-to-end against live Gemini from this sandbox (same egress caveat as every homework phase) — verified via clean `tsc -b`/`vite build`, applying the migration, and reading the deployed function back to confirm its source matches. See `db/docs/HOMEWORK_GENERATOR_ARCHITECTURE.md` §"Parse an existing paper" and `README.md` §2.10.

**Updated 2026-07-25 (multi-package enrollment requests + multi-file payment proofs)** — applied via `supabase/migrations/20260725000000_enrollment_requests_multi_package_multi_proof.sql`, verified live via `execute_sql` (40 tables now, was 38; backfill confirmed 5 `enrollment_requests` rows → exactly 5 `enrollment_request_packages` + 5 `enrollment_request_payment_proofs` rows, 1:1, since every live row had exactly one package and one proof before this split). An admin adding/renewing a student can now bundle several packages into one request instead of submitting a separate request — and getting a separate invoice email — per package, and a parent can upload more than one payment screenshot on the same `/pay/:token` link (paid in separate transactions) instead of being limited to one for the whole request. Two new child tables, one row per line item (full column detail in their own `###` sections below):
- **`enrollment_request_packages`** — what used to be `enrollment_requests.course_type_id`/`hours`/`package_size_label`/`is_bundle_pool_selection`/`bundle_pool_label`/`resulting_student_package_id`/`is_new_package_generation` now lives here instead, one row per package on the invoice (`sort_order` preserves entry order). `resulting_student_package_id`/`is_new_package_generation` are stamped per line by `review-enrollment-payment` on confirm — a multi-package confirm can create/top-up several different `student_packages` rows in one go, so there's no longer a single answer at the request level.
- **`enrollment_request_payment_proofs`** — what used to be `enrollment_requests.payment_proof_url`/`payment_proof_uploaded_at` now lives here instead, one row per uploaded screenshot (`storage_path` into the same private `payment-proofs` bucket, unchanged). `submit-payment-proof`'s POST now accepts multiple files in one multipart request (`form.getAll("file")`) and inserts one row per file before flipping the request to `payment_submitted`, same as it flipped after the single old upload.

Both new tables: RLS `admin_all_*` (`FOR ALL USING (is_admin()) WITH CHECK (is_admin())`, mirroring `enrollment_requests` itself), `authenticated`/`service_role` both granted `SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE` in the same migration (dodging the "first edge function to touch a new table" service_role gotcha documented below), `ON DELETE CASCADE` back to `enrollment_requests` so deleting a still-`pending_payment` request (the one case the UI allows deleting at all) cleans up its line items too. `enrollment_requests` itself keeps every genuinely one-per-request field (student/contact snapshot, `status`, `payment_link_token`, `note`) — one invoice email and one payment link still cover every package and accept every proof for the whole request; `enrollment_requests_hours_check` moved to `enrollment_request_packages` as `enrollment_request_packages_hours_check`.

All three enrollment-workflow edge functions were redeployed to match: `send-enrollment-invoice` (v14) and `review-enrollment-payment`'s confirmation email (v23) now render one combined package table (with a Total row once there's more than one) instead of a single course-type/hours pair; `review-enrollment-payment` processes every package line independently (its own standalone-vs-bundle pool resolution, exactly the same logic as before, just looped per line) and still runs one finalize + one renewal-request-auto-resolution pass per confirm; `submit-payment-proof` (v9, still `verify_jwt: false`) accepts and stores multiple files per submission. `AdminEnrollStudentPage.tsx`'s Package section is now a repeatable list (add/remove rows) for both New Student and Renewal; `AdminEnrollmentsPage.tsx`'s queue/detail-modal/edit-dialog were updated to show every package and every proof per request (the pending-payment quick-edit dropped its `hours`/`package_size_label` fields — packages aren't editable inline, same precedent as course type never being editable there — delete and re-create instead); `PublicPaymentPage.tsx`'s upload is now a multi-select file picker. See `db/README.md`'s "Student enrollment workflow" section and `README.md` §2.9.

**Updated 2026-07-24 ("assigned by" on Ascend Offline Work folded into `coordinator_teacher_id`, not a separate column)** — applied via `supabase/migrations/20260724000000_session_logs_assigned_by_folded_into_coordinator.sql`. A same-session correction to the 2026-07-23 change below: the standalone `assigned_by_teacher_id` column it added was dropped (after backfilling any non-null values into `coordinator_teacher_id`, verified live — one real row existed, id 48) because Ascend Offline Work has no real "coordinator/PC" concept, and `coordinator_teacher_id` is already surfaced everywhere a session log is shown (list view, CSV export, detail view) without needing new UI, unlike the standalone column which was invisible outside the form/detail view. `SessionLogFormView.tsx`/`SessionLogDetailView.tsx` now just relabel the existing Coordinator field "Assigned by" (and widen its option list to every active teacher, not just performance coaches) when the top-level program type is `ascend_offline_work`, reusing `coordinatorId`/`coordinator_teacher_id` end to end rather than a parallel field.

**Updated 2026-07-23 (session logging — Fathom summary relabeled to Fathom transcript; superseded "assigned by" design above)** — applied via `supabase/migrations/20260723000000_session_logs_assigned_by_teacher.sql`. Originally added a standalone `assigned_by_teacher_id` column for Ascend Offline Work's new "Assigned by" dropdown — **superseded the next day by the entry above**, which folded it into `coordinator_teacher_id` instead; the column no longer exists. What's still current from this entry: every user-facing "Fathom Summary" label in the session-log form/detail/list views (admin, teacher, student) was renamed to **"Fathom Transcript"** — the `fathom_summary`/`fathom_summary_doc_url` column and variable names are unchanged. The per-session transcript `.docx` a student downloads from `/student/sessions` (`buildSummaryDoc.ts`, uploaded to the `session-summaries` bucket) now also embeds the session's Fathom recording link (`video_link`) when one was entered, and its own heading was renamed "Session Transcript" — existing already-generated docs aren't retroactively regenerated, only saves from this point on. See `README.md` §2.2.

**Updated 2026-07-09 (later — PC-initiated package renewal requests)** — applied via `supabase/migrations/20260721000000_package_renewal_requests.sql`, verified live via `list_tables`/`get_advisors` (37 tables now, was 36; no new advisories). New **`package_renewal_requests`** table lets a Performance Coach flag that a specific student's package needs renewing (student + course type + optional suggested hours/label/note) from the new `/teacher/renewal-requests` page — this is *not* the enrollment/invoice flow itself, just a PC-to-admin heads-up that precedes it. `status` is `pending` → `acknowledged` → `renewed`: a PC insert always lands `pending` (RLS-enforced — see below) and fires the new **`notify-renewal-request`** edge function (fire-and-forget, no JWT, mirrors `notify-pool-issue`'s shape) which emails every active admin (`users` where `role = 'admin' and is_active`); an admin acknowledges it from the new `/admin/renewal-requests` page (a plain client-side `UPDATE`, admin has RLS `ALL` — no edge function/email involved, same as other lightweight admin state changes in this app) which stamps `acknowledged_by_user_id`/`acknowledged_at`; and **`renewed` is set automatically, never by a manual admin action** — `review-enrollment-payment` (redeployed, version 22) was extended so that right after it resolves `targetPackageId` for *any* confirm (new-student or renewal, bundle or standalone), it looks up every `pending`/`acknowledged` `package_renewal_requests` row matching `(student_id, course_type_id)` and flips it to `renewed`, stamping `resulting_student_package_id`/`renewed_at`, then emails each requesting PC a "Renewal Completed" notice in the same background confirmation-email pass. This best-effort resolution is wrapped in its own `try/catch` so a failure there can never block the confirm the admin is waiting on — the package itself is already correctly created by that point regardless. RLS: admin gets `ALL`; a PC can `INSERT` only as themselves (`requested_by_teacher_id = my_teacher_id()`) for a student currently assigned to them (`pc_student_assignments`, `unassigned_at IS NULL`), and can `SELECT` only their own submitted requests (any status) — not other coaches'. `authenticated`/`service_role` both granted `SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE` (no `DELETE` — nothing in the UI removes a request). Reuses the existing generic `touch_updated_at()` trigger function rather than adding a new per-table copy. See `db/README.md` and `README.md` §2.9/§3.2/§3.4.

**Updated 2026-07-09 (College Counselling moved into the Subjects Library)** — applied via `supabase/migrations/20260709000000_college_counselling_subjects_library.sql`, verified live via `execute_sql`. College Counselling's two `program_types` sub-programs (id 32 "College Counselling", id 33 "College Essays", both children of top-level id 31) are now **deactivated** (`is_active = false`, not deleted — verified zero `session_logs` rows referenced either id first) rather than being the way "College Counselling" vs. "College Essays" gets picked. In their place, two new `subjects` rows were added (`category = 'college_counselling'`, `category_id = NULL`, ids 613/614), picked from a new "College Counselling" tab in the Subjects Library — exactly mirroring how Beyond Academic subjects already work (flat, no grouping). Top-level `program_types` id 31 is unchanged; with no active children left, it now behaves exactly like "Beyond Academics" in the session-log form (no "Sub-program" step). `course_types` and `bundle_pool_settings` are untouched — College Counselling is still exactly one billing course type with one unlabeled 40hr All-In-One bundle pool, same as before this change. See `db/docs/SUBJECT_HIERARCHY.md` §3b for the full picture and the file registry touched.
- **Same-day follow-up:** `/admin/program-types` (`AdminProgramTypesPage.tsx`) was still showing College Counselling as an empty "section with sub-programs" rather than a standalone type — its `isSectionType()` treated a type as a section if it had *any* child `program_types` rows at all, active or not, so the deactivated (not deleted) ids 32/33 kept id 31 misclassified. Fixed by adding `college_counselling` to that file's `BEHAVIORAL_TYPE_VALUES` allowlist and making the check an unconditional override rather than one half of an `||`. No DB change — this was a pure front-end classification bug in a page this changelog's original entry didn't cover. See `db/docs/SUBJECT_HIERARCHY.md` §3b.

**Updated 2026-07-07 (student & parent dashboards merged; parent role removed)** — applied via `supabase/migrations/20260710000000_add_student_notification_curriculum_reportcard.sql` and `20260710010000_remove_parent_role_and_infra.sql`:
- **The `parent` role and the separate `parents` table are gone.** A student now has exactly one login — there is no parent account anymore. The former parent's name/phone are kept as plain fields directly on the `students` row (`parent_full_name`, new `parent_phone_number`) instead of a linked record.
- **`students`** gained four columns: `notification_email` (the "send updates to" address — where invoices/notifications go, may match or differ from `email`, falls back to `email` when blank), `curriculum` (free text — one of a fixed set: IBDP / IB MYP / IGCSE / A Levels / AP / NA / National Curriculum / OTHER, enforced app-side only), `report_card_url` (optional, private `report-cards` storage bucket path), and `parent_phone_number`.
- **`enrollment_requests`** gained the matching `notification_email`, `curriculum`, `report_card_url` columns so the intake form can capture them; `email` is now documented as the "send updates to" contact (still NOT NULL — resolves to `notification_email` if given, else `student_email`), and `student_email` is what creates the student login.
- New private **`report-cards`** storage bucket (admin-managed; any staff/teacher row can read via signed URL) for the optional report-card upload — see Storage Buckets below.
*(All four bullets below are superseded by the 2026-09-10 entry at the top of this file, which brings `parents`, `students.parent_id`, the `parent` enum value and `is_my_linked_child()` back — as a household account, not a per-student one. `parents_update_own_children` was deliberately NOT restored: parent access is read-only.)*

- **`students.parent_id` column dropped**, and the `parents` table dropped (`CASCADE`, taking its RLS policies and the `prevent_non_admin_parent_relink` trigger with it). `is_my_linked_child()` and the `parents_update_own_children` / `parents_read_children_users` policies were dropped too (see Functions/RLS below). `can_view_student()` lost its parent-lookup branch — a student can only ever see their own record now. `prevent_non_admin_student_relink()` lost its `parent_id` clause.
- The **`user_role` enum** was recreated without `'parent'` (`teacher`, `student`, `performance_coach`, `admin` only) — this required dropping and recreating five legacy "Admins can manage all X" RLS policies (`curricula`, `session_logs`, `subject_categories`, `subjects`, `teacher_subjects`) that inlined a `users.role = 'admin'` check rather than calling `is_admin()`, since Postgres won't retype a column referenced by a policy definition.
- **Data purge** (irreversible, run before the schema change so no `role = 'parent'` rows remained): every student except `S1` (Divija Pandey) was deleted along with their dependent rows (`student_packages`, `package_topups`, `pc_student_assignments`, `enrollment_requests`, session/invoice rows — none existed for the other four), and every `parents` row plus every parent/deleted-student Auth login was deleted. `S1`'s linked parent's name/phone/email were copied onto her own row (`parent_full_name`, `parent_phone_number`, `notification_email`) before her `parent_id` was nulled and the `parents` table dropped, and her own student login/account was left untouched.
- `review-enrollment-payment` no longer creates a second (parent) login on `new_student` confirm — only the student account. Confirmation/renewal emails now go to `notification_email` (falling back to the student's own email), not a separate parent address.
- Live-verified post-migration *(as of 2026-07-07; see the 2026-09-10 entry for current state)*: 29 tables (was 31 — `parents` dropped), `students` has 1 row (`S1`), `parents` table does not exist, `user_role` enum has exactly 4 values, zero `users` rows with `role = 'parent'`.

**Updated 2026-07-07 (later — everything collected on the student's own first-login form, not just at admin enrollment)** — applied via `supabase/migrations/20260711000000_students_can_upload_report_card.sql`. The mandatory "Complete your profile" gate (`StudentCompleteProfileGate.tsx`) and the ongoing `/student/profile` page originally only asked for phone/graduation year/birthday/school (unchanged since 2026-07-06); they now also collect **curriculum**, **address/country**, and the **guardian's name/phone** — all required, joining the existing four — plus an **optional** report-card upload (the only non-required field). `studentNeedsProfileCompletion()` was widened to match, so a student who's missing any of these (including `S1`'s pre-existing data, which already had them) sees the gate again until all nine are on file. New storage policy **"Students can upload their own report card"** (INSERT only, `authenticated`) lets a student write to the private `report-cards` bucket themselves for the first time — scoped to a path prefixed with their own `students.id` (e.g. `S1/<uuid>.pdf`) via `(storage.foldername(name))[1] = s.id` matched against `students.user_id = auth.uid()`, so a student can create objects only under their own folder, never read/update/delete anyone's (including their own) — the existing `is_admin()`/staff-read policies are unchanged. `useMyStudent.ts`'s `EditableFields` widened to include `address`, `country`, `parent_full_name`, `parent_phone_number`, `report_card_url`.

**Updated 2026-07-08 (Homework Generator — Phase 1 data layer, schema only)** — applied via `supabase/migrations/20260715000000_homework_generator_phase1_schema.sql` and a same-day follow-up, `20260715010000_homework_generator_phase1_perf_fixes.sql` (missing FK-covering indexes + two RLS policies rewritten to `(select auth.uid())` per the performance advisor, no behavior change). Seven new tables, **36 total** (was 29): `style_templates`, `content_uploads`, `generated_papers`, `question_bank`, `submissions`, `grades`, `session_insights` — full column/RLS detail in their own `###` sections below and in `db/docs/HOMEWORK_GENERATOR_ARCHITECTURE.md`. This is genuinely schema-only: no UI, no Gemini integration, no generation/grading code exists yet — `generated_papers.status`/`questions_json` and `grades`/`submissions` exist so later phases have somewhere to write, but nothing writes to them outside this migration yet. RLS follows the existing pattern exactly (`is_admin()` → `ALL`; a teacher → `ALL` scoped to rows they created, mirroring the existing "teachers only see their own `session_logs`" scoping; performance coaches → blanket `ALL`/`SELECT`, matching the existing blanket "Performance coaches can read all session_logs" policy rather than being scoped through `pc_student_assignments`; a student → read-only on their own non-`draft` rows via the existing `can_view_student()` helper). Every new table got explicit `GRANT`s (table privileges + identity-sequence `USAGE`) to `authenticated`/`service_role` in the same migration, per this file's own "one rule to never forget" (see `db/README.md`) about RLS-without-grants being a silent dead end. Verified live via `list_tables` (36 tables, all RLS-enabled) and `get_advisors` (no new advisories beyond the two perf-lint items fixed same-day; pre-existing warnings unrelated to this change).

**Updated 2026-07-08 (Homework Generator — Phases 2 & 3)** — no table changes. Phase 2 seeded `style_templates` with 12 rows (`supabase/migrations/20260716000000_seed_style_templates.sql`, idempotent `ON CONFLICT (code)`) — see that table's `###` section. Phase 3 added the private **`homework-content`** storage bucket + its staff-read/staff-upload policies (`supabase/migrations/20260717000000_homework_content_bucket.sql`, see Storage Buckets) for the teacher-facing Homework Generator UI (`/teacher/homework`), which now writes real `content_uploads` and `draft` `generated_papers` rows (generation still stubbed — Phase 4 is the Gemini integration).

**Updated 2026-07-08 (Homework Generator — Phase 4, generation service)** — no table/column changes; `question_bank` and `generated_papers.questions_json` now actually get written to for the first time (Phase 1 created them empty). New **`generate-homework-paper`** Edge Function (deployed via `deploy_edge_function`, `verify_jwt = true`), called by the teacher UI right after a draft paper is created. It runs as the calling teacher (anon-key client + the caller's own JWT, not `service_role`), so every read/write goes through the existing RLS policies rather than a separate authorization check — this only worked because Phase 1 already granted staff `SELECT` on `style_templates` and `INSERT`/`SELECT` on `question_bank`. Requires the **`GEMINI_API_KEY`** edge function secret (not set as part of this change — an admin must run `supabase secrets set GEMINI_API_KEY=...` before generation will succeed); optional `GEMINI_MODEL` secret overrides the default `gemini-2.5-flash`. Full pipeline detail and the two scope decisions made here (no vector store; PDF-only uploads via Gemini's native file understanding, PPT/PPTX/DOCX not yet supported) are in `db/docs/HOMEWORK_GENERATOR_ARCHITECTURE.md`'s Phase 4 section. Not yet exercised end-to-end against a live login — this session's environment has no network path to the project's REST/Auth endpoints for a browser session (org egress policy denies direct access to `uqyuczvckqxgtilpzarh.supabase.co`; only the Supabase MCP connection can reach it) — verified instead via `tsc -b`/`vite build` passing clean and reading the deployed function back to confirm its source matches.

**Updated 2026-07-08 (Homework Generator — Phase 5, review & publish)** — no table/column changes. The **`generate-homework-paper`** edge function was extended (redeployed, version 4) to accept scoped regeneration alongside full generation: `regenerate_question: <id>` (one question), `regenerate_block: <index>` (one composition block, others preserved), or neither (whole paper). The new teacher review page (`/teacher/homework/:paperId`) reads/edits a paper via the `useGeneratedPaper` hook: inline question edits are plain `UPDATE`s to `generated_papers.questions_json` (RLS lets the owning teacher edit their own draft), and **Confirm & Publish** is a plain `UPDATE` setting `status = 'published'`, `published_at = now()`. **This is the first place `generated_papers.status` is advanced by application code** — the state-machine triggers stay deferred (Phase 1), so this is app-code, not a DB trigger. The function still refuses any (re)generation on a non-`draft` paper, so a published paper (potentially already in front of the student) can't be mutated. See `db/docs/HOMEWORK_GENERATOR_ARCHITECTURE.md` Phase 5. Same live-testing caveat as Phase 4 (no browser network path from this sandbox; verified via clean `tsc -b`/`vite build` and reading the redeployed function back).

**Updated 2026-07-08 (Homework Generator — Phases 6 & 7, student attempt + grading)** — no table/column changes; `submissions` and `grades` (created empty in Phase 1) now get written to for the first time. Phase 6 added the student **`/student/homework`** + **`/student/homework/:paperId`** pages: the student writes only their own **`submissions`** row (`saveAnswers` upserts `answers_json`; `submit` sets `submitted_at`, which the Phase 1 `prevent_submission_edit_after_submit` trigger then locks). Phase 7 added the **`grade-homework-submission`** edge function (deployed, `verify_jwt = true`) — invoked on submit / by the teacher — which **runs as `service_role`** (a student has no write access to `grades` or `generated_papers`; it re-checks the caller's JWT and authorizes admin/owning-student/owning-teacher/PC first). It auto-grades MCQ/fill-blank, Gemini-grades the mark-scheme types per point, upserts the `grades` row (`per_question_json`/`total_marks`/`max_marks`/`graded_at`), and sets `generated_papers.status = 'graded'`. Teacher overrides are a plain client `UPDATE` on `grades` from the review page (RLS: paper-owning teacher → `ALL`), stamping `teacher_reviewed_by`/`teacher_reviewed_at`. **Deliberate RLS-driven decision:** the student never advances `generated_papers.status` (they can't — `SELECT`-only on their papers); `in_progress` is *derived* from the submission in the app (`src/utils/homeworkGrading.ts`), and the only post-publish writer of `generated_papers.status` is the service-role grading function (`-> graded`). If `GEMINI_API_KEY` is unset, subjective questions grade to `0` + "pending teacher review" (`ai_incomplete: true`) rather than failing. Same live-testing caveat as Phases 4-5 (no browser path from this sandbox; verified via clean `tsc -b`/`vite build`/eslint and deploying + reading the function back). See `db/docs/HOMEWORK_GENERATOR_ARCHITECTURE.md` Phases 6-7.

**Updated 2026-07-08 (Homework Generator — Phase 8, export/render)** — **no database impact at all**: purely front-end (`src/utils/exportHomeworkPaper.ts` + `HomeworkExportButtons`) turning a paper's `questions_json` into PDF/DOCX via the existing `jspdf`/`docx` deps, no new dependency, no schema/edge-function/RLS change. Noted here only so this changelog stays a complete phase-by-phase record. This completes the Homework Generator (all 8 phases). See `db/docs/HOMEWORK_GENERATOR_ARCHITECTURE.md` Phase 8.

**Updated 2026-07-08 (Homework Generator — PDF chapter/section indexing)** — applied via `supabase/migrations/20260718000000_homework_pdf_outline_scope.sql`. Adds three columns to **`content_uploads`** (`outline` jsonb, `outline_status` text CHECK `pending`/`processing`/`completed`/`failed` default `'pending'`, `outline_error` text) and one to **`generated_papers`** (`content_scope` jsonb, null = whole document). New **`index-content-upload`** Edge Function (deployed, `verify_jwt = true`, runs as the calling teacher like `generate-homework-paper`): it downloads an uploaded PDF, asks Gemini for its chapter→section outline with page ranges, and writes it to `content_uploads.outline`. The teacher picks one or more chapters/sections (a checklist) in the Homework Generator; the choice is stored on `generated_papers.content_scope` (`{ label, page_start, page_end, parts? }`, `parts` listing each selected slice), and `generate-homework-paper` (redeployed, version 9) passes it to Gemini as an "only use these pages" instruction so questions come from just those parts of the book. PDF-only (non-PDF uploads go straight to `outline_status = 'failed'`). No RLS change — the new columns inherit the tables' existing policies; the function relies on the teacher's own `content_uploads`/`generated_papers` access. Same live-testing caveat as the other phases (verified via clean `tsc -b`/`vite build` + deploying the functions and applying the migration through the Supabase MCP connection). Also in this change (no DB impact): the teacher session-log picker was upgraded from a bare `<select>` to a filterable list (subject/date-range/topic-search + full per-session detail) — `src/components/homework/SessionLogPicker.tsx`.

**Updated 2026-07-02** — added the student enrollment workflow (`enrollment_requests` table + `send-enrollment-invoice`/`submit-payment-proof`/`review-enrollment-payment` edge functions + `payment-proofs` storage bucket) and package-level locking (`student_packages.is_locked` + related columns/triggers, `session_logs.student_package_id`, `students.user_id`), applied via `supabase/migrations/20260702060000_student_enrollment_workflow.sql` and `20260702070000_enrollment_requests_add_pc_teacher.sql` and verified live via `list_tables`/`get_advisors` immediately after applying. A follow-up migration (`20260702080000_fix_service_role_grants_for_enrollment_workflow.sql`) was needed after live testing surfaced `permission denied for table enrollment_requests` — see the corrected `service_role` note under Grants below.

**Updated 2026-07-02 (later)** — added `subject_categories` row "Career Exploration" (`supabase/migrations/20260702100000_add_career_exploration_subject_category.sql`) and flattened Beyond Academic subject picking to a single dropdown app-wide (no more required "Section" step). See the new **[`db/docs/SUBJECT_HIERARCHY.md`](./SUBJECT_HIERARCHY.md)** for the full subject-hierarchy reference and the registry of every file that must move together when this hierarchy changes.

**Updated 2026-07-02 (even later) — Beyond Academic has zero grouping now, not even the `<optgroup>` from the entry above.** `supabase/migrations/20260702120000_flatten_beyond_academic_no_sections.sql` set `subjects.category_id = NULL` for every active Beyond Academic subject, deactivated the `Passion Projects`/`Career Exploration` `subject_categories` rows, and re-created both as plain, directly-pickable `subjects` rows instead — verified live via `execute_sql`. `subject_categories` now has only 1 active row (`Academic`, the academic-branch grouping, unaffected). `subjects` total is 560 (558 + the 2 new rows). See `db/docs/SUBJECT_HIERARCHY.md` §3/§6 for the full picture.

**Updated 2026-07-02 (multi-pool packages)** — added Foundation Program / All-In-One as `course_types` (reactivated a pre-existing inactive "Foundation Program" row; "All-In-One" is new), `student_packages.package_type_id` (which bundle a pool belongs to, for grouping only) and `pool_label` (distinguishes concurrent pools of the same course_type), relaxed `student_packages_one_current_per_course_type` to allow several concurrently-active pools per (student, course_type, pool_label), added `session_logs.pool_ambiguous`/`pool_fallback_used`, added the new `session_log_pool_resolutions` table, and rewrote `handle_session_log_package_lock()` to resolve among multiple pools (see those sections below). Applied via `supabase/migrations/20260702110000_multi_pool_packages_and_resolution.sql` and verified live via `execute_sql` — created a throwaway test student with two Beyond Academic pools, confirmed a session logs as `pool_ambiguous` with no package linked, confirmed it auto-resolves once a `session_log_pool_resolutions` row exists, and confirmed the zero-active-pool fallback auto-creates a zero-hour pool — then deleted all test rows. The `notify-pool-issue` edge function was deployed (`verify_jwt: false`, matching `notify-package-threshold`) but its `PUBLIC_APP_URL`/`SMTP_*` secrets still need to be confirmed set before it can actually send.

**Updated 2026-07-02 (inactive cleanup)** — confirmed the live active Beyond Academic `subjects` list (Book Publishing, Entrepreneurship, Passion Projects, Coding and Programming, Finance and literacy, Career Exploration, Creative Writing, Research Writing, Blog Writing, Journalistic Writing, Public Speaking, Podcast — 12 rows) matches what's shown on `/admin/subjects` and audited every file in `db/docs/SUBJECT_HIERARCHY.md` §4's registry — all correctly filter on `category !== 'academic'` (plus `category_id IS NULL` for the three "assign a new subject" flows), no drift found. Then, per an explicit cleanup request, deleted all 110 inactive `subjects` rows (91 academic + 19 non-academic, including the legacy `college_support`-category rows College Counselling/College Essays/Job Application Mentorship/Profile Building/Writing Development), the 1 inactive `program_types` row ("Zoom Invoice", id 7), and wiped all `session_logs` rows for a clean slate. `teacher_subjects_subject_id_fkey` is `ON DELETE CASCADE`, so 18 `teacher_subjects` rows pointing at those deleted (legacy, already-inactive) subjects were removed along with them — confirmed via `execute_sql` that none of the other subject/program_type FKs (`invoice_line_items`, `monthly_report_*_subject_stats`, `session_log_pool_resolutions`, `student_packages`, `enrollment_requests`) referenced any of the deleted rows first. Applied via `supabase/migrations/20260702130000_cleanup_inactive_subjects_program_types_and_session_logs.sql`. Live counts after: `subjects` 450, `program_types` 10, `teacher_subjects` 88, `session_logs` 0.

**Updated 2026-07-02 (direct hour-entry removed; bundle purchases via invoice)** — the admin "Learner's actual hours" surfaces (student-detail tab, `/admin/packages`) no longer let an admin add hours or create packages directly; the `/admin/students/enroll` invoice → payment → `review-enrollment-payment` confirm flow is now the only path that creates packages or adds hours (no schema change for this part, application-only). Two migrations followed to make that flow able to fully replace the direct-entry UI it took over from:
1. `supabase/migrations/20260702140000_subject_name_direct_pool_match.sql` — rewrote `handle_session_log_package_lock()` again: when a student has multiple concurrent unlocked pools for a course_type, a session whose subject name exactly equals one of those pools' `pool_label` (e.g. subject "Career Exploration" vs. an All-In-One bundle's "Career Exploration" pool) now auto-resolves directly to that pool, before the existing `session_log_pool_resolutions` sticky-cache lookup even runs — no PC "Pending Deduction" step needed for that case.
2. `supabase/migrations/20260702150000_enrollment_requests_bundle_pool_selection.sql` — added `enrollment_requests.is_bundle_pool_selection` (boolean, default `false`) and `enrollment_requests.bundle_pool_label` (nullable text). Foundation Program / All-In-One are now purchasable through the enroll form like any other course type; on confirm, a first-time bundle purchase (`is_bundle_pool_selection = false`) creates every pool in the bundle at its hardcoded default hours (as `/admin/packages`' old bundle builder used to), while renewing an already-owned bundle forces picking exactly one pool (`bundle_pool_label`, matched against `BUNDLE_POOL_DEFS` in the edge function) so only that pool's hours change — not every pool in the bundle re-filled. See §"Multi-pool packages" below for the full picture.

Also: PCs can now use the same "Pending Deduction" / pool-reassignment / pool-resolution-reset UI as admins, scoped to their own assigned students, from `/teacher/students/:id`'s "Learner's actual hours" tab (`TeacherStudentDetailPage.tsx`) — application-only, no new RLS needed since `pc_manage_assigned_session_log_pool_resolutions` and the `student_packages`/`package_topups` PC policies already scoped this correctly.

**Updated 2026-07-03 (subject/program display + reassignment-priority fix)** — three related fixes:
1. `notify-pool-issue` (redeployed) now selects `session_logs.program_type_id` and falls back to it when a session has no subject and no topic (e.g. College Counselling / College Essays sessions) — the email's "Subject / Topic" row no longer shows a bare "—" when a program name is available. Same subject → topic → program fallback (`sessionTopicLabel()` in `src/utils/subjectLabel.ts`) is now used on the "Pending Deduction" queue and the sister-pool reassignment list on both the admin and PC student-detail pages, which also gained a visible subject/program column (the reassignment list previously showed only date/teacher/hours).
2. `supabase/migrations/20260703000000_resolution_before_subject_name_match.sql` fixes a real bug the above surfaced: `handle_session_log_package_lock()` checked the subject-name → `pool_label` direct match *before* the sticky `session_log_pool_resolutions` lookup, so a PC manually reassigning a session away from its name-matching pool (e.g. moving a "Career Exploration" session to a different pool) would have that reassignment silently reverted by the very next write to the row. The resolution table is now checked first — a manual reassignment sticks, and a brand-new session with no resolution yet still auto-resolves by subject name exactly as before. Verified live via `pg_proc` inspection.

**Updated 2026-07-03 (later — never re-resolve an already-deducted session; proactive assignment rules)** — a deeper bug in the same area: `handle_session_log_package_lock()` still recomputed `student_package_id` from scratch on *every* `UPDATE` to a `session_logs` row, even one that had already been resolved and deducted. That meant changing a (teacher, subject) → pool assignment (or moving one sibling session to a different pool) planted a landmine — the next time *any other* already-deducted session from that same teacher+subject got touched for any unrelated reason (fixing an hours typo, editing the date), the trigger silently re-ran resolution and dragged that historical session onto the new pool too.

`supabase/migrations/20260703010000_never_reresolve_already_deducted_sessions.sql` fixes this: once a session has `OLD.student_package_id IS NOT NULL` (already resolved) **and** the update doesn't change which student/course_type it belongs to, the trigger no longer re-runs resolution at all — it leaves `student_package_id` exactly as the row arrives. This covers both an unrelated edit (column simply carried forward unchanged) and an explicit single-session reassignment (the application's own `UPDATE` sets a specific new value, honored exactly, affecting only that one row). Only a still-unresolved session (`OLD.student_package_id IS NULL` — still `pool_ambiguous`, or a brand-new `INSERT`) goes through the resolution/subject-match/ambiguous/fallback logic. Verified live via `pg_proc` inspection (guard clause present, checks `student_id`/`course_type_id` unchanged).

This split what used to be one `assignSessionToPool()` function (in `useStudentPackages.ts`) into three, matching three now-distinct actions:
- `resolvePendingSession` — resolves a still-ambiguous "Pending Deduction" session, writes the sticky resolution, and sweeps other still-pending sessions matching the same key (unchanged behavior).
- `reassignSingleSession` — moves **one** already-resolved session to a different pool; does **not** touch the resolution table at all, so it can never change which pool other sessions (past or future) deduct from.
- `updatePoolResolutionTarget` — the dedicated way to change a (teacher, subject) → pool assignment "going forward": updates the resolution row and sweeps only currently-pending sessions matching it; never touches an already-resolved session.
- `createPoolResolution` (new) — lets an admin/PC set up a (teacher, subject) → project assignment **proactively, before any session has even been logged**, via the same upsert path `resolvePendingSession` uses internally. The "Pool assignment rules" section (renamed "Teacher/Subject → Project assignments" in the UI) now has a "+ Add assignment" form with Project/Teacher/Subject dropdowns (subject options filtered by the chosen project's course type — no subject dropdown at all for College Counselling, whose sessions carry a program type instead) so this can be set up ahead of time, not just derived from an ambiguous session after the fact.

**Updated 2026-07-02 (report sectioning by package/pool)** — `supabase/migrations/20260702160000_invoice_line_items_package_columns.sql` added `invoice_line_items.student_package_id` (bigint → `student_packages.id`, nullable) and `invoice_line_items.course_type_id` (smallint → `course_types.id`, nullable), plus `idx_invoice_line_items_student_package_id`. A follow-up, `20260702170000_invoice_line_items_program_type.sql`, added `invoice_line_items.program_type_id` (smallint → `program_types.id`, nullable) so subject-less rows (College Counselling / College Essays) are labeled by their program name and split by program rather than collapsing into one "—" row. All three are nullable and only populated on new report generation — existing invoices must be regenerated to pick them up. Reports (invoices) used to group only by curriculum, which dumped everything non-academic (Beyond Academic, College Counselling — no curriculum) under one "No curriculum" heading and gave no per-package breakdown. Line items now record which pool/course type each belongs to, so a report is sectioned by **course type / bundle pool → subject → teacher**: College Counselling shows as its own section, and a Foundation Program / All-In-One report shows each of its pools individually plus a combined total. This is a display/aggregation model in app code (`aggregateReportSections`/`resolveReportSection` in `src/utils/buildInvoicePdf.ts`, shared by the PDF, the on-screen `ReportSectionsView`, and the admin Reports preview) — the only schema part is these two nullable columns. Both are nullable so legacy line items and package-less sessions fall back to a course-type / "Other" section.

**Updated 2026-07-03 (student & parent dashboards)** — added first-class parent accounts and read access for student/parent portals via `supabase/migrations/20260703000000_parent_student_dashboards.sql`:
- New **`parents`** table — a parent gets a unique `P`-id (e.g. `P12`, `'P' || nextval('parents_num_seq')`), mirroring students' `S`-id, and links to a login account (`parents.user_id → users.id`, unique where not null). One parent can have several children.
- **`students.parent_id`** (text → `parents.id`, nullable, indexed) — a student points at their parent.
- **`enrollment_requests.student_email`** (text, nullable) — the enroll flow now captures a separate student email; the existing `email` column is the parent/primary-contact email (invoice + payment-link recipient + parent login).
- New SECURITY DEFINER function **`can_view_student(text)`** — true if the caller is that student (`students.user_id = auth.uid()`) or a parent of it (`parents.user_id = auth.uid()` via `students.parent_id`). Backs read-only SELECT policies (`student_parent_read_*`) added to `student_packages`, `package_topups`, `session_logs`, `invoices`, `invoice_line_items`, `invoice_packages`, and `pc_student_assignments`, plus `admin_all_parents` / `parent_read_own` / `pc_read_assigned_parents` (added 2026-07-05, see below) on `parents`. No write access is granted to students/parents anywhere.
- Grants: `authenticated` gets SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE on `parents` and `service_role` full access + `USAGE` on `parents_num_seq` (the `review-enrollment-payment` edge function creates parent rows).
- The `review-enrollment-payment` edge function now creates **two** logins on new-student confirm — a student account (`student_email`, role `student`) and a parent account (parent `email`, role `parent`) linked via a `parents` row + `students.parent_id` — reusing an existing parent (same email, e.g. a sibling) rather than duplicating. Both are emailed their own credentials.
- Verified live via `execute_sql` with an impersonated `authenticated` role: a parent and the student each see the student's `student_packages` (count 1), an unrelated user sees 0.

Also `supabase/migrations/20260703000001_add_ai_incubator_beyond_academic_subject.sql` added **"AI Incubator"** as a flat Beyond Academic `subjects` row (`category = 'beyond_academic'`, `category_id = NULL`, active) — `subjects` total now 561.

**Updated 2026-07-04 (enrollment/email fixes + full testing-data wipe, no schema change)** — application-only changes, no migration:
- `send-enrollment-invoice` and the renewal-confirmation email in `review-enrollment-payment` now send to **both** the parent (`enrollment.email`) and the student (`enrollment.student_email`, deduped) instead of the parent only — new-student credential emails were already correctly split (student creds → student email, parent creds → parent email).
- `AdminEnrollStudentPage.tsx`'s renewal path previously put the student's own email into the request's `email` (parent/primary-contact) field, since `students` has no parent-email column of its own — it now looks up the linked `parents.email` via `students.parent_id` first, falling back to the student's email only if no parent account exists yet.
- Admin Students list and the student detail page's Details tab now surface every captured field, including the student's and parent's own usernames (joined via `students.user_id`/`parents.user_id` → `users.username`) and the linked `parents` row — no new columns, existing schema only.
- `useStudents.ts`'s unused `createStudent()` (a bare `students` INSERT with no login/parent creation) was deleted — the enrollment workflow's `review-enrollment-payment` remains the only path that creates a student, mirroring `create-teacher-with-user` for teachers/coaches.
- Enrollments queue: rows still `pending_payment` ("Awaiting Payment") can now be edited (resends the invoice with corrected details) or deleted outright; the status filter now defaults to "All" instead of "Awaiting Review".
- **Full data wipe for testing** (`db/scripts/wipe-students-and-testing-data.sql`, run directly — no migration, matching how `wipe-invoices-and-reports.sql`/`wipe-hours-and-revenue-data.sql` were run previously): `TRUNCATE ... CASCADE` on `students`+`parents` cleared `students`, `parents`, `session_logs`, `student_packages`, `package_topups`, `invoices`, `invoice_line_items`, `invoice_packages`, `enrollment_requests`, `pc_student_assignments`, `session_log_pool_resolutions`, and the per-student `monthly_report_*` breakout rows, all to 0; the one existing student-role login and any parent-role logins were also deleted from `auth.users` (cascading to `public.users`); `students_num_seq`/`parents_num_seq` were reset so new test data starts at `S1`/`P1`. Verified live via `execute_sql` — every count above is 0, `users` is back to 144 (143 teachers/coaches + 1 admin, no student/parent roles). Teachers, admins, and the subject/curriculum/program-type catalogue were untouched.

**Updated 2026-07-04 (later — teacher/coach deactivation fixes)**:
- New edge function **`deactivate-teacher-with-user`** (admin-only) is now the only path that deactivates a teacher (`deactivateTeacherById` in `src/hooks/useTeachers.ts` calls it instead of a bare client-side `teachers.update`; the unused, equivalent `deactivateTeacher` hook method was deleted). It refuses to deactivate a performance coach who still has an active `pc_student_assignments` row (`unassigned_at IS NULL`) — the student(s) must be reassigned from `/admin/pc-assignments` first. It does **not** touch the teacher's login email — see the revert note below.
- Fixed three coach-picker filters that showed deactivated coaches (`teachers.filter(t => t.is_performance_coach)` with no `is_active` check): `AdminPcAssignmentsPage.tsx`, `AdminSessionLogFormPage.tsx`, `TeacherSessionFormPage.tsx`. Historical list/filter pages (`AdminSessionLogsPage.tsx`, `TeacherSessionsPage.tsx`) were left as-is since a past session can legitimately have been coordinated by a since-deactivated coach.

**Updated 2026-07-04 (even later — reverted the deactivated-teacher email reuse)**: the same-day change above originally also (a) relaxed `teachers.email` to a partial unique index (`teachers_email_active_key ON teachers(email) WHERE is_active`) and (b) had `deactivate-teacher-with-user` rename the teacher's Supabase Auth login email to a parked address, so a brand-new teacher could register with the old email. This was reverted the same day, correctly flagged as unsafe: reactivating the old account later (the `activateTeacher` capability exists in `useInactiveTeachers`, even though no UI currently calls it) would leave it with a broken parked email while the real address now belonged to someone else, with no way back. `supabase/migrations/20260704010000_revert_teachers_email_unique_active_only.sql` dropped `teachers_email_active_key` and restored the original global `teachers_email_key UNIQUE (email)`; `deactivate-teacher-with-user` no longer touches login email at all; the one teacher (id 140) whose email had already been parked by the short-lived version was restored to their real email in both `auth.users` and `public.users`. **Teachers/coaches must stay deactivated (or be manually deleted) rather than have their email reused** — there is no supported way to let a new teacher register with an email that still belongs to a deactivated one.

**Updated 2026-07-04 (later still — Enrollments queue cleanup, no schema change)**: application-only.
- `AdminEnrollStudentPage.tsx`'s bundle `package_size_label` no longer repeats the course type name (was literally `"${courseTypeName} bundle"`, which then displayed as "Foundation Program — Foundation Program bundle" everywhere it was shown alongside `course_types.name` — Enrollments queue, invoice email/PDF). A first-time bundle purchase now records `"Full bundle (all pools)"`; topping up one pool of an already-owned bundle drops the redundant `(courseTypeName)` suffix too. The one enrollment_requests row already sitting with the old redundant label was corrected directly via `execute_sql`.
- Enrollments queue (`AdminEnrollmentsPage.tsx`): the "Student" column previously fell back to showing the *parent's* email (`e.email`) when a new-student request had no `student_id` yet, with no indication that's who it actually was — it now shows the student's own `student_email`, and a new "Parent" column shows `parent_full_name` + the parent's email explicitly. The compact "Package" column now shows only the course type name + hours (the redundant `package_size_label` concatenation is gone from the compact view but still shown, clearly labeled, in the new row-click "View details" modal — every field, including timestamps, rejection reason, and the payment-proof link). The always-visible Edit/Delete/Resend buttons were collapsed into a `RowMenu` ⋯ dropdown (same component `SubjectsLibraryContent.tsx` already uses). The edit form's Student email field is now `required` for `new_student` requests (it wasn't before, despite being how the student's own login gets created on confirm).

**Updated 2026-07-04 (later still — deleted all inactive teachers)**: `db/scripts/delete-inactive-teachers.sql` hard-deleted the 3 teachers that had `is_active = false` (ids 13/140/143 — none referenced by any `session_logs`, `invoice_line_items`, `pc_student_assignments`, `zoom_invoices`, `monthly_report_teacher_*_stats`, `session_log_pool_resolutions`, or `enrollment_requests` row, verified via `execute_sql` before deleting) along with their login accounts (`auth.users`, cascading to `public.users`) and their `teacher_subjects` rows (`ON DELETE CASCADE`, 4 rows). `teachers` is now 140 (all active), `users` 141. This is a one-off cleanup, not a new standing behavior — deactivation (`is_active = false`) is still the only thing the app does automatically; hard-deleting a deactivated teacher is a manual, deliberate action via this script, and only safe when nothing above still references them.

**Updated 2026-07-04 (later still — readable credentials, enroll-form validation, no schema change)**: application-only.
- `review-enrollment-payment`'s student/parent account creation generated a random-hex password (`crypto.randomUUID()...`) and a `firstname.lastname.studentid` username — neither matched what an actual person receives in the confirmation email as something they could read and type back in. Both now mirror the teacher-creation defaults (`AdminTeacherFormPage.tsx`'s auto-fill): **username = the email's local part** (before `@`, sanitized), **password = `<first name>@ascendnow`**. A new `uniqueUsername()` helper appends a numeric suffix only if that plain username is already taken (checked against `public.users` before the auth account is created) — `users.username` stays globally unique either way. The parent's first name is taken from the first word of `parent_full_name` (a single free-text column; the form now collects it as separate first/last name inputs and joins them before submitting). The old two-step "create with a temp username, then rename it once the student id exists" dance for students is gone — the username no longer depends on the not-yet-existent student id, so it's generated once, upfront. Deployed as version 10.
- `AdminEnrollStudentPage.tsx`'s "New Student" form: Country now appears above Phone number (was below); "Parent full name" split into separate "Parent first name"/"Parent last name" inputs (joined into `parent_full_name` on submit, no schema change); Parent email and Student email are now genuinely validated (a real email-shaped regex, not just non-empty) before the Send button enables. Phone was briefly made required in this same change and reverted the same day — it's optional, as originally.
- `AdminEnrollmentsPage.tsx`'s edit-request modal got the identical treatment (Country above Phone, split parent first/last name, email regex validation) for consistency, since it edits the same shape of data for a still-unpaid request.

**Updated 2026-07-04 (later still — Enrollments Confirm/Reject buttons, Details-tab dedup, Beyond Academic filter bug, no schema change)**: application-only.
- `AdminEnrollmentsPage.tsx`: Confirm/Reject are visible buttons again for `payment_submitted` rows (the rest — View details/Edit/Delete/Resend — stay in the ⋯ `RowMenu`), since reviewing payment is the primary, frequent action on this queue and shouldn't be hidden behind a menu click.
- `AdminStudentDetailPage.tsx`'s Details tab showed phone/address/country under **both** "Student Information" and "Parent/Guardian Account" (same values, copied from the same enrollment form into both `students` and `parents` rows). Split into three cards instead: Student Information (id/name/email/username/coordinator/created — no contact fields), a new Contact Information card (phone/address/country, shown once), and Parent/Guardian Account (id/name/email/username — no contact fields).
- Fixed a real bug in the "+ Add assignment" (Teacher/Subject → Project) subject dropdown on both `AdminStudentDetailPage.tsx` and `TeacherStudentDetailPage.tsx`: it filtered `subjects.category === "beyond_academic"`, but `category` is legacy free text that isn't literally `"beyond_academic"` for most rows post-flattening — only 3 of the 13 active Beyond Academic subjects (Passion Projects, Career Exploration, AI Incubator) happened to match. Replaced with the same canonical check used elsewhere in the app (e.g. `AdminTeacherFormPage.tsx`'s Beyond Academic picker): `category !== "academic" && category_id == null`. Verified live — 13 active rows match this filter.
- Renamed the pool-assignment-rule "Reset" button to "Delete" (same `deletePoolResolution` action as before — it already only affects sessions not yet logged/deducted, never touches already-resolved ones) on both the admin and PC student-detail pages, since "delete an assignment without affecting past logs" is exactly what it already did; the label just didn't say so.

**Updated 2026-07-04 (session duration is now an admin setting, not typed in per-session)**: added **`session_duration_settings`** (single-row, same pattern as `no_show_settings`) via `supabase/migrations/20260704040000_session_duration_settings.sql` — `default_duration_hrs numeric NOT NULL DEFAULT 1`, admin-editable from a new "Default session duration" control at the top of `/admin/session-logs`. The teacher and admin session-log forms no longer show a "Session duration" dropdown at all; every new session log (regular or no-show) picks up this default instead, while an existing logged session keeps whatever duration it already has when edited. Also, all three `no_show_type` values now ask for Type of program/Subject in the form (previously only No Show +) and keep whatever was already selected rather than clearing it when the no-show is marked — `program_type_id`/`subject_id`/`curriculum_id` are stored for every no-show type. `course_type_id`/`session_duration_hrs` remain gated to No Show + alone (verified against `handle_session_log_package_lock()`, which resolves/creates a `student_package_id` off `course_type_id` regardless of `no_show_type` — populating it for No Show 1/2 would incorrectly trigger pool resolution/creation for sessions that must never bill). Verified live via `execute_sql` (`SELECT * FROM session_duration_settings` returns the single default row).

**Updated 2026-07-04 (later still — removed single-session pool reassignment; fixed a real hours-used mismatch; no-shows no longer counted as "sessions")** — application-only, no schema change:
- **Removed the "Move a session to a different pool" UI entirely** from `AdminStudentDetailPage.tsx` and `TeacherStudentDetailPage.tsx` (the "N sessions in this pool" toggle button, the expandable list, and its reassign-pool `<select>`), along with the underlying `reassignSingleSession` function in `src/hooks/useStudentPackages.ts` (introduced 2026-07-03, see above) — moving an already-logged session to a different pool after the fact is no longer possible from the UI at all. `resolvePendingSession` (resolving a still-`pool_ambiguous` session), `updatePoolResolutionTarget`, and `createPoolResolution` are unaffected — those still exist and are unrelated to reassigning an already-resolved session.
- **Fixed a real bug**: `AdminPackagesPage.tsx` (`/admin/packages`, the standalone "Learner's actual hours" list) queried `session_logs` with `.is("no_show_type", null)`, excluding *every* no-show — including No Show +, which still deducts from the package (see `no_show_settings`/`utils/noShow.ts`). This undercounted hours used there vs. the per-student detail tab (`AdminStudentDetailPage.tsx`), which fetches all sessions and lets `computeHoursUsed`'s `isNonBillableNoShow` check do the (correct) filtering — the two pages showed different "hrs used" for the same package. The filter is now removed; both pages compute the same total.
- **No-shows no longer counted as plain "sessions"** in the "By subject"/"By teacher" breakdowns (`computeHoursUsedBySubject`/`computeHoursUsedByTeacher` in `useStudentPackages.ts`, used by the admin/PC student-detail pages and the student/parent portal's `StudentPackagesView.tsx`): a No Show + still adds its hour to the total (unchanged), but is now tracked as a separate `noShowCount` rather than folded into `sessionCount` — the UI shows e.g. "2 hrs · 1 session + 1 no-show" instead of "2 hrs · 2 sessions", since a no-show never actually held a session.

**Updated 2026-07-05 (real bug: zero-active-pool fallback was borrowing across course_types)** — caught live on student `S1` (`divija pandey`): she has an All-In-One bundle (Beyond Academic pools "Primary Project"/"Secondary Project"/"Extra Hours", plus a "Career Exploration" pool) but no Academic package. A teacher logged an Academic session (French A: Language and Literature, IBDP) against her, and `handle_session_log_package_lock()`'s zero-active-pool fallback borrowed the *oldest* active pool the student had of **any** course_type — the Beyond Academic "Primary Project" pool — instead of one matching the session's own Academic course_type. That's how an academic subject ended up billed inside an All-In-One bundle that's meant to hold only Beyond Academic / College Counselling pools. `supabase/migrations/20260705120000_never_borrow_across_course_types.sql` removes the cross-course_type borrow entirely: the zero-active-pool branch now *always* creates a fresh zero-hour pool of the session's own `course_type_id` (never someone else's), so an Academic overage always lands on an Academic pool (shown as e.g. "1/0 hrs used"), matching how the "more than one pool" branch already only ever picks among same-course_type candidates. Applied and verified live via `execute_sql`/`pg_get_functiondef`. The `notify-pool-issue` edge function (redeployed, version 3) also had its `pool_fallback_used` copy corrected — it used to say "hours were deducted from a different package" (describing the buggy behavior); it now says a package of the right type is missing and a 0-hour placeholder was created, calling out that the student is over that package's limit, since this case now always needs the same "set up a real package" action as before, not just an FYI. Divija's own miscategorized session (`session_logs.id = 9`) was manually repointed (direct `UPDATE`, not through the app — the "move one already-logged session" UI path was intentionally removed 2026-07-04, see above) to a newly created zero-hour Academic `student_packages` row (`id = 11`) for `S1`, and `pool_fallback_used` left `true` so it's visible as an overage needing a real package.

**Updated 2026-07-05 (invoice/report no-show tracking + report table split, no-show email speed)**:
- `supabase/migrations/20260705000000_invoice_line_items_no_show_flag.sql` added **`invoice_line_items.is_no_show`** (boolean, `NOT NULL DEFAULT false`) — verified live via `execute_sql` (column present, type `boolean`, default `false`).
