Fixes a real bug: a No Show + session's hours were already included in `invoice_line_items` (only No Show 1/2 are excluded — see `isNonBillableNoShow` in `utils/noShow.ts`), but got merged into the same line as a completed session for the same teacher/subject, so a student's Reports tab showed no-show-deducted hours as indistinguishable regular hours with no way to tell they weren't an actual session. `buildLineItems` (`src/hooks/useInvoices.ts`) now keys line items by `is_no_show` too, so a no-show's hours are never merged with a completed-session line; existing (pre-migration) line items default to `is_no_show = false` and can't be retroactively split — only newly generated invoices pick up the distinction, same caveat as the `student_package_id`/`course_type_id`/`program_type_id` additions before this one.
- Application-only (no further schema change), built on top of the column above: `aggregateReportSections`/`groupSectionsIntoBlocks` (`src/utils/buildInvoicePdf.ts`) now track a `noShowCount` alongside `session_count`/`hours` at every level (subject, teacher, section, block) — a no-show's hour still counts toward `hours` (the package really lost it), but is never counted as a `session_count`, matching the same "no-show is a deduction, not a session" convention `computeHoursUsedBySubject`/`computeHoursUsedByTeacher` already used (see 2026-07-04 entry above). The shared `sessionCountLabel()` helper (moved to `src/utils/noShow.ts`, used by `StudentPackagesView.tsx`, `ReportSectionsView.tsx`, and the PDF) renders this as e.g. "3 sessions + 1 no-show".
- **Reports (student/parent/admin/teacher invoice views and PDFs) no longer nest a per-subject teacher breakdown inside one table.** Each report section now renders as two separately labeled tables — "Hours by Subject" and "Sessions & Hours by Teacher" (the latter aggregated across every subject in the section via a new `ReportSection.teachers` field) — instead of subject rows with indented teacher sub-rows crammed into the same table. Mirrors the already-separate-tables layout the admin's monthly report (`buildFullMonthlyReportPdf`) used. `ReportSectionsView.tsx` (on-screen) and `drawSectionTable` (`buildInvoicePdf.ts`, PDF) were both rewritten to match; no-show hours render in an amber accent in both.
- `create-teacher-with-user`, `send-enrollment-invoice`, and `review-enrollment-payment` edge functions (redeployed as versions 16/6/11 respectively) now send their emails (teacher welcome credentials; enrollment invoice + payment link; confirm/reject/renewal notices) via `EdgeRuntime.waitUntil()` instead of awaiting the SMTP round trip before responding — the admin action (create teacher / send invoice / confirm or reject payment) now returns as soon as its database work is done, with the email continuing in the background. `review-enrollment-payment` also now reuses one SMTP connection for all the emails a single confirm sends (e.g. student + parent credentials, or several renewal recipients) instead of reconnecting per recipient. Application-only, no schema change.

**Updated 2026-07-05 (PC couldn't see linked parent accounts — missing RLS policy)** — on the PC student-detail page the "Parent / Guardian Account" card always showed "No parent account linked yet", even for students with a fully linked parent (e.g. S1 → P1, "krish pandey", with a login). The `parents` table had only `admin_all_parents` (admins) and `parent_read_own` (a parent reading their own row) — **no** policy granting a performance coach any read access — so the page's `parents` fetch returned zero rows under RLS, indistinguishable from "no parent" to the UI. `supabase/migrations/20260705130000_pc_read_assigned_parents.sql` adds a read-only `pc_read_assigned_parents` SELECT policy scoped to parents of the PC's own currently-assigned students (via `pc_student_assignments`, same scoping the invoice / pool-resolution PC policies use). The parent's username is read from `users`, which a PC could already read via the existing "Performance coaches can read users" policy, so no change was needed there. Verified live: the policy's inner subquery resolves `P1` as visible for teacher 133 (the coach assigned to S1). Application code was unchanged — the shared `StudentDetailView` already queried `parents` correctly; this was purely the missing policy. This surfaced right after the admin/PC student-detail pages were unified into one shared component (so the PC now renders the same rich Parent/Guardian card the admin always had).

**Updated 2026-07-05 (later — over-limit packages never re-notified past the first session)** — real bug caught right after the pool-borrowing fix above: `notify-package-threshold` unconditionally skipped every package with `total_hours_purchased <= 0` (`if (!pkg.total_hours_purchased || pkg.total_hours_purchased <= 0) return json({ skipped: true, ... })`), so the zero-hour Academic pool the pool-fallback fix creates (see entry above) never got a threshold email at all — not on the session that created it (that's `notify-pool-issue`'s job, via `pool_fallback_used`), and not on any later session either, since those resolve cleanly (`candidate_count === 1`) and never set `pool_fallback_used`. Caught live: student S1 logged a second Academic session (ACT, `session_logs.id = 10`) against the same zero-hour package created for the French A session earlier that day, pushing it to "2/0 hrs used" with no notification sent at all. Rewrote the function (redeployed, version 2, `supabase/functions/notify-package-threshold/index.ts`) to compute `hoursUsed` unconditionally and branch on whether the package is over its limit (`hoursUsed > total_hours_purchased`, which subsumes both "0-hour placeholder package" and "a real package pushed past 100%"): an over-limit package emails the PC on **every** offending session (deliberately never stamped/deduped — each additional session is more unaccounted usage, not a one-time milestone), while the existing 50%/75%/100% milestone path (still stamped once per threshold via `notified_50/75/100_pct_at`) only runs for a package that has real purchased hours and ISN'T already over limit. Verified via `tsc --noEmit` (clean) and by re-deriving the exact live data for session 10 (`student_package_id = 11`, `total_hours_purchased = 0`, `hoursUsed = 2` after the ACT session) that would now trigger the over-limit branch. Could not send the retroactive email for session 10 itself — this sandbox's egress policy blocks direct calls to `*.supabase.co`, and `pg_net` isn't installed on this project to route the call through Postgres instead — but the fix is live for every session logged from now on.

**Updated 2026-07-05 (later still — reverted "no typed duration for any session log", scoped to No Show + only)** — the 2026-07-04 `session_duration_settings` change (see that entry above) had removed the "Session duration" dropdown from **every** session log, regular sessions included, replacing it entirely with one admin-wide default. That was a real overreach: only No Show + (the one no-show type that bills) was ever meant to use a fixed default instead of asking — a regular session's duration is meaningfully different per session and needs to be typed in, same as it always was before 2026-07-04. Reverted the "no dropdown at all" part in both `src/pages/teacher/TeacherSessionFormPage.tsx` and `src/pages/admin/AdminSessionLogFormPage.tsx` (duplicated logic, kept in sync manually — see `db/docs/HOURS_AND_REVENUE_LOGIC.md`): the "Session duration" dropdown (`DURATION_OPTIONS`, 0.25–2 hrs) is back for `!noShowType || isNoShowPlus` (i.e. every regular session, plus No Show + since it still needs *a* duration). The only thing still driven by `session_duration_settings.default_duration_hrs` is the value No Show + starts at the moment that no-show type is picked (in `handleNoShowSelect`, replacing the old hardcoded `"1"`) — the dropdown still shows it and it can still be adjusted before saving, exactly like the original pre-2026-07-04 behavior did with its hardcoded default. No Show 1/2 still hide duration entirely (never bill, never store one). The admin's `/admin/session-logs` control (`SessionDurationEditor`) and `useSessionDurationSettings` hook were relabeled/recommented to describe this narrower scope; no schema or migration change — this was purely an application-level correction. Verified via `tsc --noEmit` (clean).

**Updated 2026-07-05 (later still — unified the admin/PC students LIST page, matching the earlier student-DETAIL unification)** — the PC's "My Students" page (`/teacher/students`) had drifted from the admin's "Students" page (`/admin/students`) the same way the two student-detail pages had before they were unified (see the entry above documenting that first unification): a much simpler list with none of the admin's columns (no student/parent username, email, phone, address, country), no search across those fields, and no country/package-status filters. Extracted the shared table/search/filter logic into `src/components/students/StudentsListView.tsx` — both `AdminStudentsPage.tsx` and `TeacherStudentsPage.tsx` are now thin wrappers (layout + role-specific bits only), same pattern as `StudentDetailView.tsx`. The two roles differ only in: which students are visible (`allowedIds` — undefined for admin, the PC's own assigned-student id set otherwise); the header action (admin's "Add / Renew Student" vs the PC's existing "+ Assign student" self-service flow, kept local to `TeacherStudentsPage.tsx` and passed in via a `children` slot rendered between the header and the search/filter card); and an optional per-row "Remove" action (PC-only, unassigns via the existing `unassignStudent`). **Important security nuance, called out explicitly since this was requested as a "security" fix**: `allowedIds` is a *display-scoping* prop, not row-level security — the `students` table's own RLS (`Authenticated users can read students`, `USING (true)`) has always let any authenticated user (including a PC) read every student row directly via the API; this was already true before this change and is unaffected by it. What genuinely IS RLS-scoped (unchanged by this refactor, verified earlier — see the 2026-07-02 multi-pool-packages entry) is the *package/session data* `student_packages`/`invoices`/etc. return only for a PC's own assigned students; a PC could already see every student's name/contact info via a direct query before this change, same as after. If tightening `students` SELECT itself is wanted, that's a separate, larger RLS change (would need auditing every existing read path — enrollment, admin pages, `StudentSearch`'s cross-role usage — before touching it) and was not part of this fix. Verified via `tsc -b` (the stricter project build, which caught a real type mismatch a plain `tsc --noEmit` missed — `DataTable`'s `onDelete` takes `(row: Student) => void`, not `(studentId: string) => void`) and a full `vite build`; no schema/migration change.

**Updated 2026-07-05 (later still — merged teacher/coach Profile + Settings; fixed the email-change confusion; decoupled country/dial-code; removed the profile-photo feature app-wide)** — several related fixes to `/teacher/profile`, prompted by a report that changing the "Email" field there didn't seem to work:
- **Root cause of the email confusion, confirmed live**: `TeacherProfilePage.tsx`'s "Email" field only ever updated `teachers.email` (a contact-info column) via `updateMyProfile()`. The sidebar identity badge (`DashboardShell.tsx`) reads `profile.email`, which is `public.users.email` — a completely separate column, seeded from the real Supabase Auth email only once, at account-creation time (`create_user_profile()`), with no ongoing sync afterward. So editing "Email" on the Profile page silently updated one column while the visibly-persistent sidebar badge kept showing the old value forever — indistinguishable from "the edit didn't save." Verified via a role-impersonated `execute_sql` test (`set local request.jwt.claims` as the specific teacher's `auth.uid()`) that the `teachers.email` UPDATE itself succeeds cleanly under RLS with no trigger interference — the DB was never the problem. Fix: added `AuthContext.updateEmail()` (mirrors the existing `updateUsername()` pattern) that updates `public.users.email` and refreshes local profile state; `TeacherProfilePage.tsx`'s save handler now calls both `updateMyProfile()` (teachers.email) and `updateEmail()` (users.email) together whenever the email actually changed, so the sidebar and the profile page can never disagree again. Deliberately does **not** touch `auth.users.email` (the real Supabase Auth login credential) — login here is username-based (`get_email_for_username` → `signInWithPassword`), so nothing needs it to match, and changing it would trigger Supabase's secure-email-change confirmation flow this app isn't set up to walk a user through. **⚠️ Superseded 2026-07-05 — this reasoning was wrong and caused a login lockout; see the "login lockout after email change" changelog entry below.**
- Also removed a stale, confusing defensive check in `useMyTeacherProfile.updateMyProfile()` that referenced a migration file (`20260628000006`) which doesn't exist anywhere in this repo's history — dead code from some earlier debugging session. Replaced with a real, useful check: `teachers.email` is globally `UNIQUE` (see `teachers_email_key`, restored by `20260704010000_revert_teachers_email_unique_active_only.sql`), so a genuine duplicate-email save now surfaces as "That email is already used by another teacher account." instead of the raw Postgres constraint-name error.
- **Country / phone dial-code decoupling**: the Country `<select>` used to overwrite the phone dial code every time it changed (`COUNTRY_DIAL_CODES[country]`). On an edit-existing-profile screen (never a blank first-time form — the dial code is already parsed from the stored phone number on entering edit mode) that's an unwanted surprise, not a convenience — removed the auto-sync entirely in `TeacherProfilePage.tsx`; Country and the phone dial code are now fully independent fields there. (First-time forms elsewhere, e.g. enrollment, are unaffected and keep their own auto-fill-on-create behavior — that's a different, legitimate use of the same pattern.)
- **Merged `/teacher/settings` into `/teacher/profile`**: `TeacherSettingsPage.tsx` deleted, its route and sidebar nav item removed; username and password change (previously only reachable via the separate Settings page, using the shared `SettingsContent` component) now live as two more cards directly on `TeacherProfilePage.tsx`, which no longer uses `SettingsContent` at all. `SettingsContent` remains shared by `/student/settings` and `/parent/settings` only.
- **Removed the profile-photo upload feature entirely, app-wide**: deleted `AuthContext.uploadAvatar()` and the "Profile photo" card from `SettingsContent.tsx` (also dropped its now-dead `currentPassword` field while touching that section — it was rendered but never actually read by the password-change handler, in both the old `SettingsContent` and the old `TeacherProfilePage`). The `users.avatar_url` column and the `avatars` storage bucket/RLS policies are untouched at the schema level — this was scoped as a UI-feature removal, not a schema teardown; removing the DB side too would be a separate, explicit follow-up if wanted.
- Verified via `tsc --noEmit`, the stricter `tsc -b` (project build), a full `vite build`, and a Chromium boot showing zero console/page errors (this touches `AuthContext`, consumed by every page in the app). No schema or migration change — application-only.

**Updated 2026-07-05 (later still — real security gap: `students` was the only table in the whole schema with unrestricted RLS)** — found during a full-codebase audit prompted by a request to unify duplicated role-gated pages ("maintain security at db level, role-based access at frontend"). All three of `students`' policies were `USING (true)` / `WITH CHECK (true)` scoped to role `authenticated` — meaning **any** logged-in user (a student, a parent, any teacher — not just staff) could, via a direct API call bypassing the UI/app entirely: read every other student's full PII (name, email, phone, address, parent link), update any student's row (including reassigning `parent_id` to a different family), and insert fake student rows. There was also no admin `ALL` policy on this table at all — unlike the "admin gets `ALL`" pattern used consistently everywhere else in this schema (see §"Consistent pattern across all tables" below) — with admin actions instead riding on the same blanket authenticated-role policies as everyone else. This is the exact gap the previous students-LIST-unification entry (above) flagged as "a separate, larger RLS change... not part of this fix" — this entry is that follow-up.

Checked every actual read/write path first (see `db/docs/VERIFIED_DATABASE_STATE.md`'s new `students` RLS note above for the resulting policy set): the only client-side `UPDATE` is `StudentDetailView.tsx`'s admin-gated `saveContact()`; `useStudents.ts`'s `updateStudent()` helper exists but is called nowhere; the only `INSERT` is the `service_role` enrollment-confirm edge function, unaffected by RLS regardless; broad `SELECT` is genuinely needed by every teacher (not just PCs) for `StudentSearch` when logging a session, plus admin's Analysis/Reports/Packages pages.

Applied via `supabase/migrations/20260705140000_lock_down_students_rls.sql` (dropped the three unrestricted policies; added `"Admins can do everything on students"` (`ALL`), `"Staff can read all students"` (`SELECT`, any row in `teachers`), `"student_parent_read_students"` (`SELECT`, via the existing `can_view_student()` helper)). **Verified live, not just reasoned about** — created a throwaway second student row (`S_RLSTEST`) and ran `execute_sql` with `set local request.jwt.claims` to impersonate five real identities in turn (a non-PC teacher, a student, a parent, and admin), confirming: a plain teacher sees both students (staff read-all intact); the student sees only their own row and the parent only their child's row (previously both could see `S_RLSTEST` too — leak closed); the student's and the teacher's attempted `UPDATE` on `S_RLSTEST` both silently affected 0 rows (previously either would have succeeded — leak closed); admin's `UPDATE` still succeeds. Deleted the test row afterward and confirmed `students` still contains exactly the one real row (`S1`) unchanged. Ran `get_advisors` (security) after applying — no new advisories introduced; all warnings present are pre-existing, unrelated (`function_search_path_mutable`, public-bucket-listing, anon-callable RPCs) and out of scope here.

**Updated 2026-07-05 (later still — unified the admin/teacher session-log LIST page, first of a broader duplication cleanup)** — a full-codebase audit (prompted by the same request that led to the `students` RLS fix above) found the admin "Session Logs" and teacher "Session Logging" list pages were ~90% byte-identical copy-paste: the entire filter grid (year/month/date range/student-id/name debounce/program-type cascading logic), all table columns, and the CSV export shape had been duplicated verbatim between `AdminSessionLogsPage.tsx` and `TeacherSessionsPage.tsx`. Extracted into `src/components/sessionLogs/SessionLogsListView.tsx` — both pages are now thin wrappers (layout + role-specific props only), same pattern as `StudentDetailView.tsx`/`StudentsListView.tsx`. The two roles differ only in: `scopeToTeacherId` (undefined for admin — sees every session, scoped via RLS's `is_admin()`; the logged-in teacher's own id otherwise, passed straight to `useSessionLogs`'s existing scoping param, not a new mechanism); a "Teacher" filter dropdown and table column (admin-only, since a teacher's own list would just repeat their name); the `SessionDurationEditor` widget (admin-only); and the CSV export shape — preserved **exactly** as each role's export already behaved (admin's includes Teacher + Program Type columns; teacher's omits both, an existing asymmetry from before this refactor, not something introduced or "fixed" here). Verified via `tsc --noEmit`, the stricter `tsc -b`, a full `vite build` (bundle size dropped slightly from the deduplication), and a Chromium boot with zero console errors. No schema/migration change. The FORM pages (`AdminSessionLogFormPage.tsx`/`TeacherSessionFormPage.tsx`, ~1120 lines each) and the detail/VIEW pages remain separately duplicated for now — tracked as follow-up work in this same cleanup pass.

**Updated 2026-07-05 (later still — unified the admin/teacher session-log DETAIL/view page, second of the duplication cleanup)** — `AdminSessionLogViewPage.tsx` and `TeacherSessionViewPage.tsx` rendered the identical full field list (every session field, same order, same styling — No Show pill, engagement pill colors, flag pill, video link, fathom summary, invoice preview, timestamps) via a copy-pasted local `Field` helper and near-identical lookups. Extracted into `src/components/sessionLogs/SessionLogDetailView.tsx` — both pages are now thin wrappers. The two roles differ only in: `canEditBase` (admin can always edit, ANDed with `!isLocked` inside the shared component same as before; a teacher can only if `teacher?.is_performance_coach`, matching the original `isCoach` gate exactly); `showId` (admin's field list starts with the raw session `id`, teacher's doesn't); `descriptionWhenUnlocked` (admin always shows "Full session record — every field on file.", teacher shows nothing when unlocked — preserved exactly); `cardClassName` (teacher constrains to `max-w-2xl`, admin doesn't); and route/label strings (`backPath`, `editPath`, not-found copy). Verified via `tsc --noEmit`, `tsc -b`, a full `vite build`, and a Chromium boot with zero console errors. No schema/migration change. The FORM pages remain separately duplicated — last item in this cleanup pass.

**Updated 2026-07-05 (later still — unified the admin/teacher subject-assignment editor, third of the duplication cleanup)** — `AdminTeacherSubjectsPage.tsx`'s local `SubjectEditor` sub-component and `TeacherSubjectsPage.tsx`'s inline logic were a near-verbatim copy of the same cascading Curriculum → Group → Subject picker, the flat Beyond Academics picker, the auto-assign-ungrouped-curriculum effect (creates+assigns a subject named after the curriculum when it has no subjects of its own, e.g. Standard Tests), and add/remove handlers — same state shape, same helper functions, same JSX down to comments. Extracted into `src/components/subjects/TeacherSubjectEditor.tsx` (`variant: "admin" | "teacher"`) — both pages are now thin wrappers around it, keyed by the teacher's own `teacherId` (admin picks any teacher from its own list/search/expand-accordion UI, which stays admin-only; the teacher page passes its own `teacher.id`). The two roles differ only in the "assigned subjects" presentation, preserved pixel-for-pixel: admin renders white/gray pills with an explicit "(inactive)" text label inside a bordered/tinted `Card` with a "Subjects for {name}" header (passed via the `header` prop) and its error text sits below the picker, plain and small; teacher renders blue-tinted pills for inactive subjects plus a color legend below, an "Add a subject" label above the picker, and its error renders as a boxed red banner above the picker (both exactly matching each role's pre-existing layout — not a new design). No behavior or schema change. Verified via `tsc --noEmit`, `tsc -b`, a full `vite build`, and a Chromium boot with zero console errors. The FORM pages remain separately duplicated — last item in this cleanup pass.

**Updated 2026-07-05 (later still — unified the admin/teacher session-log FORM, fourth and last of the duplication cleanup)** — `AdminSessionLogFormPage.tsx` and `TeacherSessionFormPage.tsx` were ~1120-line near-duplicates: the entire program-type cascade (top-level → sub-program → course-type derivation), the Curriculum → Group → Subject and Beyond Academics pickers, no-show handling (including the No Show + auto-duration/course-type-restriction logic), the teacher-feedback section, video-link/Fathom-summary handling, and the full create/update payload construction were copy-pasted, including comments. Extracted into `src/components/sessionLogs/SessionLogFormView.tsx` (`role: "admin" | "teacher"`) — both pages are now thin wrappers, each supplying only the one thing that differs at the data layer: which `subjects` hook backs `allSubjects` (admin's `useAllSubjects()`, including inactive subjects still assigned to a teacher, vs teacher's `useSubjects()`, active-only — preserved exactly as it already behaved) and, for the teacher/coach role, its own `useMyTeacherProfile()` identity. Inside the shared component, role branches only where actual behavior already differed: a free-choice "Teacher" `SelectInput` exists only for admin (a teacher/coach's own identity is fixed via `currentTeacher`, computed once as `effectiveTeacherId` and used everywhere the old `teacherId` state was); `StudentSearch`'s `allowedIds` restricts a coach to their own assigned students (admin's search stays unrestricted); editing an existing session is blocked for a plain (non-coach) teacher via a "Not allowed" gate (admin can always edit, subject only to the existing locked-month check); and a handful of copy/layout differences (page title "Add/Edit session log" vs "Log a session"/"Edit session", card width `max-w-3xl` vs `max-w-2xl`, coordinator placeholder, the "no subjects assigned" hint text, and — a real pre-existing asymmetry preserved rather than "fixed" — admin's Cancel button always returns to the list while a teacher's returns to the session's view page when editing). No schema/migration change. Verified via `tsc --noEmit`, `tsc -b`, a full `vite build`, and a Chromium boot with zero console errors. This closes out the admin/teacher duplication-cleanup pass that started with the `students` RLS fix above.

**Updated 2026-07-05 (later still — full DB-vs-frontend security audit + catalogue-insert lockdown)** — cross-checked every frontend role gate against its DB enforcement (the point of the whole "security at the DB layer, role-based access at the frontend" pass). Findings:

- **Privilege escalation via self-update — checked, NOT exploitable (no change needed).** `is_admin()` and `is_performance_coach()` both key off `public.users.role`. The `users` "Users can update their own row" and `teachers` "Teachers can update their own teacher profile" policies have no per-column WITH CHECK, so on paper a user could `UPDATE` their own row and set `role='admin'` (or flip `teachers.is_performance_coach`, which `sync_teacher_pc_role()` propagates into `users.role`). Both paths are blocked by the `trg_prevent_privilege_escalation` trigger (`prevent_self_privilege_escalation()`), which fires even on the SECURITY DEFINER cascade from `sync_teacher_pc_role()` and raises on any non-admin attempt to change `role` or `is_active`. **Verified live** by impersonating a real teacher and attempting both the direct `users.role` update and the indirect `teachers.is_performance_coach` flip — each raised `Changing role is not allowed`. Left as-is; the trigger is the real enforcement.
- **Session-log edit gate — matches.** The frontend blocks a plain (non-coach) teacher from editing an existing session; at the DB level `session_logs` has UPDATE policies only for `is_performance_coach()` and admin (no plain-teacher UPDATE), so the gate is genuinely DB-backed, not just UI.
- **Over-broad catalogue INSERT — FIXED.** `curricula` and `subjects` each carried a legacy "Authenticated users can insert" policy whose WITH CHECK was only `auth.uid() IS NOT NULL`. **Verified live**: a real student could insert both a curriculum and a subject (catalogue-pollution integrity gap; not a data leak). Applied `supabase/migrations/20260705150000_scope_catalogue_inserts_to_staff.sql`: dropped both broad policies, and since a plain teacher legitimately creates subjects via `/teacher/subjects` (the auto-create-subject-for-ungrouped-curriculum path in `TeacherSubjectEditor`) added `"Teachers can insert subjects"` (`WITH CHECK my_teacher_id() IS NOT NULL`). Curricula need no replacement — only coaches (existing PC policy) and admins create them. **Re-verified live** across four impersonated identities: student now blocked on both; plain teacher can insert subjects but not curricula; PC can insert both; admin unchanged.
- `teacher_subjects` insert was already correctly scoped (requires a matching `teachers` row) — a student attempt raised an RLS violation, as expected. No change.
- Ran `get_advisors` (security) after applying — no new advisories; all present warnings are pre-existing and out of scope (`function_search_path_mutable` on helper functions, `anon/authenticated_security_definer_function_executable` on the RLS helper/login RPCs which are intentionally callable, public storage-bucket listing, and the leaked-password-protection auth toggle).

**Updated 2026-07-05 (login lockout after email change — root-caused, repaired, and hardened)** — a performance coach (`test.pc`) couldn't log in ("Incorrect username or password") after their email had been changed in-app. **Root cause:** the username→login flow is `get_email_for_username(username)` → `auth.signInWithPassword(thatEmail, password)`, and Supabase Auth matches the passed email against `auth.users.email`. The old `get_email_for_username` returned `public.users.email`, but the earlier profile-email fix (see the "email confusion" entry above, now marked superseded) updated `public.users.email`/`teachers.email` on an email change while **deliberately not touching `auth.users.email`** — so after the change the resolver handed Auth an address (`anvit4418@gmail.com`) that no auth account had (auth was still `sneha203btcse24@igdtuw.ac.in`), and every login failed. **Verified live**: `public.users.email`=`teachers.email`=`anvit4418@gmail.com` but `auth.users.email`=`auth.identities`≈`sneha203…`; only this one account was desynced (not systemic).

Three-part fix:
1. **Resolver hardened** (`supabase/migrations/20260705160000_resolve_login_email_from_auth.sql`): `get_email_for_username` now joins `auth.users` and returns `auth.users.email` (the address Auth actually authenticates against), so login can never again break from a `users`/`auth` email desync no matter which path caused it. `SET search_path=public` added (clears the linter's `function_search_path_mutable` note for this function). Verified: `get_email_for_username('test.pc')` now returns the correct auth email.
2. **Email changes now sync everywhere** via a new `update-user-email` edge function (service-role): it sets `auth.users.email` (admin API, `email_confirm:true`, immediate — no confirmation round-trip), `public.users.email`, and `teachers.email` together, with a pre-check that rejects an address already used by another account. Self-service callers change their own email; admins may pass `targetUserId` to change anyone's. Wired into `AuthContext.updateEmail()` (self), `TeacherProfilePage` (email change now runs first and aborts the save on failure; `updateMyProfile` no longer sends email), and `AdminTeacherFormPage`'s edit mode (routes an email change for a login-linked teacher through the function; unlinked teachers still get a plain `teachers.email` update).
3. **Data repair** for `test.pc` (user `89e92cca-…`): direct `UPDATE` on `auth.users.email` + `auth.identities.identity_data->email` → `anvit4418@gmail.com` (the address the coach had set in-app), cleared stale `email_change*` fields, confirmed all four sources (`auth.users`, `auth.identities`, `public.users`, `teachers`) now agree. Login unblocked (assuming the account password is known — the email mismatch, not the password, was the failure).

No table schema change; one function redefinition + one new edge function + client wiring. Ran `get_advisors` context earlier the same day — the new function carries no new advisory (search_path is pinned).

**Updated 2026-07-05 (later still — credentials/confirmation emails rendering as raw quoted-printable source; the real fix, after an incorrect first attempt)** — reported: the student-login/package-confirmation email (sent from `review-enrollment-payment` on enrollment confirm) showed up in the inbox as literal encoded source (`style=3D"..."`, trailing `=` at every wrapped line) instead of rendered HTML, and in one case as the *entire* raw MIME structure (headers, boundaries and all) dumped as plain body text. A first attempt added `debug: { encodeLB: true }` to every `SMTPClient` — denomailer's own docs call this a risky debug toggle ("USE WITH CAUTION AS THIS COULD INTRODUCE BUGS"), and it turned out to not fix anything: a follow-up test still showed the same corruption. The user's own observation broke the case open: **credentials emailed to the parent rendered fine; the same confirm's email to the student did not** — same run, same denomailer client, only the Subject line differed. **Actual root cause**, confirmed by reading denomailer 1.3.0's source directly (`config/mail/encoding.ts`, `client/basic/client.ts`): `quotedPrintableEncodeInline()` (used only for the Subject header) calls `quotedPrintableEncode()`, which (a) leaves literal spaces un-encoded inside an RFC 2047 `=?utf-8?Q?...?=` encoded-word — invalid per RFC 2047 §2, which forbids embedded whitespace in encoded-word text — and (b) hard-wraps the encoded text every 74 characters with a bare `=\r\n` soft break, which is valid inside a quoted-printable *body* but is NOT valid RFC 5322 header folding (a folded header continuation line must start with whitespace; this doesn't). Any Subject containing a non-ASCII character (here, the em dash "—") that also encodes past ~74 characters triggers the malformed fold, corrupting the header block for strict parsers — short subjects with the same em dash stay under the limit and are unaffected. This is exactly why the (longer) student-credentials subject broke while the (shorter) parent one didn't: `"Welcome to Ascend Now — Your Student Login &amp; Package Confirmation"` (~70 chars, encodes past 74) vs. `"Welcome to Ascend Now — Your Parent Login"` (~42 chars, stays under it). **Fix:** reverted the ineffective `encodeLB` change everywhere, and replaced the em dash with a plain ASCII hyphen in every Subject line across all 7 mail-sending edge functions (the only 6 affected subjects, all in `review-enrollment-payment`, `send-enrollment-invoice`, and `create-teacher-with-user` — the `notify-*` functions already used ASCII hyphens and were never affected), which keeps `hasNonAsciiCharacters()` false so the subject is sent unencoded and never enters the buggy path at all — robust regardless of how long dynamic content (student/course names) makes the subject. Also fixed a literal `"&amp;"` in the student-credentials subject (a Subject header isn't HTML, so that would have shown verbatim to the recipient) to a plain `&`. Redeployed all 7 functions (`review-enrollment-payment` v13, `send-enrollment-invoice` v8, `notify-no-show` v15, `notify-flag` v5, `notify-pool-issue` v5, `notify-package-threshold` v4, `create-teacher-with-user` v18). No schema/migration change, no `verify_jwt` change. The separately-reported "confirmation email is taking long" wasn't reproduced as a code-level delay — every email send in this codebase already goes through `runInBackground`/`EdgeRuntime.waitUntil` so it never blocks the admin's response, and `review-enrollment-payment`'s confirm path already reuses one shared SMTP connection for multiple recipients — flagged as needing more detail (which step, how long) if it recurs after this fix.

**Updated 2026-07-05 (later still — coordinator auto-fill on session logging was silently broken by RLS, not application logic)** — reported as "the Coordinator field should auto-fill from the student's assigned PC." The frontend already did exactly this (`SessionLogFormView.tsx`'s `assignedPcId`/`coordinatorOptions`/the auto-select `useEffect`, all pre-existing, unchanged here) — but `pc_student_assignments` only had a SELECT policy for a PC reading **their own** assignments (`pc_read_own_assignments`, `is_performance_coach() AND pc_teacher_id = my_teacher_id()`). Any other teacher — or a PC logging a session for a student assigned to a *different* PC — got zero rows back under RLS, so `getPcForStudent` always returned `null` and the field silently fell back to an unfiltered, unselected list of every coach (reproduced live: teacher `aayush gupta` logging a session for `S1`/divija pandey saw all coaches, none pre-selected). `supabase/migrations/20260705170000_teachers_read_active_pc_assignments.sql` adds `teachers_read_active_pc_assignments`, a SELECT policy letting **any** authenticated teacher (plain teacher or PC) read **active** (`unassigned_at IS NULL`) assignment rows regardless of whose student it is — mirroring the already-broad read access on `students`/`teachers`/`program_types`. Applied via `apply_migration` and verified live (`pg_policies` shows the new policy; `pc_read_own_assignments` and the student/parent policy are unchanged). No application code change — this was purely the missing policy.

**Updated 2026-07-06 (PC assignment: fixed infinite-recursion RLS on assign, and removed the PC's ability to unassign — admin-only now)** — two fixes to `pc_student_assignments`, applied via `supabase/migrations/20260706000000_fix_pc_assignment_recursion_and_lock_unassign.sql`:
1. **Infinite recursion on assigning a student — FIXED.** The INSERT policy `pc_assign_unassigned_student_to_self` guarded against double-assigning by inlining `NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pc_student_assignments existing WHERE existing.student_id = pc_student_assignments.student_id AND existing.unassigned_at IS NULL)` — a subquery reading `pc_student_assignments` *from within a policy on `pc_student_assignments`*, so evaluating it re-applied RLS on the same table and Postgres aborted with `infinite recursion detected in policy for relation "pc_student_assignments"` on **every** PC assign (both `/teacher/students` "+ Assign student" and `/admin/pc-assignments`). Fix: moved the "does this student already have an active coach?" check into a new SECURITY DEFINER helper **`student_has_active_pc(text)`** (see Functions below); the table is not FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY, so a definer-owned function reads it without re-triggering RLS, breaking the loop — same pattern as `is_admin()`/`can_view_student()`. The guard itself is unchanged (and is also backstopped by the `uq_active_pc_per_student` partial unique index). **Verified live** by impersonating the PC assigned to `S1` (teacher 133): assigning an *unassigned* student now succeeds with no recursion; re-assigning an *already-assigned* student is cleanly blocked by the WITH CHECK (RLS violation, not a recursion error).
2. **PCs can no longer unassign their own students — admin-only.** Dropped the `pc_unassign_own_students` UPDATE policy entirely, so a performance coach can no longer set `unassigned_at` on their assignments (i.e. can't "remove" a student from themselves). Admins retain full control via `admin_all_pc_assignments` (`ALL`). PC *self-assign of an unassigned student* (INSERT) is unaffected. **Verified live**: the assigned PC's `UPDATE ... SET unassigned_at = now()` on their own student now affects 0 rows; the admin's identical update still succeeds. Frontend: removed the per-row "Remove" action from the PC "My Students" page (`TeacherStudentsPage.tsx`) — it would now silently fail under RLS — and dropped the now-unused `onRemove`/`removeLabel` props from the shared `StudentsListView`. The admin's `/admin/pc-assignments` "Remove" button is unchanged.

**Updated 2026-07-06 (later — No Show + duration is now purely an admin setting, never shown in the session-log form; both no-show settings consolidated into a new Admin Reports "Settings" tab)** — the 2026-07-05 revert (see entry above) had restored the "Session duration" dropdown for `!noShowType || isNoShowPlus`, i.e. it still showed (pre-filled from `session_duration_settings.default_duration_hrs`, but editable) whenever No Show + was picked. Per explicit clarification, this was still wrong: the hours deducted for a No Show + are meant to be a fixed admin-controlled constant, never a per-log choice for a teacher (or admin, from this same shared form) to see or adjust. Narrowed the dropdown's condition in `SessionLogFormView.tsx` from `!noShowType || isNoShowPlus` to plain `!noShowType` — it now shows only for a regular session; all three no-show types (No Show 1, No Show 2, No Show +) hide it. No Show + still stores a real `session_duration_hrs` (unchanged — `isNoShowPlus ? Number(duration) : null` in the payload), it's just populated silently from `durationSettings?.default_duration_hrs` in `handleNoShowSelect` with no UI to override it, exactly as `session_duration_settings` already existed to provide. No schema/migration change — this was purely a form-visibility correction.

Also relocated both admin-editable no-show constants into one place: a new **Settings** tab on `/admin/reports` (`SettingsTab` in `AdminReportsPage.tsx`, alongside the existing Teacher Hours/Monthly Revenue/Reports tabs). `NoShowRateEditor` (the fixed payout-rate control, `no_show_settings.payout_amount`) moved out of the top of the Teacher Hours tab; `SessionDurationEditor` (the default-duration control, `session_duration_settings.default_duration_hrs`) moved out of the top of `/admin/session-logs` (`SessionLogsListView.tsx`) — both are now rendered together in `SettingsTab`, unchanged in behavior/validation, just relocated (and `SessionDurationEditor`'s now-unused `useSessionDurationSettings`/`useAuth` imports removed from `SessionLogsListView.tsx`). Verified via `tsc -b` (clean) and `vite build` (succeeds).

**Updated 2026-07-06 (later still — Settings tab redesigned; Foundation Program / All-In-One bundle pool hours made admin-editable, added to Settings)** — two changes:

1. **Settings tab visual redesign.** The initial version (previous entry) was flat "label: value … Edit" text lines with no visual grouping — reworked into a shared `SettingRow` component (bordered row, label + description on the left, value/edit controls on the right, consistent across every setting) and a `SettingsSection` wrapper (icon badge + title + description, one `Card` per group) in `AdminReportsPage.tsx`. No behavior change, no schema/migration change — presentational only.
2. **Bundle pool hours (Foundation Program / All-In-One) are now admin-editable**, added via `supabase/migrations/20260706010000_bundle_pool_settings.sql` — new **`bundle_pool_settings`** table (see Columns section below), replacing what used to be a hardcoded `BUNDLE_POOL_DEFS` object byte-for-byte duplicated in `src/pages/admin/AdminEnrollStudentPage.tsx` (preview only) and `supabase/functions/review-enrollment-payment/index.ts` (authoritative — actually creates the pools on confirm), each carrying a comment demanding the two stay in sync by hand. Seeded with the exact 7 pool/hour values the old object had (verified live, matches exactly). `AdminEnrollStudentPage.tsx` now reads the same data via a new `useBundlePoolSettings()` hook (`poolDefsByBundle`, grouped in the identical shape the old const was, so the preview logic itself didn't need to change); `review-enrollment-payment` was redeployed (v14) to query `bundle_pool_settings` via its service-role client instead of the hardcoded const. **Per explicit instruction, editing lives ONLY in the new "Bundle pool hours" sections of the Admin Reports Settings tab** — every other consumer only reads.
   - **Caught and fixed before shipping**: the initial migration granted `authenticated` `SELECT/UPDATE` but nothing to `service_role` — would have broken the very first live bundle purchase confirmation with `permission denied for table bundle_pool_settings` (same class of bug documented several times already in the Grants section below). Fixed same-day via `supabase/migrations/20260706020000_bundle_pool_settings_service_role_grant.sql`. Verified via `information_schema.role_table_grants`.
   - Verified via `tsc -b` (clean), `vite build` (succeeds), and live `execute_sql` confirming the seeded rows and the corrected grants.

**Updated 2026-07-06 (later still — first-login mandatory profile completion for students/parents)** — added via `supabase/migrations/20260706000000_first_login_profile_completion.sql`:
- `students` gains three nullable columns — **`graduation_year`** (smallint), **`birthday`** (date), **`school`** (text) — alongside the existing `phone_number`, none of which are required at admin add/renewal time (admin may not know them). Instead, `StudentLayout.tsx`/`ParentLayout.tsx` gate every other student/parent route behind a mandatory "complete your profile" form the moment any of a student's four fields (phone, graduation year, birthday, school) or a parent's own phone is still null — see `src/utils/profileCompletion.ts`. There is deliberately no separate "profile completed" flag; the gate is purely "is a required field still null on the row", so a field admin already captured, or one side already filled in (e.g. a parent filling in a child too young for their own login), is never asked again of the other side.
- `enrollment_requests` gains the same three columns, also nullable/optional — admin's `/admin/students/enroll` "New Student" form can fill them in if known; `review-enrollment-payment` copies them straight onto the new `students` row on confirm (left null otherwise, same as it already does for `phone_number`).
- Three new RLS UPDATE policies, since `students` was locked to admin-only writes by the 2026-07-05 lockdown (see that entry below) and `parents` had no UPDATE policy at all: **`students_update_own`** (a student can update their own row, `user_id = auth.uid()`), **`parents_update_own_children`** (a parent can update their linked children's rows), **`parents_update_own`** (a parent can update their own `parents` row, `user_id = auth.uid()`). No base-`GRANT` change needed — `authenticated` already has `UPDATE` on both tables (see Grants below).
- Since RLS USING/WITH CHECK can't restrict *which columns* change within an otherwise-allowed row update, two new BEFORE UPDATE triggers close a real gap the two policies above would otherwise open: **`prevent_non_admin_student_relink`** (blocks a non-admin from changing `students.parent_id`/`user_id`) and **`prevent_non_admin_parent_relink`** (blocks a non-admin from changing `parents.user_id`) — without these, a student could re-point their own `parent_id` at any existing parent's id (or vice versa) and hand an unrelated account read access to their data via `can_view_student()`. Same pattern as the existing `prevent_self_privilege_escalation` trigger on `users`/`teachers`.
- New pages: `/student/profile` and `/parent/profile` (added to each role's nav as "Profile") — the same field set as the mandatory gate, editable on an ongoing basis after first login; a parent's Profile page also shows a picker across their linked children (reusing the same optional child-info form as the gate). The admin/PC-shared `StudentsListView.tsx` and `StudentDetailView.tsx` were also updated the same pass to show (and, on the detail page, let admin edit) the new School/Graduation year/Birthday fields.

**This migration was originally applied outside the tracked path** (this session initially had no live Supabase MCP access; the SQL was run directly, most likely via the SQL editor) — `supabase_migrations.schema_migrations` had no row for version `20260706000000` even though the columns/policies were confirmably live, which is exactly the kind of drift this doc's "why this file exists" section warns about. Reconciled 2026-07-07 by inserting the missing tracking row directly (`insert into supabase_migrations.schema_migrations (version, name) values ('20260706000000', 'first_login_profile_completion')`) once the columns/policies/triggers were confirmed to match the migration file exactly — verified via `list_tables`/`execute_sql` (see next entry for the one real bug this same live-testing pass caught and fixed).

**Updated 2026-07-07 (real bug: infinite recursion on students/parents self-update; caught in live testing)** — right after the migration above went live, saving the student mandatory profile form raised `infinite recursion detected in policy for relation "students"`. Root cause: `parents_update_own_children` (UPDATE policy on `students`) subqueried `parents` directly (`parent_id IN (SELECT id FROM parents WHERE user_id = auth.uid())`), and `parents`' own `pc_read_assigned_parents` (SELECT policy, added 2026-07-05) subqueries back into `students`+`pc_student_assignments` — any UPDATE on `students` has to plan every UPDATE policy on the table, so evaluating `parents_update_own_children` pulls in `parents`' RLS, which pulls `students`' RLS again, looping forever. Exactly the same class of bug already fixed once for `pc_student_assignments` (see `student_has_active_pc()` below) — same fix: `supabase/migrations/20260707000000_fix_students_parents_relink_recursion.sql` added a new SECURITY DEFINER helper **`is_my_linked_child(p_parent_id text)`** (checks `exists (select 1 from parents where id = $1 and user_id = auth.uid())`) and rewrote `parents_update_own_children` to call it instead of the inline subquery — the definer function runs outside RLS re-evaluation (the table is not FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY), breaking the cycle. **Verified live**: impersonated both the student (`S1`'s `user_id`) and the parent (`P1`'s `user_id`) via `execute_sql`/`set local request.jwt.claims` — both self-updates now succeed with no recursion (rolled back, not committed); re-ran the `prevent_non_admin_student_relink` trigger test from the 2026-07-06 entry above to confirm it still blocks a student from changing their own `parent_id`; `get_advisors` (security) shows no new advisory classes beyond the same pre-existing `function_search_path_mutable`/`anon`+`authenticated_security_definer_function_executable` patterns every other RLS helper in this file already carries.

**Updated 2026-07-07 (later — admin/PC views weren't showing the parent's own phone; application-only, no schema change; corrected same day, see next entry)** — the first-login feature makes `students.phone_number` and `parents.phone_number` two genuinely independent values (each side fills in their own), but the admin/PC-shared `StudentsListView.tsx` and `StudentDetailView.tsx`'s "Parent / Guardian Account" card only ever showed the student's own phone — a parent's own phone was nowhere visible. Fixed by adding a "Parent Phone" column/field alongside the existing Parent name/Email/Username group, next to a relabeled "Student Phone".

**Updated 2026-07-07 (later still — over-corrected address/country too; reverted the same day)** — the fix above initially *also* split `address`/`country` into separate "Student"/"Parent" versions in both views, on the mistaken assumption that those had diverged the same way phone did. They hadn't: address/country were only ever meant to be the **one shared family value** this app always captured once (originally at enrollment) — nothing in this feature makes a parent maintain their own separate copy. Reverted: `StudentsListView.tsx` has one shared "Address"/"Country" column again (phone stays split, "Student Phone"/"Parent Phone"); `StudentDetailView.tsx`'s Contact Information card shows Student Phone/Parent Phone side by side but one shared Address/Country below; the parent-facing forms (`ParentCompleteProfileGate.tsx`, `ParentProfilePage.tsx`) no longer ask for address/country at all — phone is the only field a parent fills in there. `useMyChildren.ts`'s `updateMyParentProfile` was narrowed back to `Pick<Parent, "phone_number">` accordingly. `parents.address`/`country` remain real columns (still populated once at enrollment, unchanged) — they're just not exposed as an independently-editable "parent's own" value anywhere in the UI.

**Updated 2026-07-07 (later still — self-service profile fields are locked once complete, application-only, no schema change)** — a student's Profile tab and a parent's Profile tab (and, within it, each child's section) let the fields be edited indefinitely even after every required value was already filled in. Now, once `studentNeedsProfileCompletion()`/`parentNeedsProfileCompletion()` is false for a given student/parent, that section renders read-only (`StudentInfoReadOnly` in `StudentInfoFields.tsx`) instead of the editable form — a student/parent can no longer come back and change their own phone/graduation year/birthday/school themselves once it's on file; only admin can, from `StudentDetailView.tsx`'s Contact/Student Information cards (unaffected by this change). The mandatory first-login gate itself was never affected by this gap (it only ever showed the editable form while something was still missing, by construction), only the ongoing Profile tab was.

**Updated 2026-07-07 (later still — Profile pages show every field and merged in Settings, application-only, no schema change)** — `/student/profile`/`/parent/profile` only showed the fields this feature itself added (phone/graduation year/birthday/school), leaving ID/name/email/username nowhere visible on either page, and a student/parent still had to visit a separate `/student/settings`/`/parent/settings` page just to change their username or password. Both pages now show a full "Account information" card (Student/Parent ID, name, and email — email is genuinely self-editable, via the same `AuthContext.updateEmail()` → `update-user-email` edge-function path `TeacherProfilePage.tsx` already uses, keeping the Auth login credential, `public.users.email`, and the `students.email`/`parents.email` contact column in sync together) and Username/Password cards merged straight in — the same Profile+Settings consolidation `/teacher/profile` did 2026-07-05. `/student/settings`, `/parent/settings`, and the `SettingsContent` component they shared were deleted; `useMyStudent.ts`'s `updateMyProfile` and `useMyChildren.ts`'s `updateMyParentProfile` were widened to also accept `email`. First/last name and ID stay read-only everywhere (not self-editable) — only email, username, and password are, matching what a student/parent account is actually meant to control themselves.

**Updated 2026-07-08 (parent can now read a linked child's username; Profile pages simplified back to view-once/fill-once, no self-service email edit)**:
- `supabase/migrations/20260708000000_parents_read_children_users.sql` adds **`parents_read_children_users`**, a SELECT policy on `public.users` letting a parent read the `users` row of their own linked children (`id IN (SELECT s.user_id FROM students s JOIN parents p ON p.id = s.parent_id WHERE p.user_id = auth.uid() AND s.user_id IS NOT NULL)`) — previously only admin and performance coaches could read any `users` row but their own, so a parent had no way to see their child's username at all. Verified live via impersonated `execute_sql` (parent `P1` successfully reads student `S1`'s `users.username`); no recursion risk since neither `students` nor `parents`' own policies query `users`.
- The 2026-07-07 email-edit feature (`AuthContext.updateEmail()` wired into both Profile pages) was reverted the same day it shipped — email, like ID/name/username, is set by admin at enrollment and was never meant to be student/parent-self-editable; only phone/graduation year/birthday/school (the fields genuinely missing at enrollment) get a one-time fill, locked immediately after. `useMyStudent.ts`/`useMyChildren.ts`'s editable-field types were narrowed back accordingly (no `email`).
- Both Profile pages were also restructured from several small cards into one merged "Your Information" card per person (ID/username/name/email always shown read-only, phone/graduation year/birthday/school shown read-only once complete or as the one-time editable form otherwise) — Username/Password stay as their own action cards below it, and the "can't be changed here" note now appears once at the bottom of the page instead of repeated under every locked section. The parent's page also now shows each child's own ID/username/email (previously only phone/graduation year/birthday/school were shown for a child) as a single card per child (no child-picker tabs — every linked child's card is just shown in a list).

**Updated 2026-07-08 (later — the renewal form never got the optional contact/school fields, application-only, no schema change)**: `AdminEnrollStudentPage.tsx`'s "Renew Existing Student" flow only ever showed a student search box + Package section — none of phone/address/country/graduation year/birthday/school were visible or editable there, even though the original ask was "not compulsory in the add/renewal form" (i.e. both forms, not just New Student). Added the identical "Student details" section to the renewal flow once a student is selected, pre-filled from that student's current row and editable if admin knows something that isn't on file yet. Since a renewal never goes through `review-enrollment-payment`'s student-creation code path (that function only creates/tops up packages for a renewal), these are saved directly to the `students` row via `useStudents().updateStudent()` on submit — only the fields actually changed are sent, so an untouched field is never overwritten. The enrollment request's own `phone_number`/`address`/`country` (used on the invoice PDF/email) now reflect whatever was just saved rather than the pre-edit value.

**Updated 2026-07-09 (real bug: one phone number was silently duplicated onto both the new student and the new parent at creation; caught in live testing)** — `review-enrollment-payment`'s `new_student` branch inserted the same `enrollment.phone_number` value into **both** the new `students` row's `phone_number` **and** the new `parents` row's `phone_number`. This directly defeated the whole point of the first-login feature (student and parent are supposed to each have their own independent phone) for every account created through the admin "New Student" flow — the admin enroll form only ever collected one phone number and the edge function fanned it out to both new rows. Fixed via `supabase/migrations/20260709000000_enrollment_requests_student_phone.sql`, adding **`enrollment_requests.student_phone_number`** (nullable text) — genuinely separate from the existing `phone_number` column, which remains the parent/primary-contact phone (also used for the invoice + the new parent account). `review-enrollment-payment`'s student insert now writes `enrollment.student_phone_number` to `students.phone_number`; the parent insert is unchanged (it already correctly used `enrollment.phone_number`). Redeployed as version 15 (ACTIVE). `AdminEnrollStudentPage.tsx`'s "New Student" form now collects the student's own phone separately from the parent's phone (both shown, clearly separated into a "Student information" section and a "Parent information" section, with Address/Country grouped under Parent since those remain one shared family value); the "Renew Existing Student" flow was similarly given its own explicit parent-phone field (fetched from and saved back to the linked `parents` row directly, alongside the existing student-phone field) so a renewal no longer risks the same one-value-two-people confusion. `useEnrollmentRequests.ts`'s `createEnrollmentRequest`/`updateEnrollmentRequest` input types and `src/types/database.ts`'s `EnrollmentRequest` interface both gained `student_phone_number`. Note: `AdminEnrollmentsPage.tsx`'s pending-payment edit modal was not extended with a `student_phone_number` field in this pass (pre-existing gap, tracked as an open item).

**Updated 2026-07-09 (later — email/username/password are self-editable exactly once, as part of the mandatory first-login form; not on the ongoing Profile page, application-only, no schema change)** — the 2026-07-08 entry above described email/username/password as fully non-self-editable (admin-set only, like ID/name). That was superseded the same feature area: a student/parent **may** set their own email/username/password, but only ever once, during `StudentCompleteProfileGate.tsx`/`ParentCompleteProfileGate.tsx` (the mandatory first-login form) — never from `/student/profile`/`/parent/profile` afterward. Both gates now have an optional "Account settings" section (Email, Username, New password/Confirm) alongside the existing required phone/graduation-year/birthday/school (and, for the parent gate, the optional per-child section); a username change goes straight to `public.users` via `AuthContext.updateUsername()`, an email change goes through `AuthContext.updateEmail()` (the `update-user-email` edge function — Auth credential + `public.users.email` + `teachers.email` if applicable) followed by `updateMyProfile({email})`/`updateMyParentProfile({email})` to keep `students.email`/`parents.email` in sync, and a password change goes straight to Supabase Auth via `updatePassword()`. Since these three fields have no "needs completion" flag of their own (the gate's trigger condition is still only phone/graduation-year/birthday/school being null), leaving them blank at the gate means they simply keep whatever admin set at enrollment — there's no way back in to change them afterward. `useMyStudent.ts`'s `EditableFields` and `useMyChildren.ts`'s `EditableParentFields` both include `email` again to support this (the dual-write path used by the gates), but neither Profile page exposes an email input — `StudentProfilePage.tsx`/`ParentProfilePage.tsx` show ID/username/name/email read-only, and phone/graduation-year/birthday/school editable once (then read-only), with a single disclaimer line at the bottom of the page instead of one per section.

**Updated 2026-07-09 (later still — last name is no longer required at enrollment; first-login gates gain editable name fields; UX fixes for password autofill and silent validation, application-only, no schema change)**:
- `AdminEnrollStudentPage.tsx`'s "New Student" form no longer requires **Last name** (student) or **Parent last name** — both are now optional, matching the same "optional at admin time, fill in yourself later" treatment already given to phone/graduation-year/birthday/school. No migration was needed: `students.last_name` and `enrollment_requests.last_name` are `NOT NULL text` columns that already accept an empty string, and every existing `${first_name} ${last_name}`-style display already tolerates a blank last name (worst case, a harmless trailing space) — there was no null-safety gap to close.
- `StudentCompleteProfileGate.tsx` now also shows an editable **First name**/**Last name** pair (first name required, last name optional) in its "Account settings" section, alongside email/username/password — the same one-time-only self-edit rule applies (never editable again from `/student/profile`). `useMyStudent.ts`'s `EditableFields` widened to include `first_name`/`last_name`.
- `ParentCompleteProfileGate.tsx` similarly gained an editable **Full name** field for the parent's own account (parents only ever had one `full_name` column, never split first/last), plus editable **First name**/**Last name** fields on each incomplete child's section — the only chance to fix a child's name who's too young to ever get their own first-login gate. `useMyChildren.ts`'s `EditableParentFields` widened to include `full_name`; `EditableChildFields` widened to include `first_name`/`last_name`.
- **UX fixes, both gates**: password fields now carry `autoComplete="new-password"` (previously the browser's password manager would sometimes autofill a saved password into "New password", since a bare `type="password"` next to email/username fields reads as a login form to browser heuristics) and email/username fields carry `autoComplete="off"`. Every required-field submit button (`StudentCompleteProfileGate`, `ParentCompleteProfileGate`, and the ongoing Profile pages' phone/school-details forms) no longer sits `disabled` while required fields are empty — previously that made the "missing field" error message unreachable, since the button couldn't be clicked in the first place. `StudentInfoFields.tsx` gained `missingStudentInfoFields()`, returning the actual list of still-blank required field names, so a submit attempt names exactly what's missing (e.g. "Please fill in: Graduation year, School.") instead of silently doing nothing.

**Updated 2026-07-09 (later still — real bug: a parent could never actually get past the mandatory gate by leaving the optional child section blank, application-only, no schema change)** — `ParentLayout.tsx`'s `needsGate` was `parentNeedsProfileCompletion(parent) || myChildren.some(studentNeedsProfileCompletion)`, i.e. it kept showing `ParentCompleteProfileGate` on every login/nav as long as **any** linked child still had an incomplete profile — directly contradicting the gate's own "Save & Continue" logic (and its own code comments), which only ever validates the parent's own phone and treats the whole child section as optional. In practice: a parent fills in their own phone/account-settings, leaves a child's section blank (as intended — a child can complete it themselves later), clicks Save & Continue, the parent-side fields save successfully, but the very same gate immediately reappears because the child is still incomplete — reading as "it's not letting me save" even though the save had actually gone through. Fixed by dropping the child-completeness check entirely: `needsGate = !!parent && parentNeedsProfileCompletion(parent)`. The ongoing nudge to finish a still-incomplete child's info now lives only on `/parent/profile` (each child's card there already stays an editable form until complete, unaffected by this fix).

**Updated 2026-07-09 (Homework Generator — grade publish gate + submission/publish notification emails)** — applied via `supabase/migrations/20260719000000_homework_grade_publish_gate.sql`. `grades` gained `published_at` (timestamptz) and `published_by_teacher_id` (bigint → `teachers.id`); the old **"Students can read their own grades"** policy was dropped and replaced with **"Students can read their own published grades"**, which additionally requires `published_at is not null`. Previously `grade-homework-submission` wrote AI/auto marks straight into `grades` and a student could read them the moment grading finished — no human-review step. Now that's enforced by RLS itself (not just hidden in the UI): a student's query against `grades` returns nothing until the paper-owning teacher explicitly clicks "Publish grade to student" on the review page (`TeacherPaperResults.tsx` → `useSubmissionGrade.publishGrade()`). `grade-homework-submission` (redeployed v10) now also nulls `published_at`/`published_by_teacher_id` on every regrade, alongside the pre-existing `teacher_reviewed_by`/`teacher_reviewed_at` reset, so a post-publish "Re-grade with AI" un-publishes the grade again rather than silently rewriting a score the student can already see. Also new: **`notify-homework-submitted`** edge function (`verify_jwt = true`, service role, same SMTP secrets as the other `notify-*` functions), fired fire-and-forget from `useStudentAttempt.submit()` — emails the paper-owning teacher (`generated_papers.created_by_teacher_id` → `teachers.email`) the moment a student submits, with a link to the review page. No new secrets. Full detail: `db/docs/HOMEWORK_GENERATOR_ARCHITECTURE.md`'s "Grade publish gate + submission notification email" section. Verified live via `execute_sql` reading back the new columns and the `pg_policies` row for the new RLS policy.

**Updated 2026-07-09 (later still — grade-published email + homework numbering, no schema change)** — new **`notify-homework-grade-published`** edge function (`verify_jwt = true`, service role), fired fire-and-forget from `useSubmissionGrade.publishGrade()`, emails the student (`notification_email` → `email` fallback) once a grade is published, with their score and a link. Also, both homework list UIs now show a per-student **"Homework #N"** label (`assignHomeworkNumbers()` in `src/utils/homeworkGrading.ts`, ranking by `published_at`/`created_at`) — purely a derived display value computed client-side from existing columns, no new column or migration.

**Updated 2026-07-09 (later still — reuse a paper across students)** — applied via `supabase/migrations/20260720000000_homework_paper_clone_for_student.sql`. `generated_papers.content_source_type` gained a fourth value, `cloned` (the `generated_papers_source_check` CHECK was rewritten so `cloned` requires neither `session_log_id` nor `content_upload_id`, unlike the other three values). New nullable self-referencing column `cloned_from_paper_id` (bigint → `generated_papers.id`) records provenance when a teacher reuses one paper's `questions_json` for a different student via the new "Or reuse an existing paper" builder option (`useGeneratedPapers.clonePaperForStudent()`), instead of generating fresh content. No RLS change — a teacher can only pick a source paper from their own already-loaded `papers` list, so the existing "teacher owns their own papers" policy already covers it correctly. Verified live via `execute_sql` reading back `pg_constraint` for both rewritten CHECKs and the new column/index.

**Updated 2026-07-09 (later still — deleted students S7/S8, no schema change)**: on request, hard-deleted students `S7` ("devi sneha") and `S8` ("vanshika") and their dependent rows — `enrollment_requests` (2), `package_topups` (6, via their pools), `student_packages` (6), `pc_student_assignments` (2), and one `draft` `generated_papers` row (id 8, no submissions) — then the students' own rows and their two Auth logins (`sneha203btcse24`, `x.sneha.x2006`), verified via `execute_sql` that both `auth.users` rows are gone (cascading `public.users`) and `students` count dropped accordingly. No `session_logs`, `invoices`, `submissions`, or `session_log_pool_resolutions` rows existed for either student. This is a one-off data cleanup via direct `execute_sql`, not a schema change — no migration file, matching the pattern of the existing `delete-inactive-teachers.sql`-style cleanups documented above.

**Updated 2026-07-09 (later still — mandatory first-login profile-completion gate added for teachers/coaches, mirroring the student one, no schema change)** — application-only. Admin's add-teacher form (`AdminTeacherFormPage.tsx`) only ever required `first_name`; `last_name`, `email`, `phone_number`, and `country` are optional there, so nothing previously forced them onto a teacher's row. New `teacherNeedsProfileCompletion()` (`src/utils/profileCompletion.ts`, alongside the existing `studentNeedsProfileCompletion()`) treats those four as required; `TeacherLayout.tsx` now checks it on every teacher route the same way `StudentLayout.tsx` already does for students, and shows a new full-screen `TeacherCompleteProfileGate.tsx` (`src/components/portal/`) instead of the dashboard until they're all on file. The gate also lets a teacher set/confirm their username and password (optional, same pattern as the student/parent gates), and an email change goes through the same `AuthContext.updateEmail()` → `update-user-email` edge-function path `TeacherProfilePage.tsx` already used, keeping the Auth login credential, `public.users.email`, and `teachers.email` in sync. No new columns or RLS — all four fields were already nullable, and the self-service `UPDATE` policy (`Teachers can update their own teacher profile`, `USING/WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id)`) has existed since the 2026-07-01 baseline. Unlike the student gate, these fields are **not** locked read-only afterward — `TeacherProfilePage.tsx`'s existing "Personal information" edit form is untouched and stays usable indefinitely.

**This file is the single source of truth for schema.** If `README.md`, `db.md`, `db/README.md`, `db/schema/*.sql`, or `supabase/migrations/*.sql` ever disagree with this file, this file wins — see "Why this file exists" at the bottom for why those other sources drift.

---

## Tables (30 total, `public` schema — `parents` added 2026-09-10)

> **The "Rows (live)" column below is a snapshot of the *old* project (`uqyuczvckqxgtilpzarh`) and is stale.** The current project (`rpavogsdladesmghwodg`) carries the same schema but is essentially empty — see the 2026-07-29 entry above for the verified counts. Table/column/policy structure below is still accurate; only the counts are not.

| Table | Rows (live) | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `users` | 1 | Central identity, 1-to-1 with `auth.users`. The bootstrap admin `admin@123`, re-seeded 2026-07-29 after the project was left with zero users (the stale pre-purge snapshot recorded 9) |
| `teachers` | 16 | Teacher profiles (a performance coach is a teacher with `is_performance_coach = true`, a college counsellor one with `is_college_counselor = true`). 5 coaches + 1 test teacher re-created after the 2026-07-27 purge, Batu's 6 subject teachers added 2026-07-28, then the counsellors and David (`DAVD26-20`, added 2026-08-01, no login) — see changelog |
| `teacher_subjects` | 8 | Junction: which subjects/curricula a teacher teaches. Emptied by the 2026-07-27 purge, repopulated 2026-07-28 with Batu's 6 teachers' IBMYP subjects |
| `admins` | 1 | Minimal admin marker row — the bootstrap admin `admin@123` (id 1) |
| `students` | 1 | Student records (structured — superseded the old free-text name fields). Emptied by the 2026-07-27 purge, then `S1` (Batu Ozcelik) seeded 2026-07-28; the earlier 2026-07-07 student/parent-merge purge and the removal of the `parents` table still stand. The former parent's info lives as plain fields on the student row when any exist |
| `parents` | 0 | Household parent/guardian accounts (added 2026-09-10). One row owns one login and any number of students via `students.parent_id`, so siblings share a parent. Read-only access to the children's data via `can_view_student()` |
| `pc_student_assignments` | 1 | Which performance coach is assigned to which student — Rana Walid → `S1` (seeded 2026-07-28) |
| `cc_student_assignments` | 1 | Which college counsellor is (or was) assigned to which student, with its own `active`/`completed` status (added 2026-07-31). Sumer Broota → `BATO26-1`, active |
| `program_types` | 10 | Top-level "type of program" dropdown, now hierarchical via `parent_id` (updated 2026-07-02 — see Seed Data below) |
| `course_types` | 6 | Package/invoice-facing course categories — Academic, Beyond Academic, and (as of 2026-07-02) College Counselling are active; Edge, General, Foundation Program are not |
| `subject_categories` | 7 | Only 1 active row now (`Academic`) — Beyond Academic subjects no longer use this table for grouping at all (see below); College Support deactivated 2026-07-02 (superseded by the College Counselling program/course type); Passion Projects/Career Exploration deactivated 2026-07-02 and re-created as plain `subjects` rows instead |
| `curricula` | 12 | IBDP, IGCSE, Standard Tests, etc. (ACT/SAT/TOEFL/IELTS consolidated into "Standard Tests" as four plain subjects, no groups — see Seed Data below) |
| `curriculum_groups` | 40 | Sub-groupings within a curriculum |
| `subjects` | 454 | Subject catalogue (mostly academic, curriculum-linked) — includes `Passion Projects` and `Career Exploration` as of 2026-07-02, now plain flat Beyond Academic subjects rather than category labels. All 110 inactive rows were deleted 2026-07-02 (see "inactive cleanup" note above). As of 2026-07-09, also includes the College Counselling subjects (`category = 'college_counselling'`) — `College Counselling`, `College Essays`, and (as of 2026-07-28) `Career Exploration - CC` |
| `session_logs` | 86 | Core session-logging table — `BATO26-1`'s 69 Academic logs across 6 teachers, 2025-12-30 → 2026-05-10 (seeded 2026-07-28); 12 College Counselling logs by Sumer Broota, 2026-01-13 → 2026-07-17; 3 Beyond Academic / Passion Project logs by David, 2026-06-07 → 2026-06-20; and 2 non-billable Demo Lessons (one per teacher) — all seeded 2026-08-01 |
| `student_packages` | 6 | Hour packages purchased per student/course type. `BATO26-1`'s standalone 100-hour Academic package (seeded 2026-07-28; 85.75 hrs used) + the 5 pools of his All-In-One bundle (seeded 2026-08-01; 122 hrs, of which the College Counselling pool is 9.25/40 used) |
| `package_topups` | 6 | Additions to a student package's purchased hours — the 100-hour top-up that funded `BATO26-1`'s Academic package, plus one per All-In-One pool |
| `student_package_members` | 12 | Which two children draw on a shared (parent-owned) package (added 2026-09-12). Backfilled with every child of the owning parent, so `DEVG26-5`'s two children sit on all six of that family's pools |
| `session_log_pool_resolutions` | 1 | Sticky (teacher, subject) → pool cache used to auto-resolve which of a student's several concurrent same-course-type pools a session counts against (see 2026-07-02 multi-pool note above). The one row: Batu + Beyond Academic + David + Passion Projects → the All-In-One bundle's "Primary Project" pool, recorded 2026-08-01 when the PC resolved his first Passion Project session |
| `invoices` | 0 | Generated invoices per student per period |
| `invoice_line_items` | 0 | Per-teacher/subject line items on an invoice |
| `invoice_packages` | 0 | Links an invoice to the package(s) it covers |
| `monthly_reports` | 0 | Locked/draft monthly reporting periods |
| `monthly_report_teacher_stats` | 0 | Snapshot stats per teacher, per locked report |
| `monthly_report_teacher_subject_stats` | 0 | Snapshot stats per teacher+subject, per locked report |
| `monthly_report_student_stats` | 0 | Snapshot stats per student, per locked report |
| `monthly_report_student_subject_stats` | 0 | Snapshot stats per student+subject, per locked report |
| `monthly_report_subject_stats` | 0 | Snapshot stats per subject, per locked report |
| `zoom_invoices` | 0 | Teacher-uploaded Zoom invoice files, per month |
| `enrollment_requests` | 0 | New-student / renewal invoice + payment-link requests, from submission through admin confirmation (added 2026-07-02). Emptied by the 2026-07-27 purge (was 5). Package and payment-proof details split out into the two child tables below as of 2026-07-25 |
| `enrollment_request_packages` | 0 | One row per package on an enrollment request's invoice — a request can carry more than one (added 2026-07-25). Emptied by the 2026-07-27 purge (was 5) |
| `enrollment_request_payment_proofs` | 0 | One row per uploaded payment screenshot on an enrollment request — a request can carry more than one (added 2026-07-25). Emptied by the 2026-07-27 purge (was 5) |
| `no_show_settings` | 1 | Single-row settings table — the fixed amount paid to a teacher for a No Show 2 or No Show + (added 2026-07-04) |
| `session_duration_settings` | 1 | Single-row settings table — the default duration (hrs) applied to a new No Show + log only (added 2026-07-04; scope corrected 2026-07-05 — see changelog) |
| `bundle_pool_settings` | 7 | One row per Foundation Program / All-In-One pool — the admin-editable hours each pool gets on purchase (added 2026-07-06, replacing the old hardcoded `BUNDLE_POOL_DEFS`) |

All 40 tables have RLS **enabled** (verified live via `execute_sql` against `pg_tables`, 2026-07-25 — this count includes every table added by every changelog entry above, superseding the original "29 tables" snapshot this line started as).

---

## Columns, per table

### `users`
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid | NO | — (matches `auth.users.id`) |
| username | text | NO | — |
