| email | text | NO | — |
| full_name | text | YES | — |
| role | `user_role` enum | NO | — |
| is_active | boolean | NO | true |
| created_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |
| updated_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |
| avatar_url | text | YES | — |

No password column anywhere — Supabase Auth (`auth.users`) owns all password hashing/verification.

### `teachers`
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | text (PK) | NO | generated by `BEFORE INSERT` trigger `trg_generate_teacher_id` — e.g. `RANW26-3` (converted from bigint 2026-07-29) |
| first_name | text | NO | — |
| last_name | text | NO | — required since 2026-07-29 (`NOT NULL` + `teachers_last_name_not_blank` CHECK `btrim(last_name) <> ''`) |
| country | text | YES | — |
| email | text | YES (unique) | — |
| phone_number | text | YES | — |
| created_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |
| updated_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |
| user_id | uuid → `users.id` | YES | — |
| is_performance_coach | boolean | NO | false |
| is_college_counselor | boolean | NO | false — added 2026-07-31; independent of `is_performance_coach`, a teacher may carry both |
| is_active | boolean | NO | true |

### `teacher_subjects`
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | bigint (identity) | NO | — |
| teacher_id | bigint → `teachers.id` | NO | — |
| subject_id | smallint → `subjects.id` | NO | — |
| curriculum_id | smallint → `curricula.id` | YES | — |
| created_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |

Unique per `(teacher_id, subject_id, curriculum_id)` when academic (curriculum set), or `(teacher_id, subject_id)` when non-academic (curriculum null) — two partial unique indexes, not one plain constraint.

### `admins`
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | text (PK) | NO | generated by `BEFORE INSERT` trigger `trg_generate_admin_id` — e.g. `ARIS26-2` (converted from bigint 2026-07-29) |
| user_id | uuid → `users.id` (unique) | NO | — |
| created_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |
| updated_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |
| phone_number | text | YES | — (added 2026-07-29, verified live) |
| country | text | YES | — (added 2026-07-29, verified live) |

`phone_number`/`country` mirror the same two optional fields `teachers` already has, so an admin's contact/location info is captured the same way a teacher's or coach's is. Collected on the inline "Add admin" form on `/admin/admins` (same `SelectInput` country picker + `PhoneInput` dial-code pair the teacher/PC form uses, so the stored phone keeps the `+<dial> <number>` shape) and written by `create-admin-with-user`. Both are nullable and nothing back-fills them, so admins created before this (including the bootstrap `admin@123`) have them NULL until edited. As of **2026-08-04** the app collects them from the admin themselves instead of leaving them NULL indefinitely: `AdminLayout` blocks every `/admin/...` route with `AdminCompleteProfileGate` while either is missing (`adminNeedsProfileCompletion()`), the same mandatory first-login gate students and teachers already had, and `/admin/profile` shows/edits both afterwards via `useMyAdminProfile` (an admin previously could not see their own phone or country anywhere). They stay nullable in the database — the gate is a UI rule, not a constraint, and a row can still be written NULL by SQL or by `create-admin-with-user`.

### `students`
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | text (PK) | NO | generated by `BEFORE INSERT` trigger `trg_generate_student_id` (see below) — e.g. `BATO26-1` |
| first_name | text | NO | — |
| last_name | text | NO | — |
| created_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |
| parent_full_name | text | YES | — the guardian's name as plain contact info, independent of the `parent_id` account link below (see the 2026-09-10 changelog) |
| parent_phone_number | text | YES | — the guardian's phone (added 2026-07-07) |
| phone_number | text | YES | — the student's own phone |
| email | text | YES | — the student's login email (creates the username) |
| notification_email | text | YES | — the "send updates to" address (added 2026-07-07); may match or differ from `email`, falls back to it when blank |
| curriculum | text | YES | — free text, one of a fixed set enforced app-side only: IBDP / IB MYP / IGCSE / A Levels / AP / NA / National Curriculum / OTHER (added 2026-07-07) |
| report_card_url | text | YES | — optional path into the private `report-cards` bucket (added 2026-07-07) |
| address | text | YES | — |
| country | text | YES | — |
| user_id | uuid → `users.id` | YES | — (added 2026-07-02; links to a login account, set only for students created via the enrollment workflow's confirm step) |
| graduation_year | smallint | YES | — (added 2026-07-06, verified live — see the first-login profile-completion entry above) |
| birthday | date | YES | — (added 2026-07-06, verified live) |
| school | text | YES | — (added 2026-07-06, verified live) |
| status | text | NO | `'active'` — ∈ `{active, paused, completed}` (added 2026-07-29, verified live); the student's engagement category, see below |
| status_changed_at | timestamptz | YES | — last time `status` was set (added 2026-07-29) |
| parent_id | text → `parents.id` | YES | — (added 2026-09-10) the household parent ACCOUNT this student belongs to; siblings share one. Null for every student enrolled before parent accounts existed, and for any family that doesn't want one. Independent of `parent_full_name`/`parent_phone_number` above. Indexed `idx_students_parent_id` |

**Student status (added 2026-07-29, `20260805000000_student_status_categories.sql`):** every student sits in one of three categories — `active`, `paused` ("On pause"), or `completed`. **Completing a student also unassigns them from their Performance Coach**, so a `completed` student never has an active `pc_student_assignments` row; that is exactly why the status lives here and not on the assignment (it has to outlive the row that produced it). Both the admin and the coach's student lists group by it (see `README.md` §3). Non-`active` values only ever come from `set_student_status()` (see Functions) — a plain `UPDATE` on this column is admin-only like every other column, because RLS is row-level and cannot grant a coach one column. Indexed by `idx_students_status`.

Unique index `students_user_id_key` on `user_id` **where `user_id IS NOT NULL`**. From 2026-07-07 to 2026-09-10 there was no `parent_id` column and no `parents` table; `parent_id` is back as of 2026-09-10 (see that changelog entry and the `parents` section below) as a nullable link to a household account. Separately from that link — the former parent/guardian's name and phone are plain fields on this row (see above).

**RLS (tightened 2026-07-05; loosened 2026-07-06 for self-updates; recursion fixed 2026-07-07; parent branch removed 2026-07-07 as part of the dashboard merge, then restored 2026-09-10 as a read-only household link — all verified live via impersonated `execute_sql`):** admin gets `ALL` (`"Admins can do everything on students"`). Any staff member (a row in `teachers`, covering plain teachers and performance coaches alike — needed since any teacher can search/pick any student when logging a session) can `SELECT` every row (`"Staff can read all students"`). A student can `SELECT` only their own row via `can_view_student(id)` (`"student_parent_read_students"` — policy name kept from before the merge, but the underlying function no longer has a parent branch). `UPDATE` is admin (`"Admins can do everything on students"`, matching `StudentDetailView.tsx`'s admin-gated edit form) **plus a student on their own row** (`"students_update_own"`, `user_id = auth.uid()`) — added for the first-login profile-completion form and the ongoing Profile tab. `INSERT`/`DELETE` remain admin-only; the enrollment workflow's `INSERT` runs via the `service_role`-based `review-enrollment-payment` edge function, unaffected by RLS. A BEFORE UPDATE trigger (`trg_prevent_non_admin_student_relink`, calling `prevent_non_admin_student_relink()`) blocks a non-admin from changing `user_id` through the self-update policy above (its `parent_id` clause was dropped along with the column).

**id generation (2026-07-29):** `id` no longer has a column `DEFAULT` — a `BEFORE INSERT` trigger (`trg_generate_student_id`, calling `generate_student_id()`) builds it instead, since the format now needs `first_name`/`last_name` from the same row, which a plain `DEFAULT` expression can't reference. Format: first 3 letters of `first_name` + first letter of `last_name` (letters only, uppercased; short/non-letter names pad or fall back to `X`) + 2-digit enrollment year + `-` + `nextval('students_num_seq')`, e.g. Batu Ozcelik enrolling in 2026 → `BATO26-1`. The trailing sequence number is still the actual uniqueness guarantee (same `students_num_seq` as before, already granted `USAGE` to `service_role`) — the mnemonic prefix is cosmetic on top of it, deliberately unpadded (a same-day follow-up dropped the initial zero-padding since a fixed width reads like an artificial cap). The trigger only fires when `NEW.id IS NULL`, so an explicit id on insert (a future seed/fixture) is still respected. Verified live via `execute_sql`: test inserts of `('Diya','Kapoor')` and `('Al',"O'Brien")` (short first name + apostrophe) produced `DIYK26-0002`/`ALXO26-0003` under the padded version, then `('Priya','Sharma')` produced `PRIS26-4` after unpadding — all three deleted immediately after confirming shape. Applied via `supabase/migrations/20260804000500_student_id_mnemonic_format.sql` and `20260804000600_student_id_sequence_no_padding.sql`.

### `parents` (added 2026-09-10)
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | text (PK) | NO | generated by `BEFORE INSERT` trigger `trg_generate_parent_id` (`generate_parent_id()`) — e.g. `SARK26-1`, the same mnemonic format students and staff use |
| first_name | text | NO | — |
| last_name | text | NO | — |
| email | text | YES | — where credentials were sent; also the duplicate check when an admin adds a sibling |
| phone_number | text | YES | — |
| user_id | uuid → `users.id` | YES | — the login, set by `create-parent-with-user`. Nullable so a record can exist without an account, mirroring `students.user_id` |
| is_active | boolean | NO | `true` |
| created_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |

A **household** account: one row owns one login and any number of students via `students.parent_id`, so a sibling enrolled later reuses the existing parent instead of creating a second account for the same family. That is the whole reason the role exists as its own login rather than as another view of the student dashboard — it is the one thing a student record cannot express, and it is why the per-student parent account removed on 2026-07-07 is not what came back.

Partial unique index `parents_user_id_key` on `user_id WHERE user_id IS NOT NULL` (one login → at most one parent). Partial index `parents_email_lower_idx` on `lower(email) WHERE email IS NOT NULL`, backing the case-insensitive "is this family already in the system?" lookup that the enroll form and `create-parent-with-user` both run.

**id generation:** `id` has no column `DEFAULT` — the `trg_generate_parent_id` BEFORE INSERT trigger builds it (3 letters of `first_name`, padded with `X`; 1 letter of `last_name` or `X`; 2-digit year; `-`; `nextval('parents_num_seq')`), and no-ops when an id is supplied so a seed row can pin its own. Letters-only regex, so a hyphenated/accented name never breaks the format — the trailing sequence number is what guarantees uniqueness.

**RLS (verified live by impersonation, 2026-09-10):** `admin_all_parents` (ALL, `is_admin()`); `parent_read_own` (SELECT, `user_id = auth.uid()`) — a parent reads their own row and cannot write it, matching how a student's own details are admin-maintained; `staff_read_parents` (SELECT, `EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM teachers WHERE user_id = auth.uid())`) so a coach sees the household on a student's detail page. That last one is scoped through `teachers` and **deliberately not** through `students` — the 2026-07-05 `pc_read_assigned_parents` policy did the latter and caused the `students` ↔ `parents` infinite recursion fixed on 2026-07-07.

**Grants:** `SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE` to `authenticated`, `ALL` to `service_role`, and explicit `USAGE` on `parents_num_seq` to both — the sequence backs a text PK through a trigger, not an identity column, so the table INSERT grant alone would not be enough.

**Access this table confers:** none directly. A parent's read access to their children's packages, session logs, invoices, homework, subject notes and PC/CC assignments all comes from `can_view_student()` having been widened to follow `students.parent_id` — see that function and the 2026-09-10 changelog entry.

### `pc_student_assignments`
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | bigint (identity) | NO | — |
| student_id | text → `students.id` | NO | — |
| pc_teacher_id | bigint → `teachers.id` | NO | — |
| assigned_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |
| unassigned_at | timestamptz | YES | — |

Unique index `uq_active_pc_per_student` on `student_id` **where `unassigned_at IS NULL`** — a student can only have one *active* coach at a time, but full assignment history is kept.

`SELECT` RLS: a PC can read their **own** assignments (`pc_read_own_assignments`), a student/parent can read their own child's (`student_parent_read_pc_assignments`), and — added 2026-07-05 (`20260705170000_teachers_read_active_pc_assignments.sql`) — **any** teacher (or PC) can read **active** (`unassigned_at IS NULL`) rows regardless of whose student it is (`teachers_read_active_pc_assignments`). That last one exists specifically so the session-logging form can auto-fill/scope "Coordinator (performance coach)" to a student's real assigned PC no matter who is logging the session; before it existed, anyone other than the assigned PC got zero rows back and the field silently fell back to an unfiltered, unselected coach list.

Write RLS (as of 2026-07-18 — assignment is now fully admin-only):
- **`INSERT` — admin only.** There is deliberately **no** PC INSERT policy. The old `pc_assign_unassigned_student_to_self` (a PC self-assigning an unassigned student) was dropped 2026-07-18 (`20260801000300_pc_cannot_self_assign_students.sql`) along with the UI's "+ Assign student" flow on `/teacher/students` — a performance coach can no longer assign a student to themselves at all. Only an admin can, via `admin_all_pc_assignments`. (The `student_has_active_pc()` SECURITY DEFINER helper that policy used still exists but is now unused by any policy.)
- **`UPDATE` — admin only.** There is deliberately **no** PC UPDATE policy (the old `pc_unassign_own_students` was dropped 2026-07-06): a performance coach cannot unassign/remove a student from themselves. Only an admin can, via `admin_all_pc_assignments`.
- **`ALL` — `admin_all_pc_assignments`** (`is_admin()`): admins can do anything — assigning, unassigning, and reassigning students between coaches (the `/admin/pc-assignments` flow).

### `cc_student_assignments` (added 2026-07-31)
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | bigint (identity) | NO | — |
| student_id | text → `students.id` | NO | — |
| cc_teacher_id | text → `teachers.id` | NO | — |
| status | text | NO | `'active'` — CHECK ∈ `{active, completed}` (no `paused`, by decision) |
| assigned_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |
| unassigned_at | timestamptz | YES | — set when the engagement completes |
| status_changed_at | timestamptz | YES | — |

Unique index `uq_active_cc_per_student` on `student_id` **where `unassigned_at IS NULL`** — one *active* counsellor per student, full history kept. `idx_cc_assignments_teacher` on `cc_teacher_id`.

Structurally the mirror of `pc_student_assignments`, with one deliberate difference: the `status` column. A PC assignment has no status of its own because its lifecycle *is* the student's (`students.status`); a CC engagement ends independently, so the state has to live here. Completing writes `status = 'completed'` **and** `unassigned_at` together via `set_cc_assignment_status()` — the row is never deleted, so session logs written during the engagement remain attributable to that counsellor and the student simply moves to the counsellor's Completed list.

RLS mirrors `pc_student_assignments` policy for policy:
- **`SELECT`** — `cc_read_own_assignments` (a counsellor's own rows), `student_parent_read_cc_assignments` (a student's own), `teachers_read_active_cc_assignments` (any teacher, `unassigned_at IS NULL` only).
- **`INSERT` / `UPDATE` — admin only**, via `admin_all_cc_assignments`. There is deliberately **no** counsellor write policy, the same rule as PCs since `20260801000300` — a CC cannot assign themselves a student. Status changes go through `set_cc_assignment_status()` instead, which is why no UPDATE policy is needed for the one write a counsellor *is* allowed.

### `pc_profiles` (added 2026-07-18)
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | bigint (identity) | NO | — |
| teacher_id | bigint → `teachers.id` (**unique**) | NO | — |
| department | text | YES | — (small badge above the name, e.g. "Management") |
| headline | text | NO | `'Performance Coach'` (subtitle under the name) |
| photo_url | text | YES | — (full public URL into the `pc-profiles` bucket, or null for the initials fallback) |
| about | text | YES | — (the "Hi there!…" intro) |
| achievements | jsonb | NO | `'[]'` — `[{ student_name, subject, institutions, institution_logo_urls?: text[], score_before, score_after, score_scale, helped: text[] }]` (`institution_logo_urls` = one or more university logo URLs, shown in place of the institutions text; legacy rows may still carry a single `institution_logo_url`, coerced into the array on read) |
| educator_experience | jsonb | NO | `'[]'` — text[] of bullets. **Unused since 2026-07-21** — the "Educator Experience" section was removed from the card/editor; the editor now always writes `[]`. Column kept to avoid a migration. |
| coach_responsibilities | jsonb | NO | `'[]'` — `[{ icon, heading, text }]`, one horizontal-timeline stop per entry (the "Performance Coach @ Ascend Now" section). `icon` is one of `"cap" \| "book" \| "certificate" \| "school" \| "calendar" \| "chart" \| "people" \| "message" \| "target" \| "heart"`. Older rows may still hold plain bullet strings; the app normalizes those to `{ icon: "cap", heading: <string>, text: "" }` on read. |
| education | jsonb | NO | `'[]'` — `[{ icon, heading, text }]`, one box per entry. `icon` is one of `"cap" \| "book" \| "certificate" \| "school" \| "calendar" \| "chart" \| "people" \| "message" \| "target" \| "heart"`. Older rows may still hold plain degree-line strings; the app normalizes those to `{ icon: "cap", heading: <string>, text: "" }` on read. |
| contact_email | text | YES | — |
| contact_phone | text | YES | — |
| is_published | boolean | NO | false (flipped true when the coach saves the profile; drives whether an assigned student sees it) |
| created_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |
| updated_at | timestamptz | NO | now() (via `touch_pc_profiles_updated_at` → `touch_updated_at()`) |

One row per **coach or counsellor** (`teacher_id` unique) — as of 2026-08-03 this one table backs both roles' profile cards; there is no `cc_profiles`. RLS: `Admins can manage all pc_profiles` (`is_admin()`, `ALL`); `Coaches manage their own pc_profile` (`teacher_id = my_teacher_id()`, `ALL`); `Staff can read all pc_profiles` (`SELECT`, any `teachers` row); `Students read their assigned coach pc_profile` (`SELECT`, an active `pc_student_assignments` row joins the caller's student to this profile's `teacher_id`); `Students read their assigned counsellor pc_profile` (added 2026-08-03, the same rule through `cc_student_assignments`). Index `pc_profiles_teacher_id_idx`. Photo lives in the public `pc-profiles` bucket (see Storage Buckets). As of 2026-07-21 the profile is created/edited by an **admin** from `/admin/pcs/:id` → **Profile** (the `Admins can manage all pc_profiles` policy lets an admin upsert any coach's row); the coach's own `/teacher/pc-profile` is **read-only** (the `Coaches manage their own pc_profile` policy still exists but the UI no longer writes). A counsellor's card is written the same way from `/admin/ccs/:id` → **Profile** (added 2026-08-03). Shown to the student on their **My PC** tab (`/student/my-pc`, moved off Overview 2026-07-21), and — when they have a counsellor — on **My CC** (`/student/my-cc`), which renders the same card with a "College Counsellor" timeline heading and without the coach card's stock closing photo.

### `program_types`
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | smallint (identity) | NO | — |
| name | text | NO | — |
| is_active | boolean | NO | true |
| created_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |
| parent_id | smallint → `program_types.id` (self-FK) | YES | — |
| type | text | YES | — |

Now a self-referencing hierarchy (e.g. "Offline Work" is a parent with children "Offline work for AN", "Offline work for students", etc.) — see seed data below.

`name` was globally `UNIQUE` (`program_types_name_key`) until 2026-07-02, when that was replaced with a coalesce-based unique index — `program_types_name_per_parent_key` on `(name, coalesce(parent_id, -1))` — so a name only has to be unique among siblings under the same parent (top-level rows, `parent_id IS NULL`, are still compared against each other via the `-1` sentinel, so duplicate top-level names are still blocked). This was needed so "College Counselling" could be both a top-level type and one of its own sub-programs. See `supabase/migrations/20260702040000_rename_college_counselling_and_standard_tests.sql`.

Unique index `subjects_name_cat_no_group_idx` on `(name, category_id)` **where `curriculum_group_id IS NULL`** — curriculum-linked subjects can repeat a name across different groups (e.g. "Math" under different boards), non-grouped ones can't.

**`curriculum_id`**: a subject previously could only be tied to a curriculum indirectly via `curriculum_group_id` → `curriculum_groups.curriculum_id`, which meant a curriculum with zero groups (e.g. ACT, SAT, TOEFL, IELTS — single standardized exams with no meaningful group breakdown) could never have any subjects at all. This column lets a subject attach directly to a curriculum with no group in between. At most one of `curriculum_group_id`/`curriculum_id` should be set for an academic subject that belongs to a specific curriculum; both null means "no specific curriculum" (the pre-existing "Other" bucket used in session-log forms).

### `student_packages`
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | bigint (identity) | NO | — |
| student_id | text → `students.id` | **YES** (since 2026-09-11) | — the owning student, for an ordinary package |
| parent_id | text → `parents.id` | YES | — (added 2026-09-11) the owning parent, for a **SHARED** package. `CHECK student_packages_one_owner`: exactly one of `student_id`/`parent_id` is set. **As of 2026-09-12 this says which household owns the pool, not who may spend it** — that is the two children named in `student_package_members`, and a sibling left off the pool cannot draw on it |
| program_type_id | smallint → `program_types.id` | YES | — |
| total_hours_purchased | numeric | NO | 0 | CHECK: `>= 0` |
| hours_used | numeric | NO | 0 | (added 2026-08-01) Stored, DB-maintained "used hours" total for this package — `SUM(session_duration_hrs)` of its linked `session_logs`, excluding No Show 1/2. Kept correct by `recompute_package_hours_used()` on every session insert/update/delete (including moves between packages), so the balance is an authoritative saved figure rather than a client-side re-count. Equals `computeHoursUsed()`'s package-scoped result; the frontend now reads this column for the headline per-package balance (per-subject/per-teacher breakdowns still aggregate the session rows). See `supabase/migrations/20260801000000_stored_package_hours_used.sql`. |
| created_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |
| updated_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |
| course_type_id | smallint → `course_types.id` | YES | — |
| status | text | NO | 'active' | CHECK: `active` / `closed` |
| closed_at | timestamptz | YES | — |
| is_locked | boolean | NO | false | (added 2026-07-02) |
| locked_at | timestamptz | YES | — | (added 2026-07-02) |
| locked_by_user_id | uuid → `users.id` | YES | — | (added 2026-07-02) |
| locked_hours_used | numeric | YES | — | (added 2026-07-02; snapshot of hours used at the moment of locking) |
| notified_50_pct_at | timestamptz | YES | — | (added 2026-07-02) |
| notified_75_pct_at | timestamptz | YES | — | (added 2026-07-02) |
| notified_100_pct_at | timestamptz | YES | — | (added 2026-07-02) |
| pool_label | text | YES | — | (added 2026-07-02) distinguishes concurrent pools of the same course_type for one student (e.g. `"Discovery Project"`, `"Primary Project"`); `NULL` for the default/general pool, which is what every ordinary single-pool package still has |
| package_type_id | smallint → `course_types.id` | YES | — | (added 2026-07-02) which bundle (Foundation Program / All-In-One) this pool is grouped under, for display/invoicing only — see "Multi-pool packages" below. `NULL` for every ordinary standalone package |

**Threshold notifications**: `notified_50/75/100_pct_at` are stamped by the `notify-package-threshold` edge function (fired from both session-log forms after a non-no-show session save) the first time hours-used crosses each threshold, so the student's assigned PC is emailed once per threshold rather than on every subsequent session. `on_package_topup_inserted()` resets all three to `NULL` on topup so they can fire again against the new, larger total. **As of 2026-07-05, these stamped columns are only used for the milestone path (0–100% within a real purchased-hours package) — a package that's actually over its limit (including a 0-hour placeholder) is never deduped through these columns at all; see the changelog entry below.**

Despite the `status`/`closed_at` columns, migration `20260702000000_packages_never_close.sql` suggests packages are meant to stay open indefinitely in current business logic — `is_locked` is a **separate, newer mechanism** (added 2026-07-02) for the same underlying need ("stop changes to this specific package") without resurrecting `status`/`closed_at`.

**Locking**: an admin manually locks a package (e.g. once its hours are fully delivered/reconciled) via the "Lock" button on `/admin/packages`. Once locked: `package_topups` inserts against it are blocked (`prevent_topup_on_locked_package()` trigger), and `session_logs` rows linked to it via `student_package_id` can no longer be inserted/updated/deleted (`handle_session_log_package_lock()` trigger). A renewal for that course type then starts a **brand-new** `student_packages` row with no reference back to the locked one — hours don't carry over. This is independent of `monthly_reports` locking, which freezes a calendar month rather than a specific package.

**Unique index** `student_packages_one_current_per_course_type` on `(student_id, course_type_id, COALESCE(pool_label, ''))` **where `NOT is_locked`** (as of the 2026-07-02 multi-pool migration, replaces the version keyed on `(student_id, course_type_id)` alone) — at most one *current* (unlocked) pool per student per course type per label, so ordinary students keep exactly one current pool per course type (label `NULL`), while a Foundation Program / All-In-One student can have several concurrently-active, distinctly-labeled pools of the same course type. Any number of locked historical pools can also coexist regardless of label.

**Multi-pool packages (Foundation Program / All-In-One)**: "Foundation Program" (reactivated a pre-existing inactive row) and "All-In-One" (new) are real `course_types` (see the 7-row seed set below) — but they're used purely for **grouping a bundle of pools on the packages UI and on invoices**, via `student_packages.package_type_id`. A session's `course_type_id` always comes from the teacher's "type of program" pick (Beyond Academic / College Counselling — the teacher-facing dropdown never shows Foundation Program or All-In-One at all), so each individual pool's own `course_type_id` — what `handle_session_log_package_lock()` actually matches against — is always the *real* deducting category, never the bundle course_type.

As of 2026-07-02, bundles are bought/renewed exclusively through `/admin/students/enroll` (the direct-entry bundle builder that used to live on `/admin/packages` was removed along with all other direct hour-entry — see the top-of-file changelog). Picking "Foundation Program"/"All-In-One" as the course type there and confirming the payment creates, via `review-enrollment-payment`: for Foundation Program, a `NULL`-labeled 10hr pool (`course_type_id` = Beyond Academic) plus a `"Discovery Project"` 24hr one (also Beyond Academic); for All-In-One, four labeled Beyond Academic pools (`Primary Project`, `Secondary Project`, `Extra Hours`, `Career Exploration`) plus one College Counselling pool — all tagged `package_type_id` = the bundle's own id. That's only the **first-time purchase** shape; renewing a bundle the student already owns instead requires picking exactly one pool on the enroll form (`enrollment_requests.bundle_pool_label`) and adds hours only to that one, leaving every other pool in the bundle untouched — see the `enrollment_requests` table entry and the edge function's own comments for the exact mechanism.

Because subject alone couldn't always reliably indicate which pool a given Beyond Academic session belongs to, `handle_session_log_package_lock()` first checks for a direct subject-name-to-`pool_label` match (e.g. a session logged under the "Career Exploration" subject unambiguously matches an All-In-One bundle's "Career Exploration" pool with zero PC involvement) before falling back to the `session_log_pool_resolutions` sticky cache, and finally to `pool_ambiguous = true` for a PC to resolve manually — see that trigger's entry for the full order. As of 2026-07-02, the same "Pending Deduction" resolution UI (assign a pool, reassign a session between sister pools, reset a sticky resolution) that admins get on the student-detail page is also available to PCs on `/teacher/students/:id`, scoped to their own assigned students via the same RLS policies (`pc_manage_assigned_packages`, `pc_manage_assigned_session_log_pool_resolutions`).

### `student_package_members` (added 2026-09-12)
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| student_package_id | bigint → `student_packages.id` ON DELETE CASCADE | NO | — | PK part 1 |
| student_id | text → `students.id` ON DELETE CASCADE | NO | — | PK part 2; also `idx_student_package_members_student_id` |
| added_by_user_id | uuid → `auth.users.id` | YES | — | the admin who assigned this child; null on the backfilled rows |
| created_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |

Which children draw on a **shared** (parent-owned) package. Owning the pool and spending it are two different things as of 2026-09-12: `student_packages.parent_id` says which household bought the hours, and these rows say which **two** of that household's children may actually use them. A sibling not named here cannot see the pool, cannot see its top-ups, and a session logged for them lands on a fresh zero-hour fallback pool of their own rather than deducting from the family's — verified live, see the changelog entry at the top of this file.

`validate_student_package_member()` (BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE) enforces all three rules a CHECK constraint cannot reach another table to enforce: the package must be **parent-owned** (a student-owned package has no members by definition), the student must be a **child of that same parent**, and a package already holding **2** other members refuses a third. The count of 2 is the business rule "2 students per programme"; change it here and in `SHARED_PACKAGE_STUDENT_COUNT` (`src/utils/sharedPackages.ts`) together.

RLS mirrors `student_packages`: `admin_all_package_members` (`is_admin()`), `student_parent_read_package_members` (the rows follow their package's own visibility through `can_view_package()`, so anyone who may see the pool may see who shares it) and `pc_manage_assigned_package_members` (through `is_my_assigned_package()`).

### `session_log_pool_resolutions`
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | bigint (identity) | NO | — |
| student_id | text → `students.id` | NO | — |
| course_type_id | smallint → `course_types.id` | NO | — |
| teacher_id | bigint → `teachers.id` | NO | — |
| subject_id | smallint → `subjects.id` | YES | — |
| student_package_id | bigint → `student_packages.id` | NO | — |
| resolved_by_user_id | uuid → `users.id` | NO | — |
| resolved_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |

Sticky (student, course_type, teacher, subject) → pool cache. When a student has more than one active pool for a course_type, `handle_session_log_package_lock()` can't auto-resolve `session_logs.student_package_id` from subject alone (see the multi-pool note under `student_packages` above), so it leaves the session as `pool_ambiguous = true` and unassigned until a PC picks a pool for it on the student's "Learner's actual hours" tab (`AdminStudentDetailPage.tsx`, "Pending Deduction" section). That choice is written here, keyed by `(student_id, course_type_id, teacher_id, COALESCE(subject_id, -1))` (unique index `session_log_pool_resolutions_key`), so the next session matching that exact key auto-resolves without asking again. A PC can delete a row to force the next matching session back into `pool_ambiguous` — e.g. once they know the underlying project has changed even though the teacher/subject pairing hasn't, which nothing in the data can detect automatically. RLS mirrors `student_packages`: admins get `ALL`, PCs get `ALL` scoped to their currently-assigned students via `pc_student_assignments`.

Same pattern as above, plus `subject_id`, `subject_name`, `subject_level`, `curriculum_id`, `curriculum_name` (and `program_type_name` on the teacher-subject variant) — all denormalized snapshots taken at report-generation time so a later rename of a subject/curriculum doesn't retroactively change a locked report.

### `no_show_settings` (added 2026-07-04)
Single row (`id = 1`, enforced by a CHECK constraint). No Show 1 pays the teacher nothing and never touches a student's package; No Show 2 pays the teacher this rate and doesn't touch a package either; No Show + pays the teacher this rate *and* still deducts an hour from the student's package (it carries a real `course_type_id`/`subject_id`/`session_duration_hrs`, unlike No Show 1/2 — see `session_logs.no_show_type` and `src/utils/noShow.ts`).
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | smallint (PK) | NO | 1 | CHECK: `id = 1` |
| payout_amount | numeric | NO | 30 | CHECK: `>= 0` |
| currency | text | NO | 'SGD' |
| updated_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |
| updated_by_user_id | uuid → `users.id` | YES | — |

RLS: any authenticated user can `SELECT` (teachers need it to compute their own payout on the Hours page); only admins can `UPDATE`.

### `session_duration_settings` (added 2026-07-04; scope corrected 2026-07-05; never shown/editable in the form as of 2026-07-06)
Single row (`id = 1`, enforced by a CHECK constraint). The default duration (hrs) applied to a new **No Show +** log only (the one no-show type that still bills) — set the moment that no-show type is picked, in place of asking, and never shown or editable in the session-log form itself (admin changes it from the "Settings" tab of `/admin/reports`, not per-log). **A regular session log's duration is typed in per-session as always** (a same-day-later 2026-07-04 change had this apply to every session log, not just no-shows — a bug, reverted 2026-07-05, see changelog). An already-logged session keeps whatever duration it was saved with when later edited.
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | smallint (PK) | NO | 1 | CHECK: `id = 1` |
| default_duration_hrs | numeric | NO | 1 | CHECK: `> 0` and rounds cleanly to 0.25 (15-min increments) |
| updated_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |
| updated_by_user_id | uuid → `users.id` | YES | — |

RLS: any authenticated user can `SELECT` (both session-log forms need it to default the duration); only admins can `UPDATE`.

### `bundle_pool_settings` (added 2026-07-06)
One row per (bundle, pool) — Foundation Program / All-In-One each fan out into several labeled pools at once when purchased, each with its own fixed hours. Replaces what used to be a hardcoded `BUNDLE_POOL_DEFS` object, byte-for-byte duplicated in `review-enrollment-payment/index.ts` (authoritative — actually creates the pools) and `AdminEnrollStudentPage.tsx` (preview only). Editable **only** from the "Settings" tab of `/admin/reports` — every other consumer (the enroll form's bundle preview, the edge function) only reads this table via `useBundlePoolSettings`/a direct `serviceClient` query.
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | smallint (PK, identity) | NO | auto |
| bundle_name | text | NO | — | CHECK: `IN ('Foundation Program', 'All-In-One')` |
| pool_label | text | YES | — | null = the bundle's own unlabeled/default pool |
| course_type_name | text → `course_types.name` (`ON UPDATE CASCADE`) | NO | — | which real course type this pool bills against (Beyond Academic / College Counselling) |
| hours | numeric | NO | — | CHECK: `> 0` |
| sort_order | smallint | NO | 0 |
| updated_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |
| updated_by_user_id | uuid → `users.id` | YES | — |

Unique index on `(bundle_name, COALESCE(pool_label, ''))` — a bundle can have at most one pool per label, including at most one unlabeled pool. RLS: any authenticated user can `SELECT`; only admins can `UPDATE`. `service_role` also has an explicit `SELECT` grant (added in a follow-up migration the same day — the initial migration missed it, and this project's `service_role` does **not** bypass base table grants; see "Grants" below) so `review-enrollment-payment` can read it. Seeded with the exact 7 rows the old `BUNDLE_POOL_DEFS` had: Foundation Program (unlabeled/Beyond Academic/10, "Discovery Project"/Beyond Academic/24); All-In-One ("Primary Project"/Beyond Academic/32, "Secondary Project"/Beyond Academic/24, "Extra Hours"/Beyond Academic/16, "Career Exploration"/Beyond Academic/10, unlabeled/College Counselling/40). Verified live via `execute_sql` immediately after the migration.

### `zoom_invoices`
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | bigint (sequence, not identity) | NO | `nextval('zoom_invoices_id_seq')` |
| teacher_id | bigint → `teachers.id` | NO | — |
| period_month | date | NO | — |
| file_url | text | NO | — |
| uploaded_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |
| uploaded_by_user_id | uuid → `auth.users.id` | YES | — |
| status | text | NO | 'pending' | CHECK: `pending` / `acknowledged` |
| acknowledged_at | timestamptz | YES | — |
| acknowledged_by_user_id | uuid → `auth.users.id` | YES | — |

Unique on `(teacher_id, period_month)` — one Zoom invoice per teacher per month.

### `enrollment_requests` (added 2026-07-02; package/payment-proof details split into child tables 2026-07-25)
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid (PK) | NO | `gen_random_uuid()` |
| enrollment_type | text | NO | — | CHECK: `new_student` / `renewal` |
| student_id | text → `students.id` | YES | — | set for renewals; set for `new_student` only after confirmation |
| first_name | text | NO | — |
| last_name | text | NO | — |
| parent_full_name | text | YES | — the guardian's name |
| email | text | NO | — the "send updates to" contact: resolves to `notification_email` if given at intake, else `student_email`, else (legacy) whatever was typed directly |
| student_email | text | YES | — creates the student's login on confirm (added 2026-07-03) |
| notification_email | text | YES | — the "send updates to" address as entered at intake, optional (added 2026-07-07) |
| curriculum | text | YES | — copied through to `students.curriculum` on confirm (added 2026-07-07) |
| report_card_url | text | YES | — optional, private `report-cards` bucket path, copied through on confirm (added 2026-07-07) |
| phone_number | text | YES | — the guardian's phone |
| address | text | YES | — |
| country | text | YES | — |
| graduation_year | smallint | YES | — (added 2026-07-06, verified live — optional, admin fills in if known; see the first-login profile-completion changelog entry above) |
| birthday | date | YES | — (added 2026-07-06, verified live) |
| school | text | YES | — (added 2026-07-06, verified live) |
| student_phone_number | text | YES | — (added 2026-07-09, verified live — the student's own phone; genuinely separate from `phone_number` above, which is the guardian's phone) |
| note | text | YES | — shared across every package line item on the request (there's no per-package note) |
| pc_teacher_id | bigint → `teachers.id` | YES | — | required (CHECK) when `enrollment_type = 'new_student'` |
| status | text | NO | 'pending_payment' | CHECK: `pending_payment` / `payment_submitted` / `confirmed` / `rejected` |
| payment_link_token | uuid (unique) | NO | `gen_random_uuid()` | the only access control on the public `/pay/:token` page — no login involved; covers every package line and accepts every payment proof for the whole request |
| rejection_reason | text | YES | — |
| created_by_user_id | uuid → `users.id` | NO | — |
| confirmed_by_user_id | uuid → `users.id` | YES | — |
| confirmed_at | timestamptz | YES | — |
| created_at / updated_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |

**As of 2026-07-25**, everything package-specific (`course_type_id`, `program_type_id`, `hours`, `package_size_label`, `is_bundle_pool_selection`, `bundle_pool_label`, `resulting_student_package_id`, `is_new_package_generation`) moved to the new **`enrollment_request_packages`** child table below — a request can now carry more than one package, so those can no longer be single columns here. Likewise `payment_proof_url`/`payment_proof_uploaded_at` moved to the new **`enrollment_request_payment_proofs`** child table — a parent can upload more than one screenshot (paid in separate transactions) on the same request. Both moves were a lossless split: every one of the 5 live rows had exactly one package and one proof, backfilled 1:1 into the child tables before the old columns were dropped.

Invoice/student details are **snapshotted** on this row (not read live off `students`) so the emailed invoice and the payment page reflect what was true at request time even if the student record changes later. No pricing/amount field yet — the invoice email lists package/hours/student details only; billing amounts are a future addition. See `db/README.md` for the full workflow (submit → email → upload proof → admin confirm/reject) and the three edge functions that drive it.

### `enrollment_request_packages` (added 2026-07-25)
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid (PK) | NO | `gen_random_uuid()` |
| enrollment_request_id | uuid → `enrollment_requests.id`, `ON DELETE CASCADE` | NO | — |
| sort_order | smallint | NO | `0` | preserves the order packages were entered in on the enroll form |
| course_type_id | smallint → `course_types.id` | NO | — |
| program_type_id | smallint → `program_types.id` | YES | — |
| hours | numeric | NO | — | CHECK: `> 0` (`enrollment_request_packages_hours_check`) |
| package_size_label | text | NO | — |
| is_bundle_pool_selection | boolean | NO | `false` | true only when `course_type_id` is a bundle (Foundation Program / All-In-One) **and** this line targets one already-owned pool rather than creating the bundle's whole set — see `bundle_pool_label` |
| bundle_pool_label | text | YES | — | identifies which of the bundle's pool definitions (matched by label against `bundle_pool_settings`) this line tops up. Itself nullable — a bundle's default/unlabeled pool is a valid target — so `is_bundle_pool_selection` is what distinguishes "no selection" from "the unlabeled pool was selected" |
| resulting_student_package_id | bigint → `student_packages.id` | YES | — | set once confirmed — stamped per line, since a multi-package confirm can resolve to several different `student_packages` rows |
| is_new_package_generation | boolean | YES | — | true if confirmation started a fresh package/pool for this line (no prior unlocked one existed) rather than topping up an existing one |
| created_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |

RLS: `admin_all_enrollment_request_packages` (`FOR ALL USING (is_admin()) WITH CHECK (is_admin())`, mirrors `enrollment_requests`). Grants: `authenticated`/`service_role` both `SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE`. `idx_enrollment_request_packages_request_id` indexes `enrollment_request_id`. Verified live: 5 rows (1:1 backfill from the 5 pre-existing `enrollment_requests` rows, each of which had exactly one package before this split).

### `enrollment_request_payment_proofs` (added 2026-07-25)
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid (PK) | NO | `gen_random_uuid()` |
| enrollment_request_id | uuid → `enrollment_requests.id`, `ON DELETE CASCADE` | NO | — |
| storage_path | text | NO | — | path within the private `payment-proofs` storage bucket (same bucket/policies as before this split) |
| uploaded_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |

RLS: `admin_all_enrollment_request_payment_proofs` (`FOR ALL USING (is_admin()) WITH CHECK (is_admin())`, mirrors `enrollment_requests`). Grants: `authenticated`/`service_role` both `SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE`. `idx_enrollment_request_payment_proofs_request_id` indexes `enrollment_request_id`. The public `/pay/:token` page never queries this table directly (same as it never queried `enrollment_requests` directly) — `submit-payment-proof` handles every read/write via the service-role key. Verified live: 5 rows (1:1 backfill from the 5 pre-existing requests' `payment_proof_url`).

### `package_renewal_requests` (added 2026-07-09)
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | uuid (PK) | NO | `gen_random_uuid()` |
| student_id | text → `students.id` | NO | — |
| course_type_id | smallint → `course_types.id` | NO | — |
| requested_hours | numeric | YES | — | CHECK: `> 0` when set — the PC's suggested hours, optional |
| package_size_label | text | YES | — | the PC's suggested label, optional |
| note | text | YES | — |
| requested_by_teacher_id | bigint → `teachers.id` | NO | — | the requesting PC |
| status | text | NO | `'pending'` | CHECK: `pending` / `acknowledged` / `renewed` |
| acknowledged_by_user_id | uuid → `users.id` | YES | — |
| acknowledged_at | timestamptz | YES | — |
| resulting_student_package_id | bigint → `student_packages.id` | YES | — | set when auto-resolved to `renewed` |
| renewed_at | timestamptz | YES | — |
| created_at / updated_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |

Not a second enrollment/invoice system — this table is only a PC's heads-up to admin that a student's package needs renewing; the actual renewal still goes entirely through the existing `/admin/students/enroll` → `review-enrollment-payment` flow. `status` moves `pending` → `acknowledged` (admin, manual, plain client `UPDATE`, no email) → `renewed` (**system-detected**, never a manual admin action — `review-enrollment-payment` matches `(student_id, course_type_id)` against any `pending`/`acknowledged` row right after it creates/tops-up the real package, on every confirm, not just the `renewal` enrollment type). Two edge-function-driven emails: `notify-renewal-request` (fire-and-forget on PC submit → every active admin) and `review-enrollment-payment`'s existing background confirmation-email pass (extended to also email the requesting PC once its request is auto-resolved to `renewed`). See `db/README.md` for the full mechanism and `README.md` §3.2/§3.4 for the two UI pages (`/admin/renewal-requests`, `/teacher/renewal-requests`).

### `subject_notes` (added 2026-07-17)
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | bigint (PK, identity) | NO | — |
| student_id | text → `students.id` | NO | — |
| teacher_id | bigint → `teachers.id` | NO | — | who filed the note |
| subject_id | smallint → `subjects.id` | NO | — |
| curriculum_id | smallint → `curricula.id` | YES | — | set for an Academic subject picked under a curriculum, null for the flat Beyond Academic / College Counselling ones — keyed identically to the student Overview's subject drill-down (`<subject_id>:<curriculum_id>`) |
| title | text | NO | — | the teacher-given note name |
| note_text | text | YES | — | optional written body |
| file_name | text | YES | — | original filename of the attachment, if any |
| file_url | text | YES | — | object path in the private `subject-notes` bucket, if any |
| file_type | text | YES | — | MIME type / extension of the attachment |
| created_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |

CHECK `subject_notes_has_content`: `note_text is not null or file_url is not null` — a note must carry a body or a file (or both). Indexes on `student_id`, `teacher_id`, `subject_id`. RLS (verified live): admin `ALL` (`is_admin()`); a teacher `ALL` on their own (`teacher_id = my_teacher_id()`); a performance coach `ALL` (`is_performance_coach()`); a student `SELECT` on their own via `can_view_student(student_id)`. `authenticated`/`service_role` granted `SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE` + sequence `USAGE`. Files are downloaded via short-lived signed URLs (`src/utils/subjectNoteFile.ts`). Feature backing: `/teacher/notes` (teacher/PC add + manage) and the student `/student/overview` subject view (read-only). See `README.md` §2.12.

## Enum Types

**`user_role`**: `teacher`, `student`, `performance_coach`, `college_counselor` (added 2026-07-31), `admin`, `parent`. `parent` was removed 2026-07-07 (the enum was dropped and recreated without it) and **re-added 2026-09-10** via a plain `ALTER TYPE … ADD VALUE` — see the changelog entry at the top. It means something different this time: a household account owning several students via `students.parent_id`, not a per-student duplicate of the student dashboard.
**`engagement_rating`**: `low`, `medium`, `high`

## Check Constraints (all, verified)

| Table | Constraint |
|---|---|
| `session_logs` | `session_duration_hrs >= 0` and rounds cleanly to 0.25 (15-min increments) |
| `session_logs` | `no_show_type` ∈ `{no_show_1, no_show_2, no_show_plus}` |
| `subject_categories` | `type` ∈ `{academic, beyond_academic}` |
| `students` | `status` ∈ `{active, paused, completed}` (`students_status_valid`, added 2026-07-29) |
| `student_packages` | `total_hours_purchased >= 0` |
| `student_packages` | `status` ∈ `{active, closed}` |
| `package_topups` | `hours_added > 0` |
| `invoices` | `status` ∈ `{draft, sent, locked}` |
| `monthly_reports` | `month` between 1 and 12 |
| `monthly_reports` | `status` ∈ `{draft, locked}` |
| `zoom_invoices` | `status` ∈ `{pending, acknowledged}` |
| `enrollment_requests` | `enrollment_type` ∈ `{new_student, renewal}` |
| `enrollment_requests` | `status` ∈ `{pending_payment, payment_submitted, confirmed, rejected}` |
| `enrollment_requests` | `enrollment_type = 'new_student' OR student_id IS NOT NULL` (renewals must name an existing student) |
| `enrollment_request_packages` | `hours > 0` (moved off `enrollment_requests` 2026-07-25, see that table's entry above) |
| `no_show_settings` | `id = 1` (single-row table) |
| `no_show_settings` | `payout_amount >= 0` |
| `session_duration_settings` | `id = 1` (single-row table) |
| `session_duration_settings` | `default_duration_hrs > 0` and rounds cleanly to 0.25 (15-min increments) |
| `enrollment_requests` | `enrollment_type = 'renewal' OR pc_teacher_id IS NOT NULL` (new students must have a coach assigned up front) |
| `bundle_pool_settings` | `bundle_name` ∈ `{Foundation Program, All-In-One}` |
| `bundle_pool_settings` | `hours > 0` |
| `bundle_pool_settings` | unique on `(bundle_name, COALESCE(pool_label, ''))` |
| `package_renewal_requests` | `status` ∈ `{pending, acknowledged, renewed}` |
| `package_renewal_requests` | `requested_hours IS NULL OR requested_hours > 0` |

## Unique Constraints / Indexes (all, verified)

- `users.username`, `teachers.email`, `program_types.name`, `course_types.name`, `curricula.name`, `admins.user_id`
- `teacher_subjects`: `(teacher_id, subject_id, curriculum_id)` where curriculum set; `(teacher_id, subject_id)` where curriculum null
- `subjects`: `(name, category_id)` where `curriculum_group_id IS NULL`
- `pc_student_assignments`: `student_id` where `unassigned_at IS NULL` (one active coach per student)
- `cc_student_assignments`: `student_id` where `unassigned_at IS NULL` (one active counsellor per student)
- `students`: `user_id` where `user_id IS NOT NULL`
- `student_packages`: `(student_id, course_type_id)` where `NOT is_locked` (partial index, replaces the old plain unique constraint as of 2026-07-02 — see package-locking notes above)
- `invoice_packages`: `(invoice_id, student_package_id)`
- `monthly_reports`: `(year, month)`
- `zoom_invoices`: `(teacher_id, period_month)`
- `enrollment_requests`: `payment_link_token`

Foreign-key columns are indexed throughout (`teacher_id`, `student_id`, `subject_id`, `curriculum_id`, `report_id`, `invoice_id`, etc. — see `pg_indexes` for the full list); a small number of low-traffic FK columns are unindexed per the performance advisor (not reproduced here — run `get_advisors(type: "performance")` for the live list).

## Functions (verified — actual live source)

### `is_admin()` → boolean, SECURITY DEFINER
```sql
select exists (select 1 from public.users where id = auth.uid() and role = 'admin');
```

### `is_coach_or_counselor()` → boolean, SECURITY DEFINER, `SET search_path = 'public'` (added 2026-08-03)

`is_performance_coach() or is_college_counselor()`. The role test behind every widened policy — a College Counsellor now carries the same access a Performance Coach does. The two halves read different sources on purpose (PC off `users.role`, CC off the `teachers` flag), which is what lets someone who is both match either way.

### `is_my_assigned_student(p_student_id text)` → boolean, SECURITY DEFINER, `SET search_path = 'public'` (added 2026-08-03)

True when the caller holds a **live** (`unassigned_at is null`) `pc_student_assignments` **or** `cc_student_assignments` row for that student. Replaces the inlined `pc_student_assignments` join that every roster-scoped PC policy used to carry, so those policies now reach a counsellor's roster too. Also used by `set_student_status()`.

### `is_performance_coach()` → boolean, SECURITY DEFINER
```sql
select exists (select 1 from public.users where id = auth.uid() and role = 'performance_coach')
```

### `is_college_counselor()` → boolean, SECURITY DEFINER, `SET search_path = 'public'` (added 2026-07-31)
```sql
select exists (
  select 1 from public.teachers
  where user_id = auth.uid() and is_college_counselor and is_active
);
```
Note this reads the **`teachers` flag**, not `users.role`, which is the one place it deliberately departs from `is_performance_coach()`. `users.role` holds a single login role, so a teacher who is both a PC and a CC could only ever carry one of them there; keying off the flag means the CC grants still apply to that person.

### `can_view_student(p_student_id text)` → boolean, SECURITY DEFINER, `SET search_path = 'public'` (parent branch removed 2026-07-07, restored 2026-09-10)
```sql
select exists (
  select 1 from public.students s
  where s.id = p_student_id
    and (
      s.user_id = auth.uid()
      or (
        s.parent_id is not null
        and exists (
          select 1 from public.parents p
          where p.id = s.parent_id and p.user_id = auth.uid()
        )
      )
    )
);
```
**READ gate only.** A policy granting writes must use `is_own_student_record()` instead — see that function below and the 2026-09-10 changelog entry; the function carries a `COMMENT` saying so.
Backs the read-only `student_parent_read_*` SELECT policies (`student_packages`, `package_topups`, `session_logs`, `invoices`, `invoice_line_items`, `invoice_packages`, `pc_student_assignments`, `students` itself). Until 2026-07-07 this also had an `OR EXISTS (... join parents ...)` branch so a linked parent could see the same rows — dropped along with the `parents` table and the parent role, since a student now only ever sees their own record.

### `is_own_student_record(p_student_id text)` → boolean, SECURITY DEFINER, `SET search_path = 'public'` (added 2026-09-10)
```sql
select exists (
  select 1 from public.students s
  where s.id = p_student_id and s.user_id = auth.uid()
);
```
Byte-for-byte what `can_view_student()` was before 2026-09-10 widened it to cover parents. It exists so the handful of policies granting a **student** write access keep meaning the student themselves: today that is `submissions`' `"Students manage their own submissions"` (FOR ALL), which would otherwise have let a parent submit their child's homework. Use this, never `can_view_student()`, in any new policy that grants writes.

### `my_parent_id()` → text, SECURITY DEFINER, `SET search_path = 'public'` (added 2026-09-10)
```sql
select id from public.parents where user_id = auth.uid() limit 1;
```

### `is_my_linked_child(p_parent_id text)` → boolean, SECURITY DEFINER, `SET search_path = 'public'` (added 2026-07-07, dropped the same day, restored 2026-09-10)
```sql
select exists (
  select 1 from public.parents p
  where p.id = p_parent_id and p.user_id = auth.uid()
);
```
Lets a policy test a `parent_id` column without subquerying `parents` inline — the SECURITY DEFINER indirection is what breaks the `students` ↔ `parents` RLS recursion originally fixed by `20260707000000`.

### `my_teacher_id()` → bigint, SECURITY DEFINER
```sql
select id from public.teachers where user_id = auth.uid() limit 1;
```

### `student_has_active_pc(p_student_id text)` → boolean, SECURITY DEFINER, `SET search_path = 'public'`
```sql
select exists (
  select 1 from public.pc_student_assignments
  where student_id = p_student_id and unassigned_at is null
);
```
Added 2026-07-06. Was used in the `pc_assign_unassigned_student_to_self` INSERT WITH CHECK to answer "does this student already have an active coach?" **from within a policy on `pc_student_assignments` itself** — being SECURITY DEFINER (and the table not being FORCE RLS) means it reads the table without re-applying that policy, which avoided the `infinite recursion detected in policy` error the previous inline subquery caused. As of 2026-07-18 that INSERT policy was dropped (PCs can no longer self-assign — see `pc_student_assignments`), so this helper is currently **unused** by any policy; it's kept in place (harmless, `EXECUTE` still granted to `authenticated`) in case coach self-assignment is ever reintroduced.

### `set_student_status(p_student_id text, p_status text)` → `students`, SECURITY DEFINER, `SET search_path = 'public'` (added 2026-07-29)
Moves a student between the three engagement categories (`active` / `paused` / `completed`) and, when completing, **closes their active `pc_student_assignments` row in the same call** — and, as of 2026-07-31, their active `cc_student_assignments` row too (set to `completed` with `unassigned_at`), since a student who is done is done with their counsellor as well. Raises `check_violation` on any other status value, and `insufficient_privilege` unless the caller is an admin **or** a performance coach with a currently-active assignment for that student.

It exists as a function rather than an RLS policy for two reasons, both structural: (1) RLS is row-level, so there is no way to write a policy that lets a coach update `students.status` and nothing else; (2) completing must also write `pc_student_assignments`, where a coach has **no** write policy at all (assign/unassign stayed admin-only — see `20260706000000` / `20260801000300`), and doing both writes here keeps that rule intact and the pair atomic. A coach therefore cannot re-open a student they completed — completing unassigned them — and an admin re-assigns from `/admin/pc-assignments` (the UI flips such a student back to `active` on re-assignment). `EXECUTE` granted to `authenticated`. Verified live by impersonation: an anonymous caller and a coach-without-assignment were both rejected, an admin and the student's own coach both succeeded, and completing closed the assignment row.

### `set_cc_assignment_status(p_assignment_id bigint, p_status text)` → `cc_student_assignments`, SECURITY DEFINER, `SET search_path = 'public'` (added 2026-07-31)
Moves a CC engagement between `active` and `completed`. Completing sets `status` **and** `unassigned_at` in the same call; reopening clears `unassigned_at` and first checks the student hasn't since been given another counsellor, raising `unique_violation` with a readable message rather than letting `uq_active_cc_per_student` surface a raw constraint error. Raises `check_violation` on any other status value, `no_data_found` for an unknown id, and `insufficient_privilege` unless the caller is an admin **or** the counsellor named on the row.

Same reasoning as `set_student_status()` for being a function rather than a policy: the status and `unassigned_at` writes must not be separable, or a counsellor could mark themselves complete while staying assigned (or the reverse). It is also why `cc_student_assignments` needs no counsellor `UPDATE` policy at all.

### `prevent_non_admin_student_relink()` → trigger, SECURITY DEFINER, `SET search_path = 'public'` (added 2026-07-06; `parent_id` clause dropped 2026-07-07)
```sql
begin
  if not public.is_admin() then
    if new.user_id is distinct from old.user_id then
      raise exception 'Only an admin can change which login account a student is linked to.';
    end if;
  end if;
  return new;
end;
```
Fires `BEFORE UPDATE ON students`. Blocks a non-admin (`NOT is_admin()`) from changing `students.user_id` — needed because the `students_update_own` self-update policy otherwise lets a student re-point their own row's login link at an arbitrary account. Same pattern as `prevent_self_privilege_escalation()` below. As of 2026-07-07 this no longer also guards `parent_id` (that column, and the sibling `is_my_linked_child()` helper / `prevent_non_admin_parent_relink()` trigger it used to pair with on the now-dropped `parents` table, were removed along with the parent role).

### `get_email_for_username(p_username text)` → text, SECURITY DEFINER
```sql
select email from public.users where username = p_username limit 1;
```

### `create_user_profile()` → trigger, SECURITY DEFINER, `SET search_path = 'public'`
Fires `AFTER INSERT ON auth.users`. Inserts the matching `public.users` row, defaulting `role` to `'student'` if none is passed in `raw_user_meta_data`.

### `create_teacher_profile(p_user_id, p_country, p_subjects[])` → bigint, SECURITY DEFINER
Inserts a `teachers` row from an existing `users` row, then seeds `teacher_subjects` for each subject in the array (on-conflict-do-nothing).

### `create_admin_profile(p_user_id)` → bigint, SECURITY DEFINER
Inserts an `admins` row.

### `sync_teacher_staff_role()` → trigger, SECURITY DEFINER, `SET search_path = 'public'` (was `sync_teacher_pc_role()`; replaced 2026-07-31)
Fires `AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE OF is_performance_coach ON teachers`. Keeps the linked `users.role` in sync: `performance_coach` if the flag is true, `teacher` otherwise.

### `prevent_self_privilege_escalation()` → trigger, SECURITY DEFINER
Fires `BEFORE UPDATE ON users`. Blocks a non-admin from changing their own `role` or `is_active`. Returns early (allows the change) when `auth.uid()` is NULL — a trusted backend/service-role context — since `users` RLS already restricts UPDATE to admins or a user editing their own row, so a NULL `auth.uid()` can never be a non-admin self-edit. This is what lets the `deactivate-teacher-with-user` edge function (service role) sync `users.is_active = false` when a teacher is deactivated.

### `prevent_session_log_change_in_locked_month()` → trigger
Fires `BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OR DELETE ON session_logs`. Blocks any insert/update/delete whose `session_date` (old or new) falls in a month with a `locked` `monthly_reports` row.

### `prevent_locked_monthly_report_update()` / `prevent_locked_monthly_report_delete()` → trigger
Block editing/deleting a `monthly_reports` row once `status = 'locked'`.

### `prevent_locked_report_stat_change()` → trigger
Blocks update/delete on any of the five `monthly_report_*_stats` tables if the parent report is locked.

### `prevent_locked_invoice_delete()` → trigger
Blocks deleting an `invoices` row once `status = 'locked'`.

### `on_package_topup_inserted()` → trigger
Fires `AFTER INSERT ON package_topups`. Adds `hours_added` onto the parent `student_packages.total_hours_purchased`.

### `prevent_topup_on_locked_package()` → trigger (added 2026-07-02)
Fires `BEFORE INSERT ON package_topups`. Blocks the insert if the target `student_packages.is_locked` is true.

### `handle_session_log_package_lock()` → trigger (added 2026-07-02; rewritten 2026-07-02 for multi-pool packages; rewritten again 2026-07-02 for subject-name direct pool match; reordered 2026-07-03; guarded against re-resolving already-deducted sessions 2026-07-03 later; stopped borrowing across course_types 2026-07-05)
Fires `BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OR DELETE ON session_logs`. Blocks the operation if the *old* linked package (on update/delete) is locked — this is what makes package locking actually stick, not just the topup guard.

**As of `20260703010000_never_reresolve_already_deducted_sessions.sql`:** before any of the resolution logic below runs, an `UPDATE` where `OLD.student_package_id IS NOT NULL` (the session was already resolved/deducted) **and** `NEW.student_id`/`NEW.course_type_id` are unchanged from `OLD` skips resolution entirely — `NEW.student_package_id` passes through exactly as the row arrives, whether that's unchanged (an unrelated edit like fixing the hours or date) or an explicit new value (the application's own single-session reassignment). This is what makes "change the (teacher, subject) → pool assignment for upcoming sessions" and "move one already-logged session" genuinely non-interfering: neither can ever cause a *different*, already-deducted session to silently jump pools just because it happened to get touched afterward for an unrelated reason. Only a still-unresolved session (`OLD.student_package_id IS NULL` — a brand-new `INSERT`, or one still sitting in `pool_ambiguous`) reaches the logic below, which counts the student's unlocked pools for `NEW.course_type_id`:
- **Exactly 1** → resolves `NEW.student_package_id` to it directly (the original, still-common behavior for every single-pool student).
- **0** → **as of `20260705120000_never_borrow_across_course_types.sql`, always creates a fresh zero-hour pool of `NEW.course_type_id` itself** — the session's own course type, never a different one. (Previously this borrowed from another of the student's active pools regardless of course_type, oldest first — a real bug: an Academic session for a student with only a Beyond Academic All-In-One bundle got silently deducted from that bundle's "Primary Project" pool. Fixed after being caught live on student S1/`divija pandey`, whose miscategorized session was manually repointed to a newly created zero-hour Academic pool.) Sets `pool_fallback_used = true`, never blocks the save.
- **More than 1** (Foundation Program / All-In-One students) → checks `session_log_pool_resolutions` by `(student_id, course_type_id, teacher_id, COALESCE(subject_id, -1))` *first* — an explicit, previously-recorded decision always wins. Only if no resolution exists yet does it fall back to the direct subject-name match (`NEW.subject_id`'s subject name exactly equals one of the candidate pools' `pool_label`, e.g. subject "Career Exploration" vs. an All-In-One bundle's "Career Exploration" pool). If neither resolves it, sets `student_package_id := NULL` and `pool_ambiguous := true` for a PC to resolve.

On the application side (`src/hooks/useStudentPackages.ts`), what used to be a single `assignSessionToPool()` function is now `resolvePendingSession` (resolves a still-`pool_ambiguous` session, writes the sticky resolution, sweeps other still-pending sessions matching the same key), `updatePoolResolutionTarget` (changes a resolution's target pool going forward — sweeps only currently-pending sessions, never an already-resolved one), and `createPoolResolution` (lets an admin/PC set up a (teacher, subject) → pool assignment proactively, before any session has even been logged against it). A fourth function, `reassignSingleSession` (moved *one* already-resolved session to a different pool), existed briefly but was **removed 2026-07-04** along with its UI — an already-logged session can no longer be moved to a different pool at all, by design. The `OLD.student_package_id IS NOT NULL` skip-resolution guard above still exists at the trigger level (it also protects a plain unrelated edit like fixing an hours typo from ever re-triggering resolution), even though nothing in the app can explicitly reassign a resolved session anymore.

**As of 2026-09-11 / 2026-09-12**, everywhere the three lookups above say "the student's unlocked pools" they mean `id IN (SELECT student_package_ids(NEW.student_id))` — the student's own pools plus the **shared pools they are a named member of**. Membership, not parentage: a sibling left off a shared pool falls into the **0-candidates** branch above and gets a fresh zero-hour pool of their own, exactly as a student with no package at all would, rather than quietly spending hours that were sold for two other children. Verified live both ways (see the 2026-09-12 changelog entry). The zero-hour fallback pool stays **student**-owned for the same reason it always has.

Now `SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path TO 'public'` (added with the 2026-07-02 rewrite) — a plain teacher has no RLS access to `student_packages` or `session_log_pool_resolutions`, but this trigger must read/write both regardless of who is logging the session, matching the pattern already used by `is_admin()`/`is_performance_coach()`/`sync_teacher_pc_role()`.

### `touch_updated_at()` / `touch_invoices_updated_at()` / `touch_student_packages_updated_at()` / `touch_package_allocations_updated_at()` → trigger
Set `updated_at = now()` on update. (Multiple near-identical copies exist rather than one shared function — see "Why this file exists" below.)

### `rls_auto_enable()` → event trigger, SECURITY DEFINER, `SET search_path = 'pg_catalog'`
**Not deliberately built** — fires on every `CREATE TABLE` in `public` and automatically runs `ALTER TABLE ... ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY`. Any new table is fully locked (including to admins) until policies are added for it. Fail-closed by design, but worth knowing.

⚠️ Security note (from advisors): none of the functions above declare `SET search_path`, except `create_user_profile`, `sync_teacher_pc_role`, `rls_auto_enable`, and (as of the 2026-07-02 multi-pool rewrite) `handle_session_log_package_lock`. This is flagged by Supabase's linter (`function_search_path_mutable`) as a best-practice gap, not an active exploit given current usage — worth hardening eventually.

---

|---|---|---|
| `auth.users` | `trg_create_user_profile` | AFTER INSERT → creates `public.users` row. **NOT present on the live database** (verified 2026-07-29 against `pg_trigger`) — `auth.users` is owned by `supabase_auth_admin`, so the baseline's `CREATE TRIGGER` never took effect. Anything that creates an Auth user must insert `public.users` itself; see the 2026-07-29 entry at the top |

| `admins` | `trg_touch_admins` | BEFORE UPDATE → `updated_at` |

| `student_packages` | `trg_touch_student_packages_updated_at` | BEFORE UPDATE → `updated_at` |

| `invoices` | `trg_touch_invoices_updated_at` | BEFORE UPDATE → `updated_at` |
| `invoices` | `trg_prevent_locked_invoice_delete` | BEFORE DELETE → blocks deleting locked invoice |
| `monthly_reports` | `trg_prevent_locked_monthly_report_update` | BEFORE UPDATE → blocks editing locked report |
| `monthly_reports` | `trg_prevent_locked_monthly_report_delete` | BEFORE DELETE → blocks deleting locked report |
| `monthly_report_teacher_stats` / `_student_stats` / `_teacher_subject_stats` / `_student_subject_stats` / `_subject_stats` | `trg_prevent_locked_*_stat_change` | BEFORE UPDATE/DELETE → blocks editing stats of a locked report |

| `session_logs` | `trg_recompute_package_hours_used` | AFTER INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE → recomputes `student_packages.hours_used` for the affected package(s); on an update that moves a session between packages, both old and new are recomputed (added 2026-08-01, `recompute_package_hours_used()`, SECURITY DEFINER `SET search_path = public`) |

All triggers show `tgenabled = O` (enabled, fires in "origin" mode) — none are disabled.
