## Row Level Security — Policies (all, verified — 60+ policies across 23 tables)

RLS is enabled on all 25 tables. Some tables carry **duplicate/overlapping policies** that appear to be leftovers from iterative migrations rather than a clean design (e.g. `session_logs` has two separate INSERT policies and two separate SELECT policies for teachers — one keyed on `my_teacher_id()`, another keyed on an inline `EXISTS (... teachers.user_id = auth.uid())` subquery — functionally redundant but both present and both permissive). Same pattern on `curricula`, `subjects`, `subject_categories`, `teacher_subjects`, `program_types`. This is flagged here rather than silently cleaned up — see "Why this file exists."

**Consistent pattern across all tables:** `is_admin()` grants full `ALL` access; most other access is scoped through `my_teacher_id()` / `is_coach_or_counselor()` / `is_my_assigned_student()` / direct `auth.uid()` matches. (Policies whose *names* still say "Performance coaches" / "PCs" cover counsellors too since 2026-08-03 — the names were kept so references elsewhere in this file stay valid.)

Notable non-obvious policies:
- **`students`**: `INSERT` and `UPDATE` policies for `authenticated` role are both `USING/WITH CHECK (true)` — i.e. **any logged-in user can insert or update any student row**, not scoped to admin/coach. Flagged by the security advisor as overly permissive. Worth revisiting if student data needs tighter scoping.
- **`session_logs`**: as of 2026-08-03 a college counsellor reads and updates session logs exactly as a coach does — the `Performance coaches can …` policies are keyed on `is_coach_or_counselor()`, so the earlier "counsellors see only their own logs" rule no longer holds. Performance coaches can `SELECT` sessions for their assigned students (`PCs can read sessions for assigned students`) *and* there's a separate blanket `Performance coaches can read all session_logs` policy (`is_performance_coach()` with no scoping) — the blanket one makes the assignment-scoped one redundant.
- **`invoices` / `invoice_line_items` / `invoice_packages` / `student_packages` / `package_topups`**: performance coaches get `ALL` access scoped to students currently assigned to them via `pc_student_assignments` (only rows where `unassigned_at IS NULL`).
- **`monthly_reports` and its stats tables**: teachers/coaches can only `SELECT` once `status = 'locked'` — draft reports are admin-only, invisible to everyone else until finalized.
- **`zoom_invoices`**: teachers can insert their own, and only replace (`UPDATE`) their own **pending** ones — once `acknowledged`, immutable to the teacher. **Performance coaches are excluded** — both the INSERT and the replace-`UPDATE` `WITH CHECK` add `AND NOT EXISTS (select 1 from teachers t where t.id = teacher_id and t.is_performance_coach)`, so a coach (who also has a `teachers` row, hence a non-null `my_teacher_id()`) cannot create or replace a Zoom invoice at the DB layer, not just in the UI. Zoom invoices are a teacher-only obligation.
- No `anon`-facing policy grants direct table reads for the login flow — `get_email_for_username()` is `SECURITY DEFINER` and bypasses RLS by design for that one narrow purpose.
- **`enrollment_requests`**: admin-only (`is_admin()` grants `ALL`). The public `/pay/:token` page's proof upload never touches this table directly — it goes through the `submit-payment-proof` edge function using the service-role key, which bypasses RLS by design (there's no Supabase Auth session to authorize with at that point).
- **`enrollment_request_packages` / `enrollment_request_payment_proofs`** (added 2026-07-25): same shape as `enrollment_requests` — admin-only (`is_admin()` grants `ALL`), the public payment page reaches both exclusively through edge functions using the service-role key.
- **`package_renewal_requests`** (added 2026-07-09): admin gets `ALL`. A PC can only `INSERT` as themselves (`requested_by_teacher_id = my_teacher_id()`) for a student currently assigned to them (`pc_student_assignments`, `unassigned_at IS NULL`), and can only `SELECT` their own submitted requests — not other coaches'. No PC `UPDATE`/`DELETE` policy exists at all — acknowledging and marking `renewed` are both admin/system-only actions.

---

## Grants (verified via `information_schema.role_table_grants`)

- **`authenticated`**: full `SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE` on nearly every table, including `enrollment_requests` (added 2026-07-02). Exceptions: `students` (no DELETE), `users` (no DELETE). `zoom_invoices` was missing this grant entirely as of the 2026-07-01 snapshot above (RLS policies existed but couldn't fire) — found during this audit and fixed the same day via a follow-up grant (`supabase/migrations/20260701000001_fix_zoom_invoices_authenticated_grant.sql`).
- **Sequence `USAGE` is a separate grant from table `INSERT`.** `zoom_invoices.id` uses a plain `nextval('zoom_invoices_id_seq')` default rather than an `IDENTITY` column (see its column table above), and the table-level grant fix above did not cover the sequence itself — `authenticated` had zero `USAGE` on `zoom_invoices_id_seq`, so every upload failed with `permission denied for sequence zoom_invoices_id_seq` until a live teacher hit it and it was fixed via `supabase/migrations/20260701000005_fix_zoom_invoices_id_seq_usage_grant.sql`. `students.id` has the same plain-sequence shape (`students_num_seq`) but was already correctly granted **for `authenticated`**, so it was unaffected at the time — see the `service_role` correction directly below, where the exact same gotcha resurfaced for a different role.
- **`anon`**: `SELECT` only on `users` (safety net for pre-login lookup; the real lookup goes through the SECURITY DEFINER function).
- **`bundle_pool_settings` (2026-07-06)**: the exact same gotcha resurfaced a third time. The initial migration granted `authenticated` `SELECT/UPDATE` but nothing to `service_role`, even though `review-enrollment-payment` reads this table via its service-role client to resolve bundle pool hours — would have failed live with `permission denied for table bundle_pool_settings` on the very first bundle purchase confirmation. Caught before it shipped and fixed via a same-day follow-up migration (`20260706020000_bundle_pool_settings_service_role_grant.sql`, `GRANT SELECT ... TO service_role`). Verified live via `information_schema.role_table_grants`.
- **`package_renewal_requests` (2026-07-09)**: granted `SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE` to both `authenticated` and `service_role` in the same migration that created the table — `review-enrollment-payment` needed `service_role` access to it from day one (it queries/updates this table to auto-resolve renewal requests on every confirm), so this dodges the "first edge function to touch a new table" gotcha documented in the `service_role` note directly below by granting both roles up front rather than discovering the gap live. No `DELETE` grant on either role — nothing in the UI removes a request.
- **`enrollment_request_packages` / `enrollment_request_payment_proofs` (2026-07-25)**: same pattern — `SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE` granted to both `authenticated` and `service_role` in the same migration that created the tables, since the enrollment-workflow edge functions needed `service_role` access to both from day one (again dodging the gotcha below rather than discovering it live).
- **`service_role`**: ~~full grants only on `admins`, `program_types`, `session_logs`, `teacher_subjects`, `teachers`, `users` — the rest show only `REFERENCES/TRIGGER/TRUNCATE` in the grants table (service_role typically bypasses RLS/grants entirely at the Postgres role level in Supabase, so this asymmetry is expected and not a bug)~~ — **this assumption was wrong, corrected 2026-07-02.** `service_role` does **not** bypass base table grants in this project; it only ever happened to work because the three pre-existing edge functions (`create-teacher-with-user`, `notify-no-show`, `notify-flag`) exclusively touched the six tables above, which is where those functions' own operations (teacher/session/user writes) landed. The first edge functions to touch other tables — `send-enrollment-invoice`, `submit-payment-proof`, `review-enrollment-payment`, needing `enrollment_requests`, `course_types`, `students`, `student_packages`, `package_topups`, `pc_student_assignments` — failed live with `permission denied for table ...` regardless of RLS, proving the old assumption false. Fixed via `supabase/migrations/20260702080000_fix_service_role_grants_for_enrollment_workflow.sql`, which also had to add `GRANT USAGE ON SEQUENCE students_num_seq TO service_role` (the same plain-sequence gotcha as `zoom_invoices` above, this time hitting `service_role` instead of `authenticated`). **Lesson: when a new edge function touches a table for the first time, explicitly grant `service_role` on it — never assume the role bypasses grants just because it bypasses RLS.**

---

## Storage Buckets (verified)

| Bucket | Public | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `avatars` | Yes | User avatar images |
| `session-summaries` | Yes | Fathom session summary documents |
| `session-invoices` | Yes | Session/teacher invoice files |
| `payment-proofs` | **No** (added 2026-07-02) | Payment screenshots uploaded via `/pay/:token`. Kept private, unlike the other three — these are financial screenshots uploaded with no Supabase Auth session, so admins view them via `createSignedUrl` (requires the `is_admin()` SELECT policy below) rather than a public URL. |
| `report-cards` | **No** (added 2026-07-07) | Optional report-card upload, captured on the enrollment/renewal form or by the student themselves (first-login gate / ongoing Profile page) and stored on `students.report_card_url`. Private — admins manage it (`is_admin()`, `ALL`), any staff member (a row in `teachers`) can read it via `createSignedUrl` (same pattern as `payment-proofs`), and as of 2026-07-07 (later) a student can `INSERT` into their own folder (path prefixed with their own `students.id`) via **"Students can upload their own report card"** — insert-only, no read/update/delete for the student. |
| `homework-content` | **No** (added 2026-07-08, Phase 3) | Teacher-uploaded Homework Generator source material (PDF/PPT/PPTX/DOCX); the object path is stored on `content_uploads.file_url`. Private, mirroring `report-cards`: admins manage it (`is_admin()`, `ALL`), any staff member (a `teachers` row) can read it via `createSignedUrl` (**"Staff read homework content"**) and `INSERT` under a folder prefixed with a real `students.id` (**"Staff upload homework content"**, insert-only). Applied via `supabase/migrations/20260717000000_homework_content_bucket.sql`. |
| `homework-answers` | **No** (added 2026-07-14) | A **student's own** uploaded homework answer photos/PDFs (manual grading — see `submissions.whole_paper_answer` / `answers_json` photo answers above). Private, mirroring `report-cards`' student-self-upload shape rather than `homework-content`'s staff-upload shape: admins manage it (`is_admin()`, `ALL`), a student can `INSERT`/`SELECT` only under their own folder (path prefixed with their own `students.id`, via **"Students can upload their own homework answers"** / **"Students can read their own homework answers"**, mirroring `report-cards`' policies), and any staff member (a `teachers` row) can `SELECT` (**"Staff read homework answers"**, mirroring `homework-content`'s staff-read) so the paper's owning teacher/coach/admin can open a submitted answer to grade it. Applied via `supabase/migrations/20260728010000_homework_manual_answer_grading.sql`. |
| `pc-profiles` | **Yes** (added 2026-07-18) | A performance coach's profile **photo** (`pc_profiles.photo_url` stores the full public URL, like `session-invoices`). Public read (**"Public read pc profile photos"**, so an assigned student renders it by URL); any staff member (a `teachers` row) can `INSERT`/`UPDATE`/`DELETE` under a folder prefixed with their own `teacher_id` (**"Staff upload/update/delete pc profile photos"**); admins manage everything (`is_admin()`, `ALL`). Applied via `supabase/migrations/20260802000000_pc_profiles.sql`. |
| `subject-notes` | **No** (added 2026-07-17) | The optional file attachment on a **Subject Note** (`subject_notes.file_url`, any format — see §2.12). Private, a hybrid of the two shapes above: admins manage it (`is_admin()`, `ALL`); any staff member (a `teachers` row) can `INSERT` under a folder prefixed with a real `students.id` (**"Staff upload subject notes files"**, like `homework-content`), `SELECT` (**"Staff read subject notes files"**) and `DELETE` (**"Staff delete subject notes files"**, so a teacher can remove a note's file); and a student can `SELECT` only files under their own `students.id` folder (**"Students read their own subject notes files"**, like `homework-answers`). Applied via `supabase/migrations/20260801000000_subject_notes.sql`. |

Security advisor flags the three *public* buckets as allowing public **listing** (not just object-URL reads) via their SELECT policies — low risk for this use case, but worth tightening (`public_bucket_allows_listing` lint) if file names shouldn't be enumerable. `payment-proofs`/`report-cards`/`homework-answers` are not public, so this doesn't apply to them.

## Edge Functions (verified, all `ACTIVE`)

| Function | JWT required | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `create-teacher-with-user` | Yes | Admin-only: creates an `auth.users` account + `users` row (via trigger) + `teachers` row + `teacher_subjects` rows in one call — the Admin API operation the frontend can't do directly |
| `create-admin-with-user` | Yes | Admin-only: creates an `auth.users` account + `users` row (role `admin`, via the `create_user_profile` trigger reading `user_metadata.role`) + `admins` marker row in one call, then emails the new admin their login credentials in the background. Mirrors `create-teacher-with-user`; backs the `/admin/admins` tab (added 2026-07-17). `admins` already has full `service_role` grants (one of the six original tables), so no grant migration was needed |
| `notify-no-show` | No | Sends a notification when a session is logged as a no-show |
| `notify-flag` | Yes | Sends a notification when a session is flagged for coach attention |
| `send-enrollment-invoice` | Yes | Admin-only: emails the invoice summary + `/pay/:token` payment link for an `enrollment_requests` row (added 2026-07-02) |
| `submit-payment-proof` | **No** | Public, token-gated: GET returns the invoice summary for the payment page, POST (multipart) uploads the screenshot to `payment-proofs` and flips status to `payment_submitted` (added 2026-07-02) |
| `review-enrollment-payment` | Yes | Admin-only: confirms (creates student/login/package/topup or tops up/starts a package for a renewal) or rejects a submitted payment (added 2026-07-02). Redeployed 2026-07-09 (v22): on every confirm, also auto-resolves any matching `pending`/`acknowledged` `package_renewal_requests` row for that `(student_id, course_type_id)` to `renewed` and emails the requesting PC — see that table's entry above. |
| `notify-renewal-request` | No | Fire-and-forget, added 2026-07-09, called right after a PC inserts a `package_renewal_requests` row: emails every active admin (`users` where `role = 'admin' and is_active`) with a link to `/admin/renewal-requests`. Mirrors `notify-pool-issue`'s shape/secrets. |
| `notify-package-threshold` | No | Fire-and-forget, called from both session-log forms after a non-no-show session is created. Two distinct alerts (added 2026-07-05, see changelog below): an **over-limit** alert (hours used exceeds hours purchased — includes a 0-hour placeholder package) emails the PC **every** offending session, never deduped; a **50%/75%/100% milestone** alert (only for a package with real purchased hours that isn't already over limit) fires once per threshold via `student_packages.notified_50_pct_at/75/100` (reset on topup) (added 2026-07-02) |
| `notify-pool-issue` | No | Fire-and-forget, called from both session-log forms when the new session has `pool_ambiguous` or `pool_fallback_used` set: emails the student's assigned PC to either choose a pool (linking to the student's "Learner's actual hours" tab) or just informs them a fallback pool was used. De-dupes so it doesn't re-email once per session while the same ambiguous (student, course_type, teacher, subject) combo is still pending. Reuses the same project-wide `PUBLIC_APP_URL`/`SMTP_*` secrets `notify-package-threshold`/`send-enrollment-invoice` already depend on — no separate secret configuration needed. |
| `generate-homework-paper` | Yes | Homework Generator Phases 4-5 (added 2026-07-08): runs as the calling teacher (not `service_role`), resolves a draft `generated_papers` row's source content, calls the Gemini API per composition block, validates/retries, dedups against `question_bank`, and writes `questions_json`. Accepts a scope in the body — whole paper (default), `regenerate_block: <index>`, or `regenerate_question: <id>` (Phase 5). As of 2026-07-08 (PDF indexing) also honors `generated_papers.content_scope` — when set, Gemini is told to draw only from the chosen chapter/section's page range. Refuses any non-`draft` paper. Requires the `GEMINI_API_KEY` secret. See `db/docs/HOMEWORK_GENERATOR_ARCHITECTURE.md` Phases 4-5 + "PDF chapter/section indexing". |
| `index-content-upload` | Yes | Homework Generator PDF indexing (added 2026-07-08): runs as the calling teacher, downloads an uploaded PDF from `homework-content`, asks Gemini for its chapter→section outline (page ranges) and writes it to `content_uploads.outline` (+ `outline_status`). PDF-only (non-PDF → `outline_status = 'failed'`). Requires the `GEMINI_API_KEY` secret. See `db/docs/HOMEWORK_GENERATOR_ARCHITECTURE.md` "PDF chapter/section indexing". |
| `grade-homework-submission` | Yes | Homework Generator Phase 7 (added 2026-07-08): **runs as `service_role`** (verifies + authorizes the caller's JWT first — admin/owning-student/owning-teacher/PC). Auto-grades MCQ/true_false/fill-blank, Gemini-grades mark-scheme types per point, upserts the `grades` row, and sets `generated_papers.status = 'graded'`. Invoked by the student on submit and by the teacher's Grade/Re-grade buttons. Degrades to "pending teacher review" for subjective questions if `GEMINI_API_KEY` is unset (`ai_incomplete: true`). See `db/docs/HOMEWORK_GENERATOR_ARCHITECTURE.md` Phase 7. |
| `parse-homework-paper` | Yes | Homework Generator "parse an existing paper" (schema/function found live 2026-07-14, backfilled into the repo — see `HOMEWORK_GENERATOR_ARCHITECTURE.md`'s "Backfilled repo tracking" note): runs as the calling teacher, transcribes a `content_source_type = 'parsed'` paper's uploaded PDF/photo into `questions_json` verbatim (no new questions invented) and builds the answer key itself, since the source paper usually has none. Requires `GEMINI_API_KEY`. **No frontend calls this yet** — `ContentSourceType` has no `'parsed'` value in `src/types/database.ts` and nothing creates a `'parsed'` draft, so this function is currently unreachable from the app. |

## Seed / Lookup Data (verified, exact live rows)

### `program_types` (10 rows, hierarchical via `parent_id` — updated 2026-07-09)
| id | name | is_active | parent_id | type |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Academics | true | — | academic |
| 2 | Beyond Academics | true | — | beyond_academic |
| 4 | Demo Lesson | true | — | demo_lesson |
| 27 | Ascend Offline Work | true | — | ascend_offline_work |
| 30 | Student Offline Work | true | — | student_offline_work |
| 28 | Academic Offline Work | true | 30 | academic_offline_work |
| 29 | Beyond Academic Offline Work | true | 30 | beyond_academic_offline_work |
| 31 | College Counselling | true | — | college_counselling |
| 32 | College Counselling | **false** | 31 | college_counselling_general |
| 33 | College Essays | **false** | 31 | college_essays |

`ECA/School collaboration` (id 3), `Others` (id 10), and `Work for Ascend Now` (id 26) were removed as unused (2026-07-01, verified zero references in `session_logs`/`student_packages` first). `Zoom Invoice` (id 7, inactive) was deleted 2026-07-02 as part of the inactive-cleanup pass (see the note near the top of this file) — confirmed zero references first. Offline Work went through two iterations: first a flat set of children under a single "Offline Work" wrapper (`supabase/migrations/20260701000002_...sql`), then flattened again — `Ascend Offline Work` promoted to its own standalone top-level type (no sub-program step needed to log it) and `Academic`/`Beyond Academic Offline Work` moved under a new top-level "Student Offline Work" group, with the now-empty "Offline Work" wrapper (old id 5) deleted — see `supabase/migrations/20260701000003_flatten_offline_work_hierarchy.sql`.

**College Counselling** (id 31) was added 2026-07-02 as a new top-level type with two sub-programs — `College Essays` and one initially named `General Counselling` (id 32) since `program_types.name` was globally unique at the time and collided with the parent's name. That constraint was relaxed the same day to a per-parent unique index (see the `program_types` column notes above), and id 32 was then renamed to match its parent exactly: **`College Counselling`**.

**As of 2026-07-09, this sub-program shape was retired.** College Counselling now works exactly like Beyond Academics: a single standalone top-level `program_types` row (id 31, unchanged) with **no active children** — ids 32/33 were deactivated (`is_active = false`, not deleted; verified zero `session_logs` referenced them at the time) rather than kept as a second, program-type-based way to model "College Counselling" vs. "College Essays". Those two now live as plain `subjects` rows instead (`category = 'college_counselling'`, see the `subjects` section below), picked from a new "College Counselling" tab in the Subjects Library — the same flat, no-grouping picker Beyond Academic subjects already use. See `supabase/migrations/20260709000000_college_counselling_subjects_library.sql` and `db/docs/SUBJECT_HIERARCHY.md`. `course_types` and `bundle_pool_settings` are untouched by this — College Counselling is still exactly one billing course type with one unlabeled 40hr All-In-One pool, same as before. Session log forms resolve `course_type_id` for College Counselling via `PROGRAM_TYPE_TO_COURSE_TYPE_NAME['college_counselling'] = 'College Counselling'` (now explicit; previously relied only on the generic name-equality fallback, which still also matches).

### `course_types` (7 rows)
Academic (green, active), Beyond Academic (orange, active), **College Counselling (sky, active — reactivated 2026-07-02)**, Edge (purple, inactive), General (navy, inactive), **Foundation Program (amber, active — reactivated 2026-07-02 for multi-pool packages; previously an inactive leftover from an abandoned taxonomy)**, **All-In-One (purple, active — added 2026-07-02 for multi-pool packages)**. Foundation Program and All-In-One are used only to group bundles of pools on the packages UI/invoices via `student_packages.package_type_id` — see the "Multi-pool packages" note under `student_packages` above; they are never a session's own `course_type_id`.

**`is_shareable` (added 2026-09-12)** is true on exactly **Academic** and **Beyond Academic** — the only two programmes sold as hours a household shares between two children. College Counselling is false, and so are both bundles (each contains a College Counselling pool). `validate_shared_package_course_type()` refuses any parent-owned package whose course type is false.

### `subject_categories` (7 rows, only 1 active)
Academic (academic, **active** — still used for the academic branch's own admin flows). **Passion Projects (beyond_academic, inactive — deactivated 2026-07-02, re-created as a plain `subjects` row instead)**, **Career Exploration (beyond_academic, inactive — deactivated 2026-07-02 the same day it was added, id 7, re-created as a plain `subjects` row instead)**, College Support (beyond_academic, inactive — deactivated 2026-07-02, superseded by the College Counselling program/course type; its 5 subjects — College Counselling, College Essays, Job Application Mentorship, Profile Building, Writing Development — were themselves inactive and were deleted 2026-07-02 in the inactive-cleanup pass, see note near the top of this file), Computer Science (beyond_academic, **inactive**), Communication skills (beyond_academic, **inactive**), Music (beyond_academic, **inactive**).

**As of 2026-07-02 (later), Beyond Academic subjects (Passion Projects, Career Exploration, Book Publishing, Finance and Literacy, Podcast, …) are ONE completely flat, ungrouped list** — no sections, no `<optgroup>`, `subjects.category_id = NULL` for all of them. This table (`subject_categories`) plays no role in Beyond Academic picking/filtering any more; every picker filters on the loose `subjects.category !== 'academic'` text check instead. See `db/docs/SUBJECT_HIERARCHY.md` §3/§6 for the full picture, the two-iteration history, and the file registry that must all move together if this changes again.

### `curricula` (12 rows)
IBDP, IBMYP, IGCSE, AS & A Levels, AP, **Standard Tests** — active. Common Core, General, Other, STEM, LANG, Human Values — inactive (legacy/unused).

**Standard Tests** (id 17, named "Standard Test" until renamed 2026-07-02) was added 2026-07-02 to replace the four standalone curricula ACT/SAT/TOEFL/IELTS. This went through two shapes the same day: first as `curriculum_groups` under it (mirroring how IBDP/IGCSE/etc. organize subjects), then flattened again — ACT/SAT/TOEFL/IELTS are plain `subjects` directly under curriculum_id 17 (`curriculum_group_id` null), with **no group step at all**, since none of the four were ever going to have subjects of their own underneath them. See `supabase/migrations/20260702030000_standard_test_curriculum_college_counselling.sql`, `20260702040000_rename_college_counselling_and_standard_tests.sql`, and `20260702050000_standard_tests_flatten_to_direct_subjects.sql`.

The free-text `category` label (`beyond_academic` / `passion_projects` / `profile_building`) is legacy and doesn't by itself drive filtering — Beyond Academic pickers use the generic `category !== 'academic'` check. **As of 2026-07-09**, that check also excludes `category === 'college_counselling'` (a new, distinct value introduced specifically so the two branches can be told apart) — College Counselling gets its own flat picker filtered on `category === 'college_counselling'`. ## Homework Generator tables (added 2026-07-08 — Phase 1 schema; Phase 4 wired up real writes)

See `db/docs/HOMEWORK_GENERATOR_ARCHITECTURE.md` for the full feature design and build-phase plan. All seven tables below were added in one migration (plus a same-day perf-fix follow-up). The full write path now exists and is deployed across Phases 4-7 — generation (`generate-homework-paper`) writes `generated_papers.questions_json`/`question_bank`, the student pages write `submissions`, and grading (`grade-homework-submission`) writes `grades` and advances `generated_papers.status`. **Updated 2026-07-14 — re-verified live via `execute_sql`, row counts below are no longer zero**: `GEMINI_API_KEY` is set and the feature has been exercised end-to-end (`content_uploads` 7, `generated_papers` 15, `question_bank` 200, `submissions` 6, `grades` 6, `session_insights` 0 — the last is still genuinely unused, see its own section below).

### `style_templates` (**5 rows live**, verified 2026-08-04 — re-seeded by `20260804000000_reseed_style_templates.sql`; the 67-row history below describes a state this database never actually had)

> **Live truth (2026-08-04).** This table was found **empty** — 0 rows — even though every seeding migration below is recorded as applied in `supabase_migrations.schema_migrations`. `style_templates_id_seq` was at `last_value = 1, is_called = false`, proving no row was ever inserted *here*: this project's early migration history was baselined (marked applied) rather than run. The 2026-08-03 purge is not the cause — it deliberately leaves the catalogue tables alone. Effect: the Homework Generator's "Question type" dropdown was permanently empty, so no paper could be built at all.
> `20260804000000_reseed_style_templates.sql` restored the **5 generic, selectable rows** — `MCQ` (id 1), `FILL_IN_BLANK` (2), `SHORT_ANSWER` (3), `SUBJECTIVE` (4), `TRUE_FALSE` (5) — with the names, `question_type`s and `prompt_fragment`s the original migrations specify and `section_key = code`.
> The **8 board family heads and the 54 subject-/group-level variants described below are NOT in this database** and were deliberately not restored: `TeacherHomeworkPage` filters the picker to `board IS NULL`, so no paper can pick a family head, and `generate-homework-paper`'s resolver only considers variants sharing the picked template's `section_key` — a generic head's `section_key` is its own `code`, which no variant shares. They are unreachable for any paper the current builder can produce. **If board families are ever offered in the builder again, re-run the data statements of `20260716000000` / `20260721000000` / `20260722000000` as a new migration first** — they're all `ON CONFLICT (code)`-idempotent. Read everything below this box as the design of the library, not as the live row set.
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | smallint (identity) | NO | — |
| code | text, unique | NO | — |
| name | text | NO | — |
| board | text | YES | — |
| question_type | text | NO | — |
| prompt_fragment | text | NO | — |
| is_active | boolean | NO | true |
| created_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |
| section_key | text | NO | — |
| curriculum_id | smallint → `curricula.id` (ON DELETE SET NULL) | YES | — |
| curriculum_group_id | smallint → `curriculum_groups.id` (ON DELETE SET NULL) | YES | — |
| subject_id | smallint → `subjects.id` (ON DELETE SET NULL) | YES | — |
| is_selectable | boolean | NO | true |

Reference library of exam-board/question-type prompt fragments. **Seeded 2026-07-08 (Phase 2, `supabase/migrations/20260716000000_seed_style_templates.sql`, idempotent `ON CONFLICT (code)`)** with 12 rows: `IBDP_SECTION_A`, `IBDP_SECTION_B`, `IB_MYP`, `IGCSE_CORE`, `IGCSE_EXTENDED`, `AS_A_LEVEL`, `AP_MCQ`, `AP_FRQ`, `MCQ`, `FILL_IN_BLANK`, `SHORT_ANSWER`, `SUBJECTIVE` — one per active curriculum's exam format plus the generic types. `question_type` is a controlled value the generation/grading phases key off (`mcq`/`true_false`/`fill_blank` auto-grade; `structured`/`extended_response`/`essay`/`short_answer`/`criterion` LLM-graded). **Updated 2026-07-14** (`20260726010000_homework_simplify_generic_templates.sql`): added a 13th generic row, `TRUE_FALSE` (`question_type='true_false'`, reuses `mcq`'s `options`/`correct_option` shape — always exactly 2 options), and renamed the 4 original generic rows' `name` to plainer labels — `MCQ` → "Multiple Choice (MCQ)", `FILL_IN_BLANK` → "Fill in the Blanks", `SHORT_ANSWER` → "Short Answer", `SUBJECTIVE` → "Long Answer" (`code`/`question_type` unchanged, so no existing paper is affected). See `HOMEWORK_GENERATOR_ARCHITECTURE.md`'s "New question type — True/False" note.

**Subject-hierarchy targeting (added `20260721000000_style_templates_subject_hierarchy.sql`):** the 5 columns above let a template pin itself to a point in the real subject hierarchy (curriculum → group → subject; SL/HL are separate `subjects` rows so `subject_id` carries the level). Templates sharing a `section_key` form a family; the `generate-homework-paper` edge function resolves the **most-specific active variant matching a paper's subject/group/curriculum**, falling back to the generic family head. Backfill set `section_key = code` and `curriculum_id` from `board` for the original 12 (so `IBDP_SECTION_A` etc. became the curriculum-level fallback of their own family). 4 **`is_selectable = false`** subject overrides were seeded under family `IBDP_SECTION_A` — `IBDP_SECTION_A__ENGLISH_LANGLIT_SL` / `_HL` (`subject_id` 109/110) and `IBDP_SECTION_A__MATH_AA_SL` / `_HL` (`subject_id` 238/239); `is_selectable=false` keeps them out of the teacher's picker (resolution-only). Four partial unique indexes (`style_templates_family_{subject,group,curriculum,generic}_uidx`) guarantee one row per specificity slot per family so resolution is deterministic; `style_templates_section_key_idx` speeds the family lookup. **`20260722000000_style_templates_group_level_all_curricula.sql` then seeded 50 `is_selectable=false` group-level rows** — one per (curriculum group × the section-families that curriculum uses) across IBDP (7 groups × Section A/B), IBMYP (9 × criterion), IGCSE (5 × Core/Extended), AS & A Levels (5 × structured) and AP (6 groups × MCQ/FRQ; AP Capstone excluded — performance-task assessed, not MCQ/FRQ). This makes every one of the 453 subjects resolve to a subject-area-appropriate template via the `curriculum_group_id` layer (e.g. IBDP French B → `IBDP_SECTION_A__LANGACQ`, Chemistry → `__SCIENCES`, History → `__INDSOC`), with subject-level overrides still winning where present. The 13 `is_selectable=true` rows are otherwise unchanged by the 2026-07-14 update above — still the complete set a caller of `useStyleTemplates()` gets back. **`TeacherHomeworkPage.tsx` additionally filters that down to the 5 with `board IS NULL`** as of 2026-07-14 (see the architecture doc's "Simplified builder" note) — the 8 curriculum-specific rows are still `is_selectable=true`/`is_active=true` in the database and still resolve correctly for any paper that already references them, they're just not offered by that one page's dropdown anymore. See `db/docs/HOMEWORK_GENERATOR_ARCHITECTURE.md` §"Subject-hierarchy templates" for the resolver. RLS: `is_admin()` → `ALL`; any row in `teachers` → `SELECT` where `is_active` (policy rewritten 2026-07-08 same-day to wrap `auth.uid()` in `(select ...)` per the performance advisor).

### `content_uploads` (7 rows, live-verified 2026-07-14)
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | bigint (identity) | NO | — |
| student_id | text → `students.id` | NO | — |
| uploaded_by_teacher_id | bigint → `teachers.id` | NO | — |
| file_name | text | NO | — |
| file_url | text | NO | — |
| file_type | text | NO | — | CHECK: `pdf` / `ppt` / `pptx` / `docx` / `jpg` / `jpeg` / `png` / `webp` / `heic` (image types added 2026-07-26 for the "parse an existing paper" flow) |
| parsed_text | text | YES | — |
| parse_status | text | NO | 'pending' | CHECK: `pending` / `processing` / `completed` / `failed` |
| parse_error | text | YES | — |
| outline | jsonb | YES | — | chapter→section tree of a PDF: `{ chapters: [{ title, page_start, page_end, sections: [{ title, page_start, page_end }] }] }` (added 2026-07-08, `20260718000000_homework_pdf_outline_scope.sql`) |
| outline_status | text | NO | 'pending' | CHECK: `pending` / `processing` / `completed` / `failed` |
| outline_error | text | YES | — |
| created_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |

Teacher-uploaded chapter/slide/notes source material. Files live in the private **`homework-content`** storage bucket (`file_url` holds the object path), written by the Homework Generator UI (Phase 3). As of Phase 4, `parse_status`/`parse_error` are set by `generate-homework-paper`: a PDF flips straight to `completed` (Gemini reads the file directly — see Phase 4 changelog above — so `parsed_text` stays `null` by design, not because extraction is unfinished); PPT/PPTX/DOCX flip to `failed` with a `parse_error` explaining that format isn't supported yet. **`outline`/`outline_status`/`outline_error` (PDF chapter indexing, 2026-07-08)** are a *separate* async job from `parse_status`: the `index-content-upload` edge function downloads the PDF, asks Gemini for its chapter→section outline (with page ranges), and writes it to `outline`. The teacher then scopes generation to one chapter/section via `generated_papers.content_scope`. PPT/PPTX/DOCX rows go straight to `outline_status = 'failed'` (only PDFs are indexable). **As of 2026-07-26, an image type (`jpg`/`jpeg`/`png`/`webp`/`heic`) is also allowed** — used only by the "parse an existing paper" flow (a photo/scan of a past paper), where the row is never indexed (`outline_status = 'failed'`) and `parse_status`/`parse_error` are driven by the `parse-homework-paper` edge function instead of `generate-homework-paper`. RLS: `is_admin()` → `ALL`; `uploaded_by_teacher_id = my_teacher_id()` → `ALL`; `is_performance_coach()` → `SELECT`.

### `generated_papers` (15 rows, live-verified 2026-07-14 — ids 1-16 minus a deleted/never-completed 8)
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | bigint (identity) | NO | — |
| student_id | text → `students.id` | NO | — |
| created_by_teacher_id | bigint → `teachers.id` | NO | — |
| subject_id | smallint → `subjects.id` | YES | — |
| curriculum_id | smallint → `curricula.id` | YES | — |
| content_source_type | text | NO | — | CHECK: `session_log` / `upload` / `both` / `cloned` (added 2026-07-09) / `parsed` (added 2026-07-26), plus the table-level `generated_papers_source_check` that the matching FK below is actually set — `cloned` requires neither (doesn't derive from this student's own source material); `parsed` requires `content_upload_id` (the uploaded paper, same rule as `upload`) |
| session_log_id | bigint → `session_logs.id` | YES | — |
| content_upload_id | bigint → `content_uploads.id` | YES | — |
| content_upload_ids | bigint[] | YES | — added 2026-07-27: parsed papers only — ordered list of every uploaded page image when a paper was photographed across several images; null for single-file parsed papers (use `content_upload_id`, which stays set to the first element) and all non-parsed papers |
| content_scope | jsonb | YES | — | null = whole document; otherwise `{ label, page_start, page_end, parts? }` scoping generation to one or more chapters/sections of the uploaded PDF — `parts` is an array of the selected `{ label, page_start, page_end }` slices (added 2026-07-08, `20260718000000_homework_pdf_outline_scope.sql`; multi-part `parts` added 2026-07-08 with no migration — jsonb shape extended additively) |
| blocks | jsonb | NO | — | requested composition, e.g. `[{"count":4,"style":"IBDP_SECTION_A"},{"count":5,"style":"MCQ"}]` |
| difficulty | text | NO | 'standard' | CHECK: `standard` / `scaffolded` / `stretch` / `eal` |
| questions_json | jsonb | YES | — | generated questions/answers/mark scheme, written by Phase 4's `generate-homework-paper` function; null while generating or if generation never ran |
| status | text | NO | 'draft' | CHECK: `draft` / `published` / `in_progress` / `submitted` / `graded` |
| generation_error | text | YES | — |
| published_at | timestamptz | YES | — |
| cloned_from_paper_id | bigint → `generated_papers.id` | YES | — added 2026-07-09: set when this paper was created by reusing another paper's `questions_json` for a different student ("Reuse an existing paper" in the builder) instead of generating fresh content |
| created_at / updated_at | timestamptz | NO | now() | `updated_at` kept current by the existing shared `touch_updated_at()` trigger |

The core paper record. **`content_source_type = 'parsed'`** (added 2026-07-26) is a paper transcribed from an uploaded existing paper by `parse-homework-paper` rather than generated — it carries a `content_upload_id` (PDF or image), or `content_upload_ids` (ordered, added 2026-07-27) when the paper was photographed across several pages; `blocks` starts `[]` and is then filled with one display-only block per extracted section (`style` = the section title, not a `style_templates.code`), and `questions_json` is null while parsing / on failure exactly like a generated paper (so the same poll + `generation_error` UI covers both). **The `status` column is not driven by any trigger** — `draft`→`published`→`in_progress`→`submitted`→`graded` is advanced by application code (deliberately deferred triggers, see `HOMEWORK_GENERATOR_ARCHITECTURE.md`). As of Phase 5 the `draft`→`published` step exists (the review page's Confirm & Publish, a plain `UPDATE` stamping `published_at`); the remaining transitions arrive with Phases 6-7. RLS: `is_admin()` → `ALL`; `created_by_teacher_id = my_teacher_id()` → `ALL`; `is_performance_coach()` → `ALL`; `can_view_student(student_id) AND status <> 'draft'` → `SELECT` (a student never sees a draft).

### `question_bank` (200 rows, live-verified 2026-07-14)
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | bigint (identity) | NO | — |
| paper_id | bigint → `generated_papers.id` | YES | — |
| style_template_id | smallint → `style_templates.id` | YES | — |
| subject_id | smallint → `subjects.id` | YES | — |
| curriculum_id | smallint → `curricula.id` | YES | — |
| topic | text | YES | — |
| difficulty | text | YES | — |
| question_text | text | NO | — |
| question_hash | text | NO | — | indexed, for Phase 4's dedup check — not a unique constraint (a duplicate is a signal to skip/reroll, not a DB-level error) |
| answer_json | jsonb | YES | — |
| created_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |

RLS: `is_admin()` → `ALL`; any row in `teachers` → `SELECT`/`INSERT` (shared bank across all staff, not scoped per-teacher).

### `submissions` (6 rows, live-verified 2026-07-14)
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | bigint (identity) | NO | — |
| paper_id | bigint → `generated_papers.id`, unique | NO | — |
| student_id | text → `students.id` | NO | — |
| answers_json | jsonb | NO | '{}' |
| started_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |
| submitted_at | timestamptz | YES | — |
| whole_paper_answer | jsonb | YES | — added 2026-07-14 (manual photo/PDF grading, see below) |

One row per paper (`UNIQUE(paper_id)`). A `BEFORE UPDATE` trigger (`submissions_lock_after_submit` → `prevent_submission_edit_after_submit()`, `SECURITY DEFINER`) raises an exception on any update once `submitted_at` is set, unless the caller is `is_admin()` — so a student can't edit answers after submitting. RLS: `is_admin()` → `ALL`; `can_view_student(student_id)` → `ALL`; teacher `SELECT` where they created the paper (`generated_papers.created_by_teacher_id = my_teacher_id()`); `is_performance_coach()` → `SELECT`.

**`whole_paper_answer` (added 2026-07-14, migration `20260728010000_homework_manual_answer_grading.sql`)** — `{ file_url, file_name } | null`. Set when a student attaches ONE PDF covering their whole worked solution instead of answering every subjective question individually (a genuine alternative path through the student attempt UI, not a parsing/extraction step — the file is never split or read by AI). It covers every subjective (`short_answer`/`structured`/`extended_response`/`essay`/`criterion`) question on the paper at once; objective (`mcq`/`true_false`/`fill_blank`) questions are still answered normally in `answers_json` and auto-graded per-question regardless. Also as of the same migration, `answers_json` itself may hold a **photo answer** for any individual subjective question instead of typed text — `{ kind: 'photo', files: [{ file_url, file_name }] }` — no schema change needed for that (jsonb already flexible), see `src/types/database.ts`'s `HomeworkAnswer`/`PhotoAnswer`.

### `grades` (6 rows, live-verified 2026-07-14)
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | bigint (identity) | NO | — |
| submission_id | bigint → `submissions.id`, unique | NO | — |
| per_question_json | jsonb | NO | '{}' | per-question marks, max marks, AI feedback, `teacher_override` |
| total_marks | numeric | YES | — |
| max_marks | numeric | YES | — |
| graded_at | timestamptz | YES | — |
| teacher_reviewed_by | bigint → `teachers.id` | YES | — |
| teacher_reviewed_at | timestamptz | YES | — |
| created_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |
| published_at | timestamptz | YES | — added 2026-07-09: set when the paper-owning teacher explicitly publishes this grade to the student (`TeacherPaperResults`' "Publish grade" action). Null = the grade may already exist (AI-graded) but is not visible to the student. |
| published_by_teacher_id | bigint → `teachers.id` | YES | — added 2026-07-09 |
| whole_paper_grade | jsonb | YES | — added 2026-07-14 (manual photo/PDF grading, see below) |

One row per submission. RLS: `is_admin()` → `ALL`; teacher `ALL` where they created the underlying paper (joined via `submissions` → `generated_papers`); `is_performance_coach()` → `ALL`; student `SELECT` own grade via **`Students can read their own published grades`** (added 2026-07-09, replacing the old unconditional read policy) — requires `published_at is not null` **in addition to** `can_view_student()` (joined via `submissions`), so a student gets nothing back from this table until the teacher publishes, enforced at the RLS level, not just hidden in the UI.

**`whole_paper_grade` (added 2026-07-14, migration `20260728010000_homework_manual_answer_grading.sql`)** — `{ awarded, max, annotations: [{ id, page, x, y, text }] } | null`. Written by `grade-homework-submission` as a *pending placeholder* (`awarded: 0`, `max` = the combined marks of every subjective question, `annotations: []`) whenever `submissions.whole_paper_answer` is set — **never AI-graded**; the teacher enters `awarded` (and may edit `max`) by hand on the review page (`useSubmissionGrade.setWholePaperMark`), and drops comment pins on the rendered PDF (`saveWholePaperAnnotations`). A re-grade preserves an already-entered `whole_paper_grade` as-is rather than resetting it, since it was never AI output to begin with. Also as of the same migration, `per_question_json` entries for a photo-answered subjective question use **`graded_by: 'manual'`** (a third value alongside the existing `'auto'`/`'ai'`) and may carry their own `annotations` array — also never AI-graded, also preserved across a re-grade, also marked by hand via the same `teacher_override` mechanism the existing AI-grade override path already used (`OverrideControl` in `TeacherPaperResults.tsx`, relabelled for this case).

### `session_insights` (0 rows, re-verified 2026-07-14 — still genuinely unused)
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | bigint (identity) | NO | — |
| session_log_id | bigint → `session_logs.id`, unique | NO | — |
| topics_covered | jsonb | YES | — |
| misconceptions | jsonb | YES | — |
| mastery_scores | jsonb | YES | — |
| created_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |

Optional structured extraction from a `session_logs` row, meant to steer Phase 4's prompt builder — nothing populates this yet. RLS: `is_admin()` → `ALL`; teacher `SELECT` where `session_logs.teacher_id = my_teacher_id()`; `is_performance_coach()` → `SELECT`.

## Coordinator Logs tables (added 2026-07-29 — admin + PC only)

Periodic PC coaching check-ins per student. See `README.md` §2.11 for the feature summary and `db/README.md` for the RLS-scoping rationale.

### `coordinator_log_options` (39 rows — 6 lists, seeded from the client's spreadsheet)
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | bigint (identity) | NO | — |
| list_key | text | NO | — | CHECK: `primary_goal` / `progress_status` / `biggest_challenge` / `next_action` / `renewal_status` / `referral_status` |
| label | text | NO | — |
| sort_order | int | NO | 0 |
| is_active | boolean | NO | true |
| created_at / updated_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |
| updated_by_user_id | uuid → `users.id` | YES | — |

`UNIQUE(list_key, label)`. Backs all 6 admin-editable Coordinator Log dropdowns from one shared table instead of 6 near-identical ones — managed only from Admin → Reports → Settings (`CoordinatorLogOptionListSection` in `AdminReportsPage.tsx`: add/rename/reorder via `sort_order` swap/deactivate; plain text only, no per-option color). Seed counts: `primary_goal` 5, `progress_status` 6, `biggest_challenge` 10, `next_action` 8, `renewal_status` 5, `referral_status` 5. RLS: `is_admin()` → `ALL`; `is_performance_coach()` → `SELECT` where `is_active` (reads the live list to populate the log form's dropdowns; never writes). Grants: `authenticated`/`service_role` both `SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE` + sequence `USAGE`.

### `coordinator_logs` (12 rows, live-verified 2026-07-16)
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | bigint (identity) | NO | — |
| student_id | text → `students.id` | NO | — |
| teacher_id | bigint → `teachers.id` | NO | — | the logging PC; admin gets a free-choice "Coordinator" dropdown, auto-filled from the student's assigned PC same as session logs |
| log_date | date | NO | `current_date` |
| course_type_ids | smallint[] | NO | `'{}'` | "type(s) of program/package" — array since 2026-07-15 (was a single `course_type_id` FK); **fixed/read-only as of 2026-07-16** — `CoordinatorLogFormView.tsx` derives it from the student's own `student_packages` at save time instead of a coordinator-editable multi-select, so it always holds every program the student currently has a package in. Same Course Types list used as "packages" everywhere else. GIN-indexed (`coordinator_logs_course_type_ids_idx`) for the list filter's `contains` query. No FK on array elements — course types are soft-deleted (`is_active`), not removed. Drives which subject blocks the form shows |
| primary_goal_option_id | bigint → `coordinator_log_options.id` | YES | — |
| goal_timeline | date | YES | — | month+year only, day always `01` |
| progress_status_option_id | bigint → `coordinator_log_options.id` | YES | — |
| biggest_challenge_option_id | bigint → `coordinator_log_options.id` | YES | — |
| next_action_option_id | bigint → `coordinator_log_options.id` | YES | — |
| final_outcome_university_placement | text | YES | — |
| final_outcome_project_achievement | text | YES | — |
| evidence_link_profile_building | text | YES | — |
| student_engagement_rating | smallint | YES | — | CHECK 0-5 |
| parent_engagement_rating | smallint | YES | — | CHECK 0-5 |
| academic_progress_rating | smallint | YES | — | CHECK 0-5 |
| referral_potential_rating | smallint | YES | — | CHECK 0-5 |
| renewal_status_option_id | bigint → `coordinator_log_options.id` | YES | — | **deprecated 2026-07-15** — renewal is now per-package (see `coordinator_log_package_statuses`); always `NULL` on new logs |
| referral_status_option_id | bigint → `coordinator_log_options.id` | YES | — |
| ideal_outcome | text | YES | — | shown next to Primary Goal in the form as of 2026-07-15 |
| parent_involvement_rating | smallint | YES | — | CHECK 0-5 — "Engagement": how involved are the parents? |
| transformation_outcomes_rating | smallint | YES | — | CHECK 0-5 — did the student actually grow? |
| loyalty_retention_rating | smallint | YES | — | CHECK 0-5 — how committed are they? |
| referral_advocacy_rating | smallint | YES | — | CHECK 0-5 |
| parent_belief_rating | smallint | YES | — | CHECK 0-5 |
| primary_relationship_owner | text | YES | — | CHECK: `devi` / `pc_cc` / `ascend_now_system` (fixed enum, given directly — not admin-editable) |
| is_automated | boolean | NO | `false` | **added 2026-07-16** — `true` when the row was filed automatically by `sync_coordinator_log_for_student` (per-package renewal automation / initial backfill); `false` for a log a PC/admin filed via the form. Existing rows were backfilled the same day: any log with every hand-entered field null and no subject baseline/grade was set `true`, the rest (logs 32/33 for S1, 31 for S12) left `false`. Drives the **"First logged by Performance Coach"** date shown on the student's Performance Coach Log tab — the min `log_date` over that student's `is_automated = false` logs |
| created_at / updated_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |

Only `student_id`/`teacher_id`/`course_type_ids` (at least one program) are required — everything else is filled in as it becomes known. The form is **append-only** as of 2026-07-15: there is no edit/field-locking; selecting a student prefills every field from their latest log and any save writes a new row, so history is preserved for analysis. All 9 rating columns share one 0-5 scale (the CHECK constraints are combined into `coordinator_logs_ratings_check`, and the form now renders all 9 as star pickers); the month/year-only `goal_timeline` column is enforced by `coordinator_logs_month_dates_check` (`extract(day from ...) = 1`) — the same "one real DATE column, derive month/year at display" convention as the rest of this schema, applied as a CHECK safety net the same way session-duration rounding is (see `README.md` §4). RLS: `is_admin()` → `ALL`; a PC gets `ALL` where `exists(select 1 from pc_student_assignments a where a.student_id = coordinator_logs.student_id and a.pc_teacher_id = my_teacher_id() and a.unassigned_at is null)` — no blanket PC read like `session_logs` has, since field 1 of the spec explicitly requires a PC to only reach their own assigned students. No plain-teacher or student policy at all. Indexed on `student_id`, `teacher_id`, `log_date`. Grants: `authenticated`/`service_role` both `SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE` + sequence `USAGE`. **Verified live** via impersonation: PC (teacher 133, assigned to S1) could insert+read a row for S1 (rolled back, not committed); a plain non-PC teacher (id 66) attempting the identical insert for the same student was rejected with `42501: new row violates row-level security policy`.

**Automatic per-package renewal logging (added 2026-07-15; Upsell Opportunity dropped and Upsell Timing renamed to Renewal Timing 2026-07-16).** The renewal "section" of a coordinator log is **per package** — a set of `coordinator_log_package_statuses` child rows (one per package/pool), snapshotted onto every log at insert time. When any package's derived status changes, the system files a **brand-new coordinator log** (today's date, everything else carried forward from the latest log, subjects copied, filed on the assigned PC's behalf; `primary_relationship_owner` is carried forward too, defaulting to `pc_cc` when there's no prior log) — so the coordinator-log history is itself the dated record of "on this date this package's status changed." Nothing here is entered by a PC/admin (removed from the form); only `referral_status_option_id` stays manual. Per-package mapping (in `coordinator_log_package_status_rows()`): `not_due` (<75%) → `upcoming` (75%+, `renewal_timing` = month it crossed 75%) → `in_discussion` (an open pending/acknowledged renewal request matching this exact package: a request against a leaf/standalone course type only matches a standalone package of that type — `pkg.package_type_id is null` — a request against a bundle course type matches every pool with `pkg.package_type_id` = that bundle; fixed 2026-07-15 so a leaf-course-type request stops bleeding into a same-named pool of an unrelated bundle) → `renewed` (a request fulfilled into a pool that's since been reset <75%) → `not_renewing` (100% with no renewal in motion). Functions/triggers:

- **`coordinator_log_package_status_rows(text)`** (SECURITY DEFINER) — returns the current per-package status set for a student.
- **`trg_coordinator_logs_snapshot_statuses`** — `AFTER INSERT ON coordinator_logs` → `trg_snapshot_coordinator_log_package_statuses()`; snapshots the current per-package set onto every new log (manual PC logs included), so each log carries its section as of its own date.
- **`sync_coordinator_log_for_student(text)`** (SECURITY DEFINER) — appends a new log only when the per-package set (a sorted signature) differs from the latest log's; no-op otherwise; skipped when the student has no active PC or no unlocked packages. As of 2026-07-16 it stamps **`is_automated = true`** on the log it inserts (so only genuine PC form-inserts stay `false`) and carries the subject **`baseline_score_date`** forward alongside `baseline_score` when copying the previous log's subjects.
- **`trg_sync_coordinator_log_sp_ins` / `trg_sync_coordinator_log_sp_upd`** (`AFTER INSERT` / `AFTER UPDATE OF notified_75_pct_at, notified_100_pct_at ON student_packages`) and **`trg_sync_coordinator_log_req`** (`AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ON package_renewal_requests`) — call the sync at the same moments the coaches are emailed.

Verified live (rolled back): the backfill filed one system log per student with per-package child rows (S1: pool at College Counselling `in_discussion`, Academic `renewed`, rest `not_due`); a `NULL → stamped` 75% update on a pool appended a **new** coordinator log dated today with that pool flipped to `upcoming` (`renewal_timing` set to that month); an unrelated `student_packages` update appended nothing (dedup).

### `coordinator_log_subjects` (6 rows, live-verified 2026-07-15)
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | bigint (identity) | NO | — |
| coordinator_log_id | bigint → `coordinator_logs.id` (ON DELETE CASCADE) | NO | — |
| subject_id | smallint → `subjects.id` | NO | — |
| curriculum_id | smallint → `curricula.id` | YES | — | mirrors `session_logs`' subject/curriculum pairing |
| baseline_score | text | YES | — |
| baseline_score_date | date | YES | — | **added 2026-07-16** — the date the baseline score was recorded, **auto-stamped as today** the moment the PC adds an academic subject with a baseline (not asked for in the form; academic subjects only; Beyond Academic / College Counselling picks leave it null). Shown inline in brackets next to the baseline score (e.g. `5 (16 Jul 2026)`) in the form and every read view. Carried forward like `baseline_score` when a new log prefills from the latest, so it keeps the original record date |
| final_outcome_grade | text | YES | — | the subject's **current grade** at this check-in (UI label "Current Grade"; was "Grade Improvement"). Versioned across the student's logs — the form shows earlier logs' grades beside a blank new-grade input, and the read view shows the whole per-subject timeline |
| sort_order | int | NO | 0 |
| created_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |

One row per subject a PC adds to a log. A subject's bucket (Academic / Beyond Academic / College Counselling) is derived from the `subjects` catalogue at render time (`subjectCategoryOf`), so the same flat list backs all three form blocks: **only Academic subjects carry a `baseline_score`/`final_outcome_grade`** (the Profile Building and College Counselling pickers add subjects with both null). `UNIQUE(coordinator_log_id, subject_id, curriculum_id)`. Since the log form is append-only, each save just inserts the current set against the new log id (the older log keeps its own rows as history). RLS mirrors `coordinator_logs` via an `exists(select 1 from coordinator_logs cl join pc_student_assignments a on a.student_id = cl.student_id where cl.id = coordinator_log_subjects.coordinator_log_id and ...)` subquery, since this table has no `student_id` of its own. Indexed on `coordinator_log_id`, `subject_id`. Grants: `authenticated`/`service_role` both `SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE` + sequence `USAGE`.

### `coordinator_log_package_statuses` (added 2026-07-15; `upsell_opportunity` dropped and `upsell_timing` renamed to `renewal_timing` 2026-07-16)
| Column | Type | Nullable | Default |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | bigint (identity) | NO | — |
| coordinator_log_id | bigint → `coordinator_logs.id` (ON DELETE CASCADE) | NO | — |
| student_package_id | bigint → `student_packages.id` (ON DELETE SET NULL) | YES | — | the package/pool this row is the status of |
| course_type_id | smallint → `course_types.id` | YES | — |
| package_type_id | smallint → `course_types.id` | YES | — | the bundle (Foundation Program / All-In-One) this pool belongs to, if any |
| pool_label | text | YES | — |
| renewal_status | text | NO | — | CHECK: `not_due` / `upcoming` / `in_discussion` / `renewed` / `not_renewing` |
| renewal_timing | date | YES | — | month it crossed 75% (non-null ⇔ package is at 75%+ usage) — the timing for that package's renewal. Renamed from `upsell_timing` 2026-07-16 |
| sort_order | int | NO | 0 |
| created_at | timestamptz | NO | now() |

The renewal "section" of a coordinator log: one row per package/pool, snapshotted onto the log at insert time by `trg_coordinator_logs_snapshot_statuses` (see the automatic-logging note above) from the live package state — **never entered by a PC/admin**. A coordinator log's per-package table (and the whole append-a-new-log-on-change flow) is driven off these rows. RLS mirrors `coordinator_log_subjects` (admin `ALL`; PC `SELECT` via an `exists(... coordinator_logs cl join pc_student_assignments ...)` subquery, since it has no `student_id`). Indexed on `coordinator_log_id`. Grants: `authenticated` `SELECT`; `service_role` `ALL`. Reads on the frontend via `fetchCoordinatorLogById` (`package_statuses`), rendered by `RenewalSection`.

## Why this file exists (root cause of prior drift — now resolved)

This project used to have **two parallel, overlapping schema-change systems** that had gone out of sync with each other and with the live database:

1. **`db/schema/01_*.sql` through `28_*.sql`** — numbered files meant to be run in order on a fresh project.
2. **`supabase/migrations/*.sql`** — 35 timestamped files (`20260623000000` through `20260707000000`) in the standard Supabase CLI migration format.

Problems found during the 2026-07-01 audit that produced this document:

- **Supabase's own migration-history table was empty** (`list_migrations` returned zero rows) despite 35 files existing in `supabase/migrations/`. This meant none of those migrations were ever applied through `supabase db push`/tracked CLI flow — they (or equivalent SQL) were run directly against the database (SQL Editor, MCP `execute_sql`, or similar), so those files could not be trusted as an accurate history of what was live.
- **Duplicate timestamp**: two files were both named `20260627000001_*`.
- **The two systems overlapped in coverage** — e.g. `db/schema/25`–`28` covered subject/curriculum hierarchy changes that also appeared (differently) in `supabase/migrations/20260628000000_hierarchy_relocation.sql` and neighbors, with no way to tell which one actually produced the live state.
- **The prior docs were dated June 23, 2026** and described only 6 tables, while the live database had grown to 25 tables with 90+ RLS policies (60+ on user tables, plus storage policies).

**Resolution:** both `db/schema/` and the old contents of `supabase/migrations/` have been deleted and replaced with one file — `supabase/migrations/20260701000000_baseline_full_schema.sql` — generated directly from this document's introspection data, reproducing the entire live schema exactly (tables, constraints, indexes, functions, triggers, RLS policies, grants, storage buckets/policies). It is schema-only, not a data restore. **`supabase/migrations/` is now the single migration system for this project going forward** — new changes should be added there as new timestamped files and applied via tracked `supabase db push` (or Supabase MCP `apply_migration`) so the migration-history table stays accurate this time. Before writing a new migration, still verify current live state directly (`list_tables`, `execute_sql`) rather than assuming the migrations folder alone is current — it's now trustworthy going forward, but only if that discipline holds.
